# abstract_nodes.py

"""This module provides containers for python objects that are valid
printing targets but are not a subclass of SymPy's Printable.
"""


from sympy.core.containers import Tuple


class List(Tuple):
    """Represents a (frozen) (Python) list (for code printing purposes)."""
    def __eq__(self, other):
        if isinstance(other, list):
            return self == List(*other)
        else:
            return self.args == other

    def __hash__(self):
        return super().__hash__()

# adaptive-node.py

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
import networkx as nx

class AdaptiveNode:
    def __init__(self, node_id: str, memory_threshold: float, initial_data: Optional[Dict] = None):
        self.node_id = node_id
        self.memory_threshold = memory_threshold
        self.memory_usage = 0.0
        self.processed_data = []
        self.child_nodes = []
        self.insights = []
        
    def process_data(self, data_chunk: Dict) -> bool:
        """Process incoming data and potentially trigger node replication."""
        processed_size = self._calculate_data_size(data_chunk)
        
        if self.memory_usage + processed_size > self.memory_threshold:
            # Create child node with specialized characteristics
            child_node = self._replicate_node(processed_size)
            self.child_nodes.append(child_node)
            return child_node.process_data(data_chunk)
            
        self.memory_usage += processed_size
        insight = self._generate_insight(data_chunk)
        self.insights.append(insight)
        return True
        
    def _replicate_node(self, initial_load: float) -> 'AdaptiveNode':
        """Create a new specialized node based on current insights."""
        new_threshold = self.memory_threshold * 1.5  # Growth factor
        new_id = f"{self.node_id}_child_{len(self.child_nodes)}"
        
        # Transfer relevant insights to new node
        specialized_data = self._extract_specialization_data()
        
        return AdaptiveNode(new_id, new_threshold, specialized_data)
        
    def _generate_insight(self, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Generate insights from processed data using probabilistic analysis."""
        # Implementation of complex insight generation
        weights = np.random.dirichlet(np.ones(len(data)))
        insight = {
            'source_node': self.node_id,
            'weight_distribution': weights,
            'confidence_score': np.random.beta(2, 2),
            'derived_patterns': self._extract_patterns(data)
        }
        return insight
        
    def _extract_patterns(self, data: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
        """Extract underlying patterns from data using graph theory."""
        # Create a graph representation of data relationships
        G = nx.Graph()
        
        # Add nodes and edges based on data relationships
        for key, value in data.items():
            G.add_node(key)
            for other_key, other_value in data.items():
                if key != other_key:
                    correlation = self._calculate_correlation(value, other_value)
                    if correlation > 0.5:
                        G.add_edge(key, other_key, weight=correlation)
        
        # Find communities in the graph
        communities = nx.community.louvain_communities(G)
        
        patterns = []
        for community in communities:
            pattern = {
                'elements': list(community),
                'centrality': nx.degree_centrality(G.subgraph(community)),
                'density': nx.density(G.subgraph(community))
            }
            patterns.append(pattern)
            
        return patterns
        
    @staticmethod
    def _calculate_correlation(a: any, b: any) -> float:
        """Calculate correlation between two data elements."""
        if isinstance(a, (int, float)) and isinstance(b, (int, float)):
            return abs(np.corrcoef([a], [b])[0,1])
        return 0.5  # Default correlation for non-numeric data
        
    @staticmethod
    def _calculate_data_size(data: Dict) -> float:
        """Calculate the memory footprint of data."""
        return sum(sys.getsizeof(value) for value in data.values())

# addiction_module.py

# AddictionModule: Free Movement Through Any Data Format or Structure
import os
import json
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from typing import Union

class AddictionModule:
    def __init__(self):
        self.supported_formats = ["json", "xml", "csv", "text"]

    def parse_data(self, file_path: str) -> Union[dict, list, str]:
        ext = os.path.splitext(file_path)[-1].lower()
        if ext == ".json":
            return self._parse_json(file_path)
        elif ext == ".xml":
            return self._parse_xml(file_path)
        elif ext == ".csv":
            return self._parse_csv(file_path)
        else:
            return self._parse_text(file_path)

    def _parse_json(self, file_path: str) -> dict:
        with open(file_path, 'r') as f:
            return json.load(f)

    def _parse_xml(self, file_path: str) -> dict:
        tree = ET.parse(file_path)
        root = tree.getroot()
        return self._xml_to_dict(root)

    def _xml_to_dict(self, element):
        return {element.tag: {child.tag: child.text for child in element}}

    def _parse_csv(self, file_path: str) -> list:
        with open(file_path, 'r') as f:
            return [line.strip().split(',') for line in f.readlines()]

    def _parse_text(self, file_path: str) -> str:
        with open(file_path, 'r') as f:
            return f.read()

    def traverse_and_extract(self, data: Union[dict, list, str]):
        if isinstance(data, dict):
            return self._extract_from_dict(data)
        elif isinstance(data, list):
            return self._extract_from_list(data)
        elif isinstance(data, str):
            return data

    def _extract_from_dict(self, data: dict) -> list:
        extracted = []
        for key, value in data.items():
            if isinstance(value, (dict, list)):
                extracted.extend(self.traverse_and_extract(value))
            else:
                extracted.append(value)
        return extracted

    def _extract_from_list(self, data: list) -> list:
        extracted = []
        for item in data:
            if isinstance(item, (dict, list)):
                extracted.extend(self.traverse_and_extract(item))
            else:
                extracted.append(item)
        return extracted

# advanced-analysis.py

import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier, IsolationForest
from sklearn.decomposition import PCA
import networkx as nx
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional
import pandas as pd
from scipy.stats import pearsonr
from scipy.cluster.hierarchy import linkage, dendrogram

class AdvancedInsightEngine:
    """
    Enhanced analysis engine with sophisticated pattern recognition
    and insight generation capabilities.
    """
    def __init__(self):
        self.pattern_memory = {}
        self.graph_patterns = nx.Graph()
        self.anomaly_detector = IsolationForest(contamination=0.1)
        self.deep_learner = self._initialize_deep_learner()
        
    def analyze_complex_patterns(self, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """
        Perform sophisticated pattern analysis using multiple techniques.
        """
        results = {
            'temporal_patterns': self._analyze_temporal_patterns(data),
            'network_insights': self._analyze_network_patterns(data),
            'anomalies': self._detect_anomalies(data),
            'correlations': self._analyze_correlations(data),
            'emergent_properties': self._identify_emergent_properties(data)
        }
        
        # Combine insights using knowledge graph
        combined_insights = self._combine_multimodal_insights(results)
        return combined_insights
    
    def _analyze_temporal_patterns(self, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """
        Analyze complex temporal patterns and cycles.
        """
        if 'time_series' not in data:
            return {}
            
        ts_data = data['time_series']
        analysis = {}
        
        # Fourier analysis for cycle detection
        frequencies = np.fft.fft(ts_data)
        dominant_cycles = np.abs(frequencies[:len(frequencies)//2])
        
        # Wavelet analysis for multi-scale patterns
        try:
            import pywt
            wavelet = 'db1'
            coeffs = pywt.wavedec(ts_data, wavelet)
            wavelet_patterns = [np.mean(np.abs(c)) for c in coeffs]
        except ImportError:
            wavelet_patterns = []
        
        # Trend detection using polynomial fitting
        x = np.arange(len(ts_data))
        z = np.polyfit(x, ts_data, 3)
        trend = np.poly1d(z)
        
        analysis['cycles'] = {
            'dominant_frequencies': dominant_cycles.tolist(),
            'wavelet_patterns': wavelet_patterns,
            'trend_coefficients': z.tolist()
        }
        
        return analysis
    
    def _analyze_network_patterns(self, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """
        Analyze complex network patterns and relationships.
        """
        # Create interaction network
        G = nx.Graph()
        
        # Add nodes and edges based on correlations
        if 'variables' in data:
            variables = data['variables']
            for i, var1 in enumerate(variables):
                for j, var2 in enumerate(variables[i+1:], i+1):
                    corr = self._calculate_correlation(data[var1], data[var2])
                    if abs(corr) > 0.5:  # Significant correlation threshold
                        G.add_edge(var1, var2, weight=corr)
        
        # Analyze network properties
        analysis = {
            'centrality': nx.degree_centrality(G),
            'communities': list(nx.community.greedy_modularity_communities(G)),
            'key_nodes': self._identify_key_nodes(G),
            'structural_patterns': self._analyze_network_structure(G)
        }
        
        return analysis
    
    def _detect_anomalies(self, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """
        Detect anomalies and unusual patterns using multiple methods.
        """
        results = {}
        
        # Statistical anomaly detection
        if 'numerical_data' in data:
            numerical_data = np.array(data['numerical_data'])
            isolation_forest_scores = self.anomaly_detector.fit_predict(numerical_data.reshape(-1, 1))
            
            # Z-score based detection
            z_scores = np.abs((numerical_data - np.mean(numerical_data)) / np.std(numerical_data))
            statistical_anomalies = np.where(z_scores > 3)[0]
            
            results['statistical_anomalies'] = {
                'isolation_forest': isolation_forest_scores.tolist(),
                'z_score_anomalies': statistical_anomalies.tolist()
            }
        
        # Pattern-based anomaly detection
        if 'patterns' in data:
            pattern_anomalies = self._detect_pattern_anomalies(data['patterns'])
            results['pattern_anomalies'] = pattern_anomalies
            
        return results
    
    def _analyze_correlations(self, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """
        Analyze complex correlations and dependencies.
        """
        if not isinstance(data, dict):
            return {}
            
        correlations = {}
        
        # Pairwise correlations
        variables = list(data.keys())
        for i, var1 in enumerate(variables):
            if isinstance(data[var1], (list, np.ndarray)):
                correlations[var1] = {}
                for var2 in variables[i+1:]:
                    if isinstance(data[var2], (list, np.ndarray)):
                        corr, p_value = pearsonr(data[var1], data[var2])
                        correlations[var1][var2] = {
                            'correlation': corr,
                            'p_value': p_value
                        }
        
        # Hierarchical clustering of correlations
        correlation_matrix = self._create_correlation_matrix(correlations)
        linkage_matrix = linkage(correlation_matrix, method='ward')
        
        return {
            'pairwise_correlations': correlations,
            'hierarchical_clustering': {
                'linkage_matrix': linkage_matrix.tolist(),
                'variables': variables
            }
        }
    
    def _identify_emergent_properties(self, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """
        Identify emergent properties and complex patterns.
        """
        emergent_properties = {}
        
        # Dimensionality reduction to find hidden patterns
        if 'numerical_data' in data:
            pca = PCA(n_components=0.95)  # Keep 95% of variance
            transformed_data = pca.fit_transform(data['numerical_data'])
            
            emergent_properties['dimensional_reduction'] = {
                'explained_variance_ratio': pca.explained_variance_ratio_.tolist(),
                'n_components': pca.n_components_,
                'transformed_data': transformed_data.tolist()
            }
        
        # Identify clusters and their properties
        if 'cluster_data' in data:
            cluster_properties = self._analyze_cluster_properties(data['cluster_data'])
            emergent_properties['cluster_properties'] = cluster_properties
        
        return emergent_properties
    
    def _initialize_deep_learner(self) -> nn.Module:
        """
        Initialize deep learning model for pattern recognition.
        """
        model = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(100, 64),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Dropout(0.2),
            nn.Linear(64, 32),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Linear(32, 16)
        )
        return model
    
    def _combine_multimodal_insights(self, results: Dict) -> Dict:
        """
        Combine insights from different analysis methods.
        """
        combined = {}
        
        # Create knowledge graph
        G = nx.Graph()
        
        # Add nodes for different types of insights
        for analysis_type, findings in results.items():
            if isinstance(findings, dict):
                G.add_node(analysis_type, type='analysis')
                for key, value in findings.items():
                    G.add_node(f"{analysis_type}_{key}", type='finding')
                    G.add_edge(analysis_type, f"{analysis_type}_{key}")
        
        # Find relationships between findings
        for node1 in G.nodes():
            for node2 in G.nodes():
                if node1 != node2 and G.nodes[node1]['type'] == 'finding' and G.nodes[node2]['type'] == 'finding':
                    relationship = self._find_relationship(node1, node2, results)
                    if relationship:
                        G.add_edge(node1, node2, relationship=relationship)
        
        # Extract main insights
        combined['main_findings'] = self._extract_main_findings(G)
        combined['relationships'] = self._extract_relationships(G)
        
        return combined
    
    @staticmethod
    def _find_relationship(node1: str, node2: str, results: Dict) -> Optional[str]:
        """Find relationships between different findings."""
        # Implementation depends on specific domain knowledge
        pass
    
    @staticmethod
    def _extract_main_findings(G: nx.Graph) -> List[str]:
        """Extract main findings from the knowledge graph."""
        # Use centrality measures to identify key findings
        centrality = nx.degree_centrality(G)
        main_findings = sorted(centrality.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:5]
        return [finding[0] for finding in main_findings]
    
    @staticmethod
    def _extract_relationships(G: nx.Graph) -> List[Dict]:
        """Extract relationships between findings."""
        relationships = []
        for edge in G.edges(data=True):
            if 'relationship' in edge[2]:
                relationships.append({
                    'source': edge[0],
                    'target': edge[1],
                    'type': edge[2]['relationship']
                })
        return relationships

```python
class ConceptFormationSystem:
    """Advanced system for autonomous concept development and evolution"""
    def __init__(self):
        self.concept_network = nx.DiGraph()
        self.concept_prototypes = {}
        self.abstraction_layers = defaultdict(set)
        self.concept_strengths = defaultdict(float)
        self.relationship_matrix = defaultdict(dict)
        
        # Dynamic thresholds
        self.thresholds = {
            'similarity': 0.7,
            'abstraction': 0.6,
            'integration': 0.5,
            'evolution': 0.4
        }
        
        # Concept evolution tracking
        self.evolution_history = defaultdict(list)
        self.merge_candidates = defaultdict(set)
        
        # Initialize base layer
        self.current_layer = 0
        self.max_layers = 10

class Concept:
    """Represents a single concept in the network"""
    def __init__(self, core_features: Dict):
        self.id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.core_features = core_features
        self.associated_features = defaultdict(float)
        self.instances = []
        self.abstraction_level = 0
        self.creation_time = time.time()
        self.last_update = time.time()
        self.strength = 0.1
        self.connections = defaultdict(float)
        self.evolution_path = []
        
        # Concept properties
        self.flexibility = 0.5  # Ability to adapt
        self.stability = 0.5   # Resistance to change
        self.generality = 0.0  # Level of abstractness
        
        # Learning parameters
        self.learning_rate = 0.1
        self.adaptation_rate = 0.1

    def update(self, new_instance: Dict) -> float:
        """Update concept with new instance"""
        similarity = self.calculate_similarity(new_instance)
        
        if similarity > 0.3:  # Minimum similarity threshold
            # Update feature weights
            self._update_features(new_instance, similarity)
            
            # Adjust concept properties
            self._adjust_properties(similarity)
            
            # Track evolution
            self._track_evolution(new_instance)
            
            # Update metadata
            self.instances.append(new_instance)
            self.last_update = time.time()
            self.strength = min(1.0, self.strength + 0.1 * similarity)
            
        return similarity

    def _update_features(self, new_instance: Dict, similarity: float):
        """Update feature weights based on new instance"""
        # Update core features
        for feature, value in new_instance.items():
            if feature in self.core_features:
                # Weighted average based on concept stability
                current_value = self.core_features[feature]
                new_value = (
                    current_value * self.stability +
                    value * (1 - self.stability)
                )
                self.core_features[feature] = new_value
            else:
                # Add to associated features
                self.associated_features[feature] += similarity * self.learning_rate

    def _adjust_properties(self, similarity: float):
        """Adjust concept properties based on new information"""
        # Update flexibility
        self.flexibility *= 0.95  # Natural decay
        self.flexibility += 0.05 * (1 - similarity)  # Increase with novelty
        
        # Update stability
        instance_count = len(self.instances)
        self.stability = min(0.9, 0.1 + 0.8 * (instance_count / (instance_count + 10)))
        
        # Update generality
        feature_variance = np.mean([
            np.var([instance.get(f, 0) for instance in self.instances])
            for f in self.core_features
        ])
        self.generality = 1 / (1 + np.exp(-feature_variance))

class ConceptLayer:
    """Represents a layer of abstraction in concept hierarchy"""
    def __init__(self, level: int):
        self.level = level
        self.concepts = {}
        self.relationships = defaultdict(dict)
        self.emergence_patterns = defaultdict(list)
        self.layer_statistics = {
            'concept_count': 0,
            'average_strength': 0.0,
            'abstraction_quality': 0.0
        }

    def add_concept(self, concept: Concept):
        """Add concept to layer"""
        self.concepts[concept.id] = concept
        self._update_statistics()
        self._check_emergence_patterns()

    def _update_statistics(self):
        """Update layer statistics"""
        self.layer_statistics['concept_count'] = len(self.concepts)
        self.layer_statistics['average_strength'] = np.mean([
            c.strength for c in self.concepts.values()
        ])
        self.layer_statistics['abstraction_quality'] = self._calculate_abstraction_quality()

    def _calculate_abstraction_quality(self) -> float:
        """Calculate quality of abstractions in layer"""
        if not self.concepts:
            return 0.0
            
        # Consider multiple factors
        coherence = self._calculate_coherence()
        distinctiveness = self._calculate_distinctiveness()
        stability = np.mean([c.stability for c in self.concepts.values()])
        
        return (coherence + distinctiveness + stability) / 3

class ConceptHierarchy:
    """Manages hierarchy of concept layers"""
    def __init__(self):
        self.layers = []
        self.current_layer = 0
        self.abstraction_thresholds = [0.5, 0.6, 0.7, 0.8, 0.9]
        
        # Initialize base layer
        self.add_layer()

    def add_layer(self):
        """Add new abstraction layer"""
        new_layer = ConceptLayer(len(self.layers))
        self.layers.append(new_layer)

    def process_concept(self, concept_data: Dict):
        """Process new concept data through hierarchy"""
        # Start at bottom layer
        current_concepts = self._process_base_layer(concept_data)
        
        # Propagate up through layers
        for layer_idx in range(1, len(self.layers)):
            current_concepts = self._process_layer(
                current_concepts,
                layer_idx
            )
            
        # Check if new layer needed
        if self._should_add_layer():
            self.add_layer()

    def _process_base_layer(self, concept_data: Dict) -> List[Concept]:
        """Process concept at base layer"""
        base_layer = self.layers[0]
        matching_concepts = []
        
        # Find matching concepts
        for concept in base_layer.concepts.values():
            similarity = concept.calculate_similarity(concept_data)
            if similarity > self.abstraction_thresholds[0]:
                matching_concepts.append(concept)
                concept.update(concept_data)
                
        # Create new concept if no matches
        if not matching_concepts:
            new_concept = Concept(concept_data)
            base_layer.add_concept(new_concept)
            matching_concepts.append(new_concept)
            
        return matching_concepts

    def _process_layer(self, lower_concepts: List[Concept], layer_idx: int) -> List[Concept]:
        """Process concepts at higher layers"""
        current_layer = self.layers[layer_idx]
        abstract_concepts = []
        
        # Find or create abstract concepts
        concept_groups = self._group_related_concepts(lower_concepts)
        
        for group in concept_groups:
            # Try to find matching abstract concept
            abstract_concept = self._find_matching_abstract_concept(
                group,
                current_layer
            )
            
            if abstract_concept:
                self._update_abstract_concept(abstract_concept, group)
            else:
                abstract_concept = self._create_abstract_concept(group, layer_idx)
                current_layer.add_concept(abstract_concept)
                
            abstract_concepts.append(abstract_concept)
            
        return abstract_concepts

    def _group_related_concepts(self, concepts: List[Concept]) -> List[List[Concept]]:
        """Group related concepts for abstraction"""
        groups = []
        remaining = concepts.copy()
        
        while remaining:
            current = remaining.pop(0)
            current_group = [current]
            
            # Find related concepts
            for concept in remaining[:]:
                if self._are_concepts_related(current, concept):
                    current_group.append(concept)
                    remaining.remove(concept)
                    
            groups.append(current_group)
            
        return groups

    def _create_abstract_concept(self, concept_group: List[Concept], layer_idx: int) -> Concept:
        """Create new abstract concept from group of concepts"""
        # Extract common features
        common_features = self._extract_common_features(concept_group)
        
        # Create abstracted features
        abstract_features = {
            feature: np.mean([c.core_features.get(feature, 0) for c in concept_group])
            for feature in common_features
        }
        
        # Create new concept
        abstract_concept = Concept(abstract_features)
        abstract_concept.abstraction_level = layer_idx
        
        # Set properties based on group
        abstract_concept.generality = np.mean([c.generality for c in concept_group]) + 0.1
        abstract_concept.flexibility = np.mean([c.flexibility for c in concept_group])
        abstract_concept.stability = np.mean([c.stability for c in concept_group])
        
        return abstract_concept

    def _extract_common_features(self, concepts: List[Concept]) -> Set[str]:
        """Extract features common to all concepts in group"""
        feature_sets = [set(c.core_features.keys()) for c in concepts]
        return set.intersection(*feature_sets)

    def visualize_hierarchy(self):
        """Generate visualization of concept hierarchy"""
        G = nx.DiGraph()
        
        # Add nodes for each concept
        for layer_idx, layer in enumerate(self.layers):
            for concept in layer.concepts.values():
                G.add_node(
                    concept.id,
                    layer=layer_idx,
                    strength=concept.strength,
                    generality=concept.generality
                )
                
        # Add edges between related concepts
        for layer_idx in range(len(self.layers) - 1):
            current_layer = self.layers[layer_idx]
            next_layer = self.layers[layer_idx + 1]
            
            for concept in current_layer.concepts.values():
                for abstract_concept in next_layer.concepts.values():
                    if concept.id in abstract_concept.connections:
                        G.add_edge(
                            concept.id,
                            abstract_concept.id,
                            weight=abstract_concept.connections[concept.id]
                        )
        
        return G
```

# File: advanced_knowledge_management.py
# Description: Categorizes and prioritizes knowledge for better learning.

class KnowledgeManager:
    """Manages categorized and prioritized knowledge for nodes."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.knowledge_categories = {"scientific": [], "heuristic": [], "environmental": []}

    def categorize_knowledge(self, node):
        """Categorizes node knowledge into predefined categories."""
        for data in node.dna["knowledge"]:
            if "science" in data:
                self.knowledge_categories["scientific"].append(data)
            elif "heuristic" in data:
                self.knowledge_categories["heuristic"].append(data)
            else:
                self.knowledge_categories["environmental"].append(data)
        node.logs.append(f"Knowledge categorized: {self.knowledge_categories}")

    def prioritize_knowledge(self):
        """Prioritizes knowledge for processing."""
        prioritized = []
        for category, items in self.knowledge_categories.items():
            if category == "scientific":
                prioritized.extend(items)
            elif category == "heuristic":
                prioritized.extend(items[:2])  # Limit heuristic processing
            else:
                prioritized.extend(items[:1])  # Limit environmental processing
        return prioritized


# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Create a node and knowledge manager
    node = EnhancedNode()
    manager = KnowledgeManager()

    # Simulate learning
    node.perceive("science_1")
    node.perceive("heuristic_1")
    node.perceive("environmental_1")
    node.perceive("science_2")
    node.perceive("heuristic_2")

    # Categorize and prioritize knowledge
    manager.categorize_knowledge(node)
    prioritized_knowledge = manager.prioritize_knowledge()

    # Display results
    print("\n--- Knowledge Categorization ---")
    print(manager.knowledge_categories)
    print("\n--- Prioritized Knowledge ---")
    print(prioritized_knowledge)
    print("\n--- Node Status ---")
    print(node.status())


# advanced-learning (1).py

from typing import Dict, List, Any, Optional, Tuple
import numpy as np
from dataclasses import dataclass
import json
import time
import logging
from collections import defaultdict

@dataclass
class LearningPattern:
    """Represents a learned pattern with confidence and timestamps."""
    pattern: Any
    confidence: float
    first_seen: float
    last_seen: float
    occurrences: int
    related_patterns: List[str] = None

    def update(self, confidence_delta: float = 0.1) -> None:
        """Update pattern with new occurrence."""
        self.occurrences += 1
        self.last_seen = time.time()
        self.confidence = min(1.0, self.confidence + confidence_delta)

    def decay(self, decay_rate: float = 0.01) -> None:
        """Apply time-based decay to confidence."""
        time_diff = time.time() - self.last_seen
        decay = decay_rate * time_diff
        self.confidence = max(0.0, self.confidence - decay)

class KnowledgeGraph:
    """Represents relationships between learned patterns."""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.connections: Dict[str, Dict[str, float]] = defaultdict(dict)
    
    def add_connection(self, pattern_a: str, pattern_b: str, strength: float) -> None:
        """Add or update connection between patterns."""
        self.connections[pattern_a][pattern_b] = strength
        self.connections[pattern_b][pattern_a] = strength
    
    def get_related_patterns(self, pattern: str, threshold: float = 0.5) -> List[str]:
        """Get patterns related to the given pattern above threshold strength."""
        if pattern not in self.connections:
            return []
        return [p for p, s in self.connections[pattern].items() if s >= threshold]

class AdvancedLearningSystem:
    """Implements advanced learning mechanisms for nodes."""
    
    def __init__(self, node_id: str):
        self.node_id = node_id
        self.patterns: Dict[str, LearningPattern] = {}
        self.knowledge_graph = KnowledgeGraph()
        self.learning_threshold = 0.3
        self.decay_interval = 3600  # 1 hour
        self.last_decay_time = time.time()
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(f"Learning-{node_id}")

    def learn(self, input_data: Any) -> None:
        """Learn from input data, identifying and storing patterns."""
        try:
            patterns = self._extract_patterns(input_data)
            
            for pattern_id, pattern_data in patterns.items():
                if pattern_id in self.patterns:
                    # Update existing pattern
                    self.patterns[pattern_id].update()
                    self._update_relationships(pattern_id, patterns)
                else:
                    # Create new pattern
                    self.patterns[pattern_id] = LearningPattern(
                        pattern=pattern_data,
                        confidence=0.5,
                        first_seen=time.time(),
                        last_seen=time.time(),
                        occurrences=1
                    )
                    self._update_relationships(pattern_id, patterns)
            
            self._apply_decay()
            self.logger.info(f"Learned {len(patterns)} patterns from input data")
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Error during learning: {str(e)}")

    def _extract_patterns(self, input_data: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Extract patterns from input data."""
        patterns = {}
        
        if isinstance(input_data, dict):
            # Extract patterns from dictionary
            for key, value in input_data.items():
                pattern_id = f"dict_pattern_{key}"
                patterns[pattern_id] = value
                
        elif isinstance(input_data, (list, tuple)):
            # Extract patterns from sequences
            for i, item in enumerate(input_data):
                pattern_id = f"seq_pattern_{i}"
                patterns[pattern_id] = item
                
        elif isinstance(input_data, str):
            # Extract patterns from text
            words = input_data.split()
            for i, word in enumerate(words):
                pattern_id = f"text_pattern_{word}"
                patterns[pattern_id] = word
        
        return patterns

    def _update_relationships(self, pattern_id: str, current_patterns: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Update relationships between patterns in the knowledge graph."""
        for other_id in current_patterns:
            if other_id != pattern_id:
                # Calculate relationship strength based on co-occurrence
                strength = self._calculate_relationship_strength(
                    self.patterns[pattern_id],
                    self.patterns.get(other_id)
                )
                self.knowledge_graph.add_connection(pattern_id, other_id, strength)

    def _calculate_relationship_strength(self, pattern_a: LearningPattern, 
                                      pattern_b: Optional[LearningPattern]) -> float:
        """Calculate the strength of relationship between two patterns."""
        if not pattern_b:
            return 0.0
            
        # Consider multiple factors for relationship strength
        time_proximity = 1.0 / (abs(pattern_a.last_seen - pattern_b.last_seen) + 1)
        confidence_product = pattern_a.confidence * pattern_b.confidence
        occurrence_ratio = min(pattern_a.occurrences, pattern_b.occurrences) / \
                         max(pattern_a.occurrences, pattern_b.occurrences)
        
        return (time_proximity + confidence_product + occurrence_ratio) / 3

    def _apply_decay(self) -> None:
        """Apply time-based decay to pattern confidences."""
        current_time = time.time()
        if current_time - self.last_decay_time >= self.decay_interval:
            for pattern in self.patterns.values():
                pattern.decay()
            self.last_decay_time = current_time
            
            # Remove patterns with very low confidence
            self.patterns = {
                k: v for k, v in self.patterns.items() 
                if v.confidence >= self.learning_threshold
            }

    def query(self, pattern_prefix: str, min_confidence: float = 0.5) -> List[Tuple[str, LearningPattern]]:
        """Query learned patterns matching a prefix with minimum confidence."""
        return [
            (pid, pattern) for pid, pattern in self.patterns.items()
            if pid.startswith(pattern_prefix) and pattern.confidence >= min_confidence
        ]

    def get_insights(self) -> Dict:
        """Generate insights about learned patterns."""
        return {
            'total_patterns': len(self.patterns),
            'average_confidence': np.mean([p.confidence for p in self.patterns.values()]),
            'highly_connected_patterns': [
                pattern_id for pattern_id in self.patterns
                if len(self.knowledge_graph.get_related_patterns(pattern_id)) > 5
            ],
            'recent_patterns': [
                pattern_id for pattern_id, pattern in self.patterns.items()
                if time.time() - pattern.last_seen < 3600  # Patterns seen in last hour
            ]
        }

    def save_state(self, filepath: str) -> None:
        """Save learning system state to file."""
        state = {
            'patterns': {
                pid: {
                    'pattern': str(p.pattern),  # Convert pattern to string for JSON
                    'confidence': p.confidence,
                    'first_seen': p.first_seen,
                    'last_seen': p.last_seen,
                    'occurrences': p.occurrences
                }
                for pid, p in self.patterns.items()
            },
            'knowledge_graph': dict(self.connections)
        }
        
        with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(state, f, indent=2)

    def load_state(self, filepath: str) -> None:
        """Load learning system state from file."""
        with open(filepath, 'r') as f:
            state = json.load(f)
            
        self.patterns = {
            pid: LearningPattern(
                pattern=p['pattern'],
                confidence=p['confidence'],
                first_seen=p['first_seen'],
                last_seen=p['last_seen'],
                occurrences=p['occurrences']
            )
            for pid, p in state['patterns'].items()
        }
        
        self.knowledge_graph.connections = defaultdict(dict, state['knowledge_graph'])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Configure logging
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
    
    # Create learning system
    learner = AdvancedLearningSystem("test_node")
    
    # Learn from different types of data
    learner.learn({"key1": "value1", "key2": "value2"})
    learner.learn(["item1", "item2", "item3"])
    learner.learn("This is a test sentence with patterns")
    
    # Query patterns
    text_patterns = learner.query("text_pattern_")
    print("Text Patterns:", text_patterns)
    
    # Get insights
    insights = learner.get_insights()
    print("Learning Insights:", insights)
    
    # Save state
    learner.save_state("learning_state.json")

```python
class AdaptiveLearningSystem:
    """Advanced system for developing and evolving learning strategies"""
    def __init__(self):
        self.learning_patterns = {}
        self.success_metrics = defaultdict(list)
        self.learning_strategies = {}
        self.adaptation_rate = 0.1
        self.mutation_chance = 0.05
        self.strategy_effectiveness = defaultdict(float)
        
        # Initialize base learning strategies
        self._initialize_strategies()

    def _initialize_strategies(self):
        """Initialize base learning strategies that can evolve"""
        self.learning_strategies = {
            'pattern_recognition': {
                'weights': np.random.random(5),  # Different aspects of pattern recognition
                'activation_threshold': 0.5,
                'learning_rate': 0.1,
                'pattern_memory': defaultdict(float),
                'evolution_history': []
            },
            'relationship_mapping': {
                'weights': np.random.random(4),  # Different types of relationships
                'connection_threshold': 0.6,
                'mapping_depth': 3,
                'relationship_memory': defaultdict(dict),
                'evolution_history': []
            },
            'concept_formation': {
                'weights': np.random.random(6),  # Different concept aspects
                'abstraction_level': 0.4,
                'generalization_rate': 0.2,
                'concept_hierarchy': defaultdict(set),
                'evolution_history': []
            }
        }

class DynamicLearningNode:
    """Node capable of evolving its own learning methods"""
    def __init__(self, node_type: str):
        self.node_type = node_type
        self.learning_systems = {}
        self.meta_learning = MetaLearningSystem()
        self.learning_history = []
        self.adaptation_models = {}
        
        # Initialize learning systems
        self._initialize_learning_systems()

    def _initialize_learning_systems(self):
        """Initialize different types of learning systems"""
        self.learning_systems = {
            'associative': AssociativeLearning(),
            'reinforcement': ReinforcementLearning(),
            'pattern': PatternLearning(),
            'abstract': AbstractLearning()
        }

class AssociativeLearning:
    """Learns by forming associations between different pieces of information"""
    def __init__(self):
        self.associations = defaultdict(dict)
        self.strength_matrix = {}
        self.recent_associations = deque(maxlen=100)
        self.association_patterns = {}

    def learn(self, input_data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Learn associations from input data"""
        new_associations = self._extract_associations(input_data)
        self._strengthen_associations(new_associations)
        self._discover_patterns()
        
        return {
            'new_associations': len(new_associations),
            'total_patterns': len(self.association_patterns)
        }

    def _extract_associations(self, data: Dict) -> List[Tuple]:
        """Extract potential associations from data"""
        associations = []
        items = list(data.items())
        
        for i, (key1, val1) in enumerate(items):
            for key2, val2 in items[i+1:]:
                similarity = self._calculate_similarity(val1, val2)
                if similarity > 0.5:
                    associations.append((key1, key2, similarity))
                    
        return associations

class ReinforcementLearning:
    """Learns through trial and error with feedback"""
    def __init__(self):
        self.action_values = defaultdict(float)
        self.experience_replay = deque(maxlen=1000)
        self.learning_rate = 0.1
        self.discount_factor = 0.9
        
    def learn(self, state: str, action: str, reward: float, next_state: str):
        """Update knowledge based on action results"""
        # Store experience
        self.experience_replay.append((state, action, reward, next_state))
        
        # Update action values
        current_value = self.action_values[(state, action)]
        next_value = max(self.action_values[(next_state, a)] 
                        for a in self._get_possible_actions(next_state))
        
        # Q-learning update
        new_value = current_value + self.learning_rate * (
            reward + self.discount_factor * next_value - current_value
        )
        
        self.action_values[(state, action)] = new_value
        
        # Periodically replay experiences
        if len(self.experience_replay) >= 100:
            self._replay_experiences()

class PatternLearning:
    """Learns by recognizing and generalizing patterns"""
    def __init__(self):
        self.patterns = defaultdict(list)
        self.pattern_strengths = defaultdict(float)
        self.pattern_hierarchy = defaultdict(set)
        self.abstraction_levels = defaultdict(int)

    def learn(self, data: any) -> Dict:
        """Learn patterns from input data"""
        # Extract patterns at different abstraction levels
        concrete_patterns = self._extract_concrete_patterns(data)
        abstract_patterns = self._extract_abstract_patterns(concrete_patterns)
        
        # Update pattern hierarchy
        self._update_hierarchy(concrete_patterns, abstract_patterns)
        
        # Strengthen successful patterns
        self._strengthen_patterns(concrete_patterns + abstract_patterns)
        
        return {
            'concrete_patterns': len(concrete_patterns),
            'abstract_patterns': len(abstract_patterns)
        }

    def _extract_concrete_patterns(self, data: any) -> List[Dict]:
        """Extract specific patterns from data"""
        patterns = []
        
        if isinstance(data, str):
            # Text patterns
            patterns.extend(self._extract_text_patterns(data))
        elif isinstance(data, np.ndarray):
            # Visual patterns
            patterns.extend(self._extract_visual_patterns(data))
        elif isinstance(data, dict):
            # Structural patterns
            patterns.extend(self._extract_structural_patterns(data))
            
        return patterns

class AbstractLearning:
    """Learns by forming abstract concepts and generalizations"""
    def __init__(self):
        self.concepts = defaultdict(dict)
        self.abstractions = defaultdict(set)
        self.generalization_rules = []
        self.concept_hierarchy = nx.DiGraph()

    def learn(self, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Learn abstractions from concrete data"""
        # Extract features for abstraction
        features = self._extract_features(data)
        
        # Form new concepts
        new_concepts = self._form_concepts(features)
        
        # Update concept hierarchy
        self._update_concept_hierarchy(new_concepts)
        
        # Generate new abstraction rules
        self._generate_abstraction_rules(new_concepts)
        
        return {
            'new_concepts': len(new_concepts),
            'total_concepts': len(self.concepts)
        }

class MetaLearningSystem:
    """System that learns how to learn better"""
    def __init__(self):
        self.learning_strategies = {}
        self.strategy_performance = defaultdict(list)
        self.meta_patterns = defaultdict(list)
        self.learning_history = []

    def evolve_strategies(self) -> Dict:
        """Evolve learning strategies based on performance"""
        # Analyze strategy performance
        performance_metrics = self._analyze_performance()
        
        # Generate new strategies
        new_strategies = self._generate_strategies(performance_metrics)
        
        # Test and integrate successful strategies
        successful_strategies = self._test_strategies(new_strategies)
        
        # Update strategy pool
        self._update_strategy_pool(successful_strategies)
        
        return {
            'new_strategies': len(successful_strategies),
            'performance_improvement': self._calculate_improvement()
        }

    def _analyze_performance(self) -> Dict:
        """Analyze performance of current learning strategies"""
        metrics = {}
        
        for strategy, history in self.strategy_performance.items():
            if history:
                # Calculate various performance metrics
                success_rate = sum(1 for h in history if h['success']) / len(history)
                learning_speed = np.mean([h['learning_time'] for h in history])
                efficiency = np.mean([h['resource_usage'] for h in history])
                
                metrics[strategy] = {
                    'success_rate': success_rate,
                    'learning_speed': learning_speed,
                    'efficiency': efficiency,
                    'overall_score': success_rate * (1 / learning_speed) * efficiency
                }
                
        return metrics

    def _generate_strategies(self, performance_metrics: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
        """Generate new learning strategies based on performance analysis"""
        new_strategies = []
        
        # Find best performing strategies
        sorted_strategies = sorted(
            performance_metrics.items(),
            key=lambda x: x[1]['overall_score'],
            reverse=True
        )
        
        # Generate variations of successful strategies
        for strategy, metrics in sorted_strategies[:3]:
            variations = self._create_strategy_variations(strategy)
            new_strategies.extend(variations)
            
        return new_strategies

    def _test_strategies(self, strategies: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
        """Test new strategies and return successful ones"""
        successful = []
        
        for strategy in strategies:
            # Test strategy on sample data
            performance = self._evaluate_strategy(strategy)
            
            if performance['success_rate'] > 0.7:  # Success threshold
                successful.append({
                    'strategy': strategy,
                    'performance': performance
                })
                
        return successful

    def _evaluate_strategy(self, strategy: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Evaluate a learning strategy's performance"""
        results = {
            'success_rate': 0.0,
            'learning_time': 0.0,
            'resource_usage': 0.0,
            'adaptability': 0.0
        }
        
        try:
            # Run strategy through test scenarios
            test_scenarios = self._generate_test_scenarios()
            
            for scenario in test_scenarios:
                start_time = time.time()
                outcome = self._run_strategy_scenario(strategy, scenario)
                end_time = time.time()
                
                # Update results
                results['success_rate'] += outcome['success']
                results['learning_time'] += end_time - start_time
                results['resource_usage'] += outcome['resource_usage']
                results['adaptability'] += outcome['adaptation_score']
                
            # Average results
            num_scenarios = len(test_scenarios)
            for key in results:
                results[key] /= num_scenarios
                
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Strategy evaluation error: {e}")
            
        return results
```
