# File: advanced_logical_reasoning.py
# Description: Expands logical reasoning with multi-step inference and probabilities.

class AdvancedLogicalNode(LogicalReasoningNode):
    """Extends LogicalReasoningNode with multi-step inference and probabilistic reasoning."""
    
    def infer(self):
        """Performs multi-step logical inference."""
        patterns = self.analyze_patterns()
        if patterns == "No patterns identified.":
            self.logs.append("Inference failed: Insufficient patterns.")
            return "No inference possible."

        steps = [f"Step_{i}: Inferring from {pattern}" for i, pattern in enumerate(patterns)]
        conclusion = f"Conclusion: {patterns[-1]} confirms the initial hypothesis."
        self.logs.append(f"Multi-step inference completed: {steps} -> {conclusion}")
        return conclusion

    def probabilistic_reasoning(self):
        """Performs probabilistic reasoning based on patterns."""
        patterns = self.analyze_patterns()
        if patterns == "No patterns identified.":
            self.logs.append("Probabilistic reasoning failed: Insufficient patterns.")
            return "No reasoning possible."

        probabilities = {pattern: random.uniform(0.5, 1.0) for pattern in patterns}
        most_likely = max(probabilities, key=probabilities.get)
        self.logs.append(f"Probabilistic reasoning: Most likely outcome is {most_likely}.")
        return most_likely


# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Create an advanced logical node
    advanced_node = AdvancedLogicalNode()

    # Simulate learning
    inputs = ["Observation_1", "Observation_2", "Observation_3"]
    for input_data in inputs:
        advanced_node.perceive(input_data)

    # Perform advanced reasoning
    inference = advanced_node.infer()
    most_likely = advanced_node.probabilistic_reasoning()

    # Display results
    print("\n--- Advanced Logical Node Status ---")
    print(advanced_node.status())
    print("\n--- Inference ---")
    print(inference)
    print("\n--- Probabilistic Reasoning ---")
    print(most_likely)


# annotate_getitem_nodes.py

import operator

import torch


def annotate_getitem_nodes(graph: torch.fx.Graph) -> None:
    """
    Annotate the type of getitem nodes, inferred from the type of sequence node.
    If sequence node is not annotated with a type, do nothing.
    Currently support getitem nodes from Tuple, List, and NamedTuple sequence node.

    This is helpful since annotations on local names within function are lost during FX transforms.
    Adding back known type annotation for getitem nodes to improve jit scriptability.

    Args:
        graph (Graph): The graph to be annotated
    """
    for node in graph.nodes:
        if node.target == operator.getitem:
            sequence_node, index_node = node.args
            if not sequence_node.type:
                continue
            # container types
            if hasattr(sequence_node.type, "_name"):
                parameterized_types = sequence_node.type.__args__
                if sequence_node.type._name == "Tuple":
                    if len(parameterized_types) == 2 and isinstance(
                        parameterized_types[1], type(...)
                    ):
                        node.type = parameterized_types[0]
                    else:
                        assert len(parameterized_types) > index_node
                        node_type = parameterized_types[index_node]
                        node.type = node_type
                elif sequence_node.type._name == "List":
                    assert len(parameterized_types) == 1
                    node.type = parameterized_types[0]
            # NamedTuple type
            elif hasattr(sequence_node.type, "__annotations__"):
                if sequence_node.type == torch.Tensor:
                    continue
                sequence_node_field_types = sequence_node.type.__annotations__
                field_name = sequence_node.type._fields[index_node]
                node.type = sequence_node_field_types[field_name]

# autonomy_module.py

# Autonomy Module for Node Self-Replication
class AutonomyModule:
    def __init__(self, nodes, replication_threshold):
        self.nodes = nodes
        self.replication_threshold = replication_threshold

    def check_and_replicate(self):
        for node in self.nodes:
            if node.memory_usage() >= self.replication_threshold:
                new_node = node.replicate()
                self.nodes.append(new_node)
                print(f"Node {node.node_id} replicated into Node {new_node.node_id}")

# base-analysis-node.py

# nodes/base_node.py

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
import logging
from queue import Queue
import threading
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass
class NodeState:
    """Node state information."""
    status: str
    energy: float
    memory_usage: float
    tasks_completed: int
    last_active: float
    error_count: int

class AnalysisNode(ABC):
    """
    Base class for all specialized analysis nodes.
    Provides common functionality and interface for analysis nodes.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, node_id: str, node_type: str):
        self.node_id = node_id
        self.node_type = node_type
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(f"node.{node_type}.{node_id}")
        
        # Queues for tasks and results
        self.input_queue = Queue()
        self.output_queue = Queue()
        
        # Node state
        self.state = NodeState(
            status='initialized',
            energy=100.0,
            memory_usage=0.0,
            tasks_completed=0,
            last_active=time.time(),
            error_count=0
        )
        
        # Threading
        self.active = False
        self.processing_thread = None
        
    def start(self):
        """Start node processing."""
        self.active = True
        self.state.status = 'active'
        self.processing_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._process_loop)
        self.processing_thread.daemon = True
        self.processing_thread.start()
        self.logger.info(f"Node {self.node_id} started")
        
    def stop(self):
        """Stop node processing."""
        self.active = False
        self.state.status = 'stopped'
        if self.processing_thread:
            self.processing_thread.join(timeout=5.0)
        self.logger.info(f"Node {self.node_id} stopped")
        
    def add_task(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Add a task to the node's input queue."""
        self.input_queue.put(task)
        self.state.last_active = time.time()
        
    def get_result(self, timeout: Optional[float] = None) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Get a result from the node's output queue."""
        try:
            return self.output_queue.get(timeout=timeout)
        except Queue.Empty:
            return None
            
    @abstractmethod
    def process(self, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Process input data. Must be implemented by subclasses."""
        pass
        
    @abstractmethod
    def can_handle(self, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        """Check if node can handle given data. Must be implemented by subclasses."""
        pass
        
    def _process_loop(self):
        """Main processing loop."""
        while self.active:
            try:
                # Get task from input queue
                task = self.input_queue.get(timeout=1.0)
                self.state.last_active = time.time()
                
                # Process task
                result = self.process(task)
                
                # Update state
                self.state.tasks_completed += 1
                self.output_queue.put(result)
                
            except Queue.Empty:
                continue
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Error processing task: {str(e)}")
                self.state.error_count += 1
                
                if self.state.error_count > 5:  # Max error threshold
                    self.state.status = 'error'
                    self.active = False
                    break
                    
    def update_energy(self, energy_change: float):
        """Update node energy."""
        self.state.energy = max(0.0, self.state.energy + energy_change)
        if self.state.energy <= 0:
            self.state.status = 'depleted'
            
    def update_memory_usage(self, usage: float):
        """Update node memory usage."""
        self.state.memory_usage = usage
        
    def get_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get node status."""
        return {
            'id': self.node_id,
            'type': self.node_type,
            'status': self.state.status,
            'energy': self.state.energy,
            'memory_usage': self.state.memory_usage,
            'tasks_completed': self.state.tasks_completed,
            'last_active': self.state.last_active,
            'error_count': self.state.error_count,
            'queue_sizes': {
                'input': self.input_queue.qsize(),
                'output': self.output_queue.qsize()
            }
        }
        
    def reset(self):
        """Reset node state."""
        # Clear queues
        while not self.input_queue.empty():
            self.input_queue.get()
        while not self.output_queue.empty():
            self.output_queue.get()
            
        # Reset state
        self.state = NodeState(
            status='active',
            energy=100.0,
            memory_usage=0.0,
            tasks_completed=0,
            last_active=time.time(),
            error_count=0
        )
        
        self.logger.info(
# base-node.py

from __future__ import annotations
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple, Any, Set
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import logging
import uuid
from datetime import datetime
from threading import Lock
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

from ..dna.structure import DNASequence
from ..dna.traits import TraitManager
from ...utils.validation import validate_data_chunk
from ...utils.metrics import NodeMetrics

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

@dataclass
class NodeState:
    """Represents the current state of a node."""
    energy: float
    health: float
    tasks_completed: int
    last_activity: datetime
    processing_capacity: float
    memory_usage: float
    connections: Set[str]
    state_hash: str = field(init=False)

    def __post_init__(self):
        self.update_state_hash()

    def update_state_hash(self):
        """Updates the state hash based on current values."""
        state_values = [
            self.energy,
            self.health,
            self.tasks_completed,
            self.processing_capacity,
            self.memory_usage
        ]
        self.state_hash = hash(tuple(state_values))

class BaseNode:
    """
    Base class for all processing nodes in the Kaleidoscope AI system.
    Handles core functionality including data processing, energy management,
    and DNA-based trait evolution.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        node_id: Optional[str] = None,
        initial_dna: Optional[DNASequence] = None,
        initial_energy: float = 100.0,
        parent_node: Optional[BaseNode] = None
    ):
        self.node_id = node_id or str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.dna = initial_dna or DNASequence(
            sequence_id=self.node_id,
            traits={},
            generation=0
        )
        
        # Initialize core components
        self.trait_manager = TraitManager()
        self.metrics = NodeMetrics(self.node_id)
        self._lock = Lock()
        
        # State management
        self.state = NodeState(
            energy=initial_energy,
            health=1.0,
            tasks_completed=0,
            last_activity=datetime.now(),
            processing_capacity=self.dna.traits.get('processing_speed', 0.5),
            memory_usage=0.0,
            connections=set()
        )
        
        # Processing queues
        self.input_queue: List[Dict] = []
        self.output_queue: List[Dict] = []
        self.processing_buffer: Dict = {}
        
        # Parent reference for inheritance
        self.parent_node = parent_node
        
        # Thread pool for parallel processing
        self.executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(
            max_workers=int(self.dna.traits.get('processing_speed', 0.5) * 10)
        )
        
        logger.info(f"Node {self.node_id} initialized with DNA generation {self.dna.generation}")

    async def process_data_chunk(self, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Processes a chunk of data using the node's traits and current state.
        
        Args:
            data: Input data chunk to process
            
        Returns:
            Processed data or None if processing failed
        """
        if not self._can_process():
            logger.warning(f"Node {self.node_id} cannot process data: insufficient energy or health")
            return None

        try:
            # Validate input data
            if not validate_data_chunk(data):
                raise ValueError("Invalid data chunk format")

            # Calculate energy cost
            energy_cost = self._calculate_energy_cost(data)
            if not self._consume_energy(energy_cost):
                return None

            async with self._lock:
                # Apply DNA traits to processing
                processing_rate = self.dna.traits.get('processing_speed', 0.5)
                efficiency = self.dna.traits.get('energy_efficiency', 0.5)
                
                # Process data using traits
                processed_data = await self._apply_processing(
                    data,
                    processing_rate,
                    efficiency
                )

                # Update metrics
                self.metrics.record_processing(
                    input_size=len(str(data)),
                    output_size=len(str(processed_data)),
                    energy_used=energy_cost,
                    processing_time=processing_rate
                )

                # Update state
                self.state.tasks_completed += 1
                self.state.last_activity = datetime.now()
                self.state.update_state_hash()

                return processed_data

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error processing data in node {self.node_id}: {str(e)}")
            self.state.health -= 0.1  # Penalty for processing failure
            return None

    async def _apply_processing(
        self,
        data: Dict[str, Any],
        processing_rate: float,
        efficiency: float
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Applies the actual data processing based on node traits.
        
        Args:
            data: Input data to process
            processing_rate: Speed of processing
            efficiency: Energy efficiency factor
            
        Returns:
            Processed data
        """
        processed_data = {}
        
        # Process each field based on node's traits
        for key, value in data.items():
            if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
                # Numerical processing
                processed_data[key] = self._process_numerical(
                    value,
                    processing_rate,
                    efficiency
                )
            elif isinstance(value, str):
                # Text processing
                processed_data[key] = self._process_text(
                    value,
                    processing_rate
                )
            elif isinstance(value, (list, dict)):
                # Recursive processing for nested structures
                processed_data[key] = await self._process_complex(
                    value,
                    processing_rate,
                    efficiency
                )
            else:
                processed_data[key] = value

        # Apply node's specialization if any
        if 'specialization' in self.dna.traits:
            processed_data = self._apply_specialization(processed_data)

        return processed_data

    def _process_numerical(
        self,
        value: float,
        processing_rate: float,
        efficiency: float
    ) -> float:
        """Processes numerical data using node traits."""
        # Apply processing noise based on efficiency
        noise = np.random.normal(0, 1 - efficiency)
        processed = value + (noise * (1 - processing_rate))
        
        # Apply trait-based transformations
        if 'numerical_accuracy' in self.dna.traits:
            accuracy = self.dna.traits['numerical_accuracy']
            processed = round(processed, int(accuracy * 10))
            
        return processed

    def _process_text(self, text: str, processing_rate: float) -> str:
        """Processes text data using node traits."""
        # Apply node's language processing capabilities
        if len(text) > 100:  # Only process longer texts
            words = text.split()
            processed_words = []
            
            for word in words:
                if len(word) > 3:  # Process only significant words
                    if 'language_understanding' in self.dna.traits:
                        understanding = self.dna.traits['language_understanding']
                        if np.random.random() < understanding:
                            word = self._enhance_word(word)
                processed_words.append(word)
                
            return ' '.join(processed_words)
        return text

    def _enhance_word(self, word: str) -> str:
        """Enhances a word based on node's language understanding."""
        # Placeholder for more sophisticated language processing
        return word.lower() if np.random.random() < 0.5 else word.upper()

    async def _process_complex(
        self,
        data: Any,
        processing_rate: float,
        efficiency: float
    ) -> Any:
        """Processes complex nested data structures."""
        if isinstance(data, list):
            return [
                await self._apply_processing(
                    item if isinstance(item, dict) else {'value': item},
                    processing_rate,
                    efficiency
                ) for item in data
            ]
        elif isinstance(data, dict):
            return await self._apply_processing(
                data,
                processing_rate,
                efficiency
            )
        return data

    def _apply_specialization(self, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Applies node specialization to processed data."""
        specialization = self.dna.traits['specialization']
        if specialization > 0.8:  # High specialization
            # Apply advanced processing based on specialization
            data['specialization_factor'] = specialization
            data['processing_quality'] = self.state.health * specialization
            
        return data

    def _calculate_energy_cost(self, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> float:
        """Calculates energy cost for processing a data chunk."""
        base_cost = len(str(data)) * 0.01
        efficiency = self.dna.traits.get('energy_efficiency', 0.5)
        return base_cost * (2 - efficiency)

    def _consume_energy(self, amount: float) -> bool:
        """
        Attempts to consume energy for processing.
        
        Returns:
            bool: Whether energy consumption was successful
        """
        with self._lock:
            if self.state.energy >= amount:
                self.state.energy -= amount
                return True
            return False

    def _can_process(self) -> bool:
        """Checks if the node is capable of processing data."""
        return (
            self.state.energy > 10.0 and
            self.state.health > 0.3 and
            not self.processing_buffer
        )

    async def replicate(self) -> Optional[BaseNode]:
        """
        Creates a copy of the node with potentially mutated DNA.
        
        Returns:
            Optional[BaseNode]: New node instance or None if replication fails
        """
        if self.state.energy < 50.0:  # Minimum energy for replication
            return None

        # Create mutated DNA for new node
        new_dna = self.dna.mutate(environmental_pressure=1.0)
        
        # Create new node
        new_node = self.__class__(
            node_id=f"{self.node_id}_offspring_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}",
            initial_dna=new_dna,
            initial_energy=self.state.energy * 0.4,  # Transfer some energy
            parent_node=self
        )
        
        # Reduce parent's energy
        self.state.energy *= 0.6
        
        logger.info(f"Node {self.node_id} replicated to create node {new_node.node_id}")
        return new_node

    def connect_to(self, other_node: BaseNode) -> bool:
        """Establishes a connection with another node."""
        if other_node.node_id not in self.state.connections:
            self.state.connections.add(other_node.node_id)
            other_node.state.connections.add(self.node_id)
            return True
        return False

    def get_state(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Returns the current state of the node."""
        return {
            'node_id': self.node_id,
            'energy': self.state.energy,
            'health': self.state.health,
            'tasks_completed': self.state.tasks_completed,
            'dna_generation': self.dna.generation,
            'connections': len(self.state.connections),
            'last_activity': self.state.last_activity.isoformat(),
            'metrics': self.metrics.get_summary()
        }

    def __del__(self):
        """Cleanup when the node is destroyed."""
        self.executor.shutdown(wait=False)
        logger.info(f"Node {self.node_id} destroyed")

# base_node.py

# base_node.py

import time
import uuid
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any
from memory import Memory
from core_laws import CoreLaws
from understanding_metrics import UnderstandingMetrics
import numpy as np

class BaseNode:
    """Foundation for all node types"""
    def __init__(self, node_type: str, core_laws: CoreLaws):
        self.id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.type = node_type
        self.laws = core_laws
        self.energy = 10.0
        self.birth_time = time.time()
        self.memories: Dict[str, Memory] = {}
        self.connections: Dict[str, float] = {}
        self.mirror_connection: Optional[str] = None
        self.specialization: Dict[str, float] = {}
        self.experience_history: list = []
        self.understanding = UnderstandingMetrics()

    def update(self) -> bool:
        """Natural update cycle"""
        # Energy decay
        self.energy *= (1.0 - self.laws.energy_decay_rate)

        # Memory maintenance
        self._maintain_memories()

        # Connection decay
        self._decay_connections()

        # Update understanding
        self._update_understanding()

        # Return survival status
        return self.energy > self.laws.minimum_survival_energy

    def _maintain_memories(self):
        """Maintain memory systems"""
        # Decay memories
        self.memories = {
            mem_id: mem for mem_id, mem in self.memories.items()
            if mem.decay(self.laws.energy_decay_rate)
        }

        # Fuse similar memories
        self._fuse_memories()

    def _fuse_memories(self):
        """Fuse similar memories based on content similarity"""
        memory_ids = list(self.memories.keys())
        for i in range(len(memory_ids)):
            for j in range(i + 1, len(memory_ids)):
                mem1 = self.memories[memory_ids[i]]
                mem2 = self.memories[memory_ids[j]]
                similarity = mem1.validate(mem2.content)
                if similarity > self.laws.memory_fusion_threshold:
                    self._perform_memory_fusion(mem1, mem2)
                    break  # Restart after fusion to avoid key errors
            else:
                continue
            break

    def _perform_memory_fusion(self, mem1: Memory, mem2: Memory):
        """Fuse two memories into one"""
        # Combine content, connections, and strengths
        fused_content = self._combine_contents(mem1.content, mem2.content)
        fused_connections = {**mem1.connections, **mem2.connections}
        fused_strength = (mem1.strength + mem2.strength) / 2

        # Create new memory
        new_memory = Memory(
            content=fused_content,
            memory_type=mem1.memory_type,
            connections=fused_connections,
            strength=fused_strength
        )

        # Remove old memories and add new one
        del self.memories[mem1.content]
        del self.memories[mem2.content]
        self.memories[new_memory.content] = new_memory

    def _combine_contents(self, content1: Any, content2: Any) -> Any:
        """Combine contents of two memories"""
        if isinstance(content1, str) and isinstance(content2, str):
            return content1 + " " + content2
        elif isinstance(content1, np.ndarray) and isinstance(content2, np.ndarray):
            return (content1 + content2) / 2
        else:
            return content1  # Fallback to one of the contents

    def _decay_connections(self):
        """Decay connections over time"""
        self.connections = {
            conn_id: strength * (1.0 - self.laws.connection_decay_rate)
            for conn_id, strength in self.connections.items()
            if strength * (1.0 - self.laws.connection_decay_rate) > 0.1
        }

    def _update_understanding(self):
        """Update understanding metrics"""
        # Information score based on valid memories
        valid_memories = sum(1 for mem in self.memories.values() if mem.validation_score > 0.7)
        total_memories = len(self.memories)
        if total_memories > 0:
            self.understanding.information_score = valid_memories / total_memories

        # Reasoning score based on connections
        self.understanding.reasoning_score = min(1.0, len(self.connections) / 100)

        # Probability score can be updated based on predictions (if implemented)

    def can_split(self) -> bool:
        """Determine if node should split"""
        energy_condition = self.energy >= self.laws.minimum_survival_energy * 2
        memory_condition = len(self.memories) >= 100
        return energy_condition and memory_condition


# capability_evolution.py

# capability_evolution.py

from typing import Dict, List
from core.organic_node import OrganicNode

class CapabilityEvolution:
    """Manages the emergence of new capabilities in the network"""

    def __init__(self, nodes: Dict[str, OrganicNode]):
        self.nodes = nodes  # All active nodes
        self.capabilities = {"speech_synthesis": False, "object_detection": False}
        self.thresholds = {
            "speech_synthesis": {"text_understanding": 0.8, "node_count": 10},
            "object_detection": {"visual_recognition": 0.8, "node_count": 10},
        }

    def evaluate_capabilities(self):
        """Evaluates nodes for potential capability evolution"""
        performance_metrics = self._gather_metrics()

        for capability, requirements in self.thresholds.items():
            if all(performance_metrics[metric] >= threshold for metric, threshold in requirements.items()):
                self.capabilities[capability] = True

    def _gather_metrics(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """Aggregates performance metrics from all nodes"""
        metrics = {"text_understanding": 0.0, "visual_recognition": 0.0, "node_count": len(self.nodes)}
        total_text_score = 0.0
        total_visual_score = 0.0
        text_nodes = 0
        visual_nodes = 0

        for node in self.nodes.values():
            if node.specialization == "text":
                total_text_score += node.calculate_understanding()
                text_nodes += 1
            elif node.specialization == "visual":
                total_visual_score += node.calculate_understanding()
                visual_nodes += 1

        if text_nodes > 0:
            metrics["text_understanding"] = total_text_score / text_nodes
        if visual_nodes > 0:
            metrics["visual_recognition"] = total_visual_score / visual_nodes

        return metrics

    def trigger_capabilities(self):
        """Activates evolved capabilities"""
        activated = []
        if self.capabilities["speech_synthesis"]:
            self._activate_speech_synthesis()
            activated.append("speech_synthesis")
        if self.capabilities["object_detection"]:
            self._activate_object_detection()
            activated.append("object_detection")
        return activated

    def _activate_speech_synthesis(self):
        """Implements speech synthesis across relevant nodes"""
        for node in self.nodes.values():
            if node.specialization == "text":
                node.add_capability("speech_synthesis")

    def _activate_object_detection(self):
        """Implements object detection across relevant nodes"""
        for node in self.nodes.values():
            if node.specialization == "visual":
                node.add_capability("object_detection")

# cluster_formation.py

# File: cluster_formation.py
# Description: Nodes dynamically form clusters based on their specialization.

class ClusterManager:
    """Manages the formation and specialization of clusters in the network."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.clusters = []  # List of clusters (each cluster is a list of nodes)
        self.logs = []

    def form_clusters(self, network):
        """Groups nodes into clusters based on their knowledge similarity."""
        knowledge_sets = [set(node.dna["knowledge"]) for node in network.nodes]
        cluster_map = {}

        for i, node in enumerate(network.nodes):
            for j, knowledge in enumerate(knowledge_sets):
                if len(knowledge.intersection(node.dna["knowledge"])) > 2:  # Similar knowledge threshold
                    if j not in cluster_map:
                        cluster_map[j] = []
                    cluster_map[j].append(node)

        self.clusters = list(cluster_map.values())
        self.logs.append(f"Formed {len(self.clusters)} clusters.")
        return self.clusters

    def assign_cluster_task(self, task):
        """Assigns a task to the most relevant cluster."""
        for cluster in self.clusters:
            if any(task in node.dna["knowledge"] for node in cluster):
                for node in cluster:
                    node.perceive(task)
                self.logs.append(f"Task '{task}' assigned to cluster with {len(cluster)} nodes.")
                return f"Task '{task}' assigned to cluster."
        return f"No cluster could handle the task '{task}'."

    def status(self):
        """Displays the current cluster status."""
        return {
            "Clusters": len(self.clusters),
            "Logs": self.logs[-10:],  # Last 10 log entries
        }


# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Create a network and cluster manager
    network = CollaborationNetwork()

    print("\n--- Task Assignment Result ---")
    print(task_result)


# clustering_optimization.py

# File: clustering_optimization.py

import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
from sklearn.metrics import silhouette_score
import logging
from sklearn.cluster import KMeans
from scipy.cluster.hierarchy import linkage, fcluster

# Configure logging
logging.basicConfig(
    filename="clustering_optimization.log",
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s"
)

def optimal_clusters_kmeans(data: pd.DataFrame, min_clusters: int = 2, max_clusters: int = 10) -> int:
    """
    Determines the optimal number of clusters for KMeans using Silhouette Score.

    Parameters:
        data (pd.DataFrame): Preprocessed data.
        min_clusters (int): Minimum number of clusters to consider.
        max_clusters (int): Maximum number of clusters to consider.

    Returns:
        int: Optimal number of clusters.
    """
    best_score = -1
    optimal_k = min_clusters
    for k in range(min_clusters, max_clusters + 1):
        kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=k, random_state=42)
        cluster_labels = kmeans.fit_predict(data)
        score = silhouette_score(data, cluster_labels)
        logging.info(f"Silhouette Score for k={k}: {score}")
        if score > best_score:
            best_score = score
            optimal_k = k
    logging.info(f"Optimal number of clusters determined: {optimal_k}")
    return optimal_k

def optimal_clusters_hierarchical(data: pd.DataFrame, max_clusters: int = 10) -> int:
    """
    Determines the optimal number of clusters for Hierarchical Clustering using Silhouette Score.

    Parameters:
        data (pd.DataFrame): Preprocessed data.
        max_clusters (int): Maximum number of clusters to consider.

    Returns:
        int: Optimal number of clusters.
    """
    best_score = -1
    optimal_k = 2
    linkage_matrix = linkage(data, method='ward')
    for k in range(2, max_clusters + 1):
        cluster_labels = fcluster(linkage_matrix, k, criterion='maxclust')
        score = silhouette_score(data, cluster_labels)
        logging.info(f"Silhouette Score for k={k}: {score}")
        if score > best_score:
            best_score = score
            optimal_k = k
    logging.info(f"Optimal number of clusters for Hierarchical Clustering determined: {optimal_k}")
    return optimal_k

# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Example data
    data = pd.DataFrame({
        "feature1": np.random.rand(100),
        "feature2": np.random.rand(100),
        "feature3": np.random.rand(100)
    })

    # Find optimal clusters for KMeans
    optimal_k = optimal_clusters_kmeans(data)
    print(f"Optimal clusters for KMeans: {optimal_k}")

    # Find optimal clusters for Hierarchical Clustering
    optimal_h = optimal_clusters_hierarchical(data)
    print(f"Optimal clusters for Hierarchical Clustering: {optimal_h}")

# cluster_manager.py

# node_management/cluster_manager.py

import logging
import random

# Configure logging for ClusterManager
logging.basicConfig(
    filename="cluster_manager.log",
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s"
)

class ClusterManager:
    def __init__(self, dynamic_node_management=False, enable_supernode_integration=False):
        """
        Manages the formation and dynamic adjustment of clusters based on node activity and resource utilization.

        Args:
            dynamic_node_management (bool): Enables dynamic management of nodes within clusters.
            enable_supernode_integration (bool): Enables integration with supernodes for hierarchical clustering.
        """
        self.clusters = {}
        self.dynamic_node_management = dynamic_node_management
        self.enable_supernode_integration = enable_supernode_integration

    def form_clusters(self, nodes):
        """
        Forms initial clusters based on node characteristics and resource requirements.

        Args:
            nodes (list): List of nodes to be clustered.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        # Implement your clustering logic here
        # This is a placeholder, replace with actual clustering algorithm
        self.clusters = self._simple_clustering(nodes)
        logging.info(f"Formed initial clusters: {self.clusters}")

    def _simple_clustering(self, nodes):
        """
        Performs a simple clustering based on even distribution of nodes.

        Args:
            nodes (list): List of nodes to cluster.

        Returns:
            dict: Dictionary representing clusters and their nodes.
        """
        num_clusters = 3  # Example: Fixed number of clusters
        clusters = {i: [] for i in range(num_clusters)}
        for i, node in enumerate(nodes):
            cluster_id = i % num_clusters
            clusters[cluster_id].append(node)
        return clusters

    def adjust_clusters(self):
        """
        Dynamically adjusts clusters based on node activity, resource utilization, and potential anomalies.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        if self.dynamic_node_management:
            # Implement dynamic cluster adjustment logic here
            # This is a placeholder, replace with actual adjustment algorithm
            self._simulate_cluster_adjustment()
            logging.info("Clusters adjusted dynamically.")

    def _simulate_cluster_adjustment(self):
        """
        Simulates cluster adjustment by randomly moving nodes between clusters.
        """
        # Example: Randomly move a node to a different cluster
        if self.clusters:
            cluster_ids = list(self.clusters.keys())
            from_cluster = random.choice(cluster_ids)
            to_cluster = random.choice(cluster_ids)
            if self.clusters[from_cluster]:
                node = self.clusters[from_cluster].pop()
                self.clusters[to_cluster].append(node)

    def generate_superclusters(self):
        """
        Generates superclusters by grouping clusters based on higher-level patterns or objectives.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        if self.enable_supernode_integration:
            # Implement supercluster generation logic here
            # This is a placeholder, replace with actual supercluster generation algorithm
            self._simulate_supercluster_generation()
            logging.info("Superclusters generated.")

    def _simulate_supercluster_generation(self):
        """
        Simulates supercluster generation by grouping clusters.
        """
        # Example: Group clusters into a single supercluster
        if self.clusters:
            supercluster = {
                "id": "supercluster_1",
                "clusters": list(self.clusters.keys())
            }
            logging.info(f"Simulated supercluster: {supercluster}")

# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    cluster_manager = ClusterManager(dynamic_node_management=True, enable_supernode_integration=True)
    nodes = [{"id": f"node_{i}"} for i in range(10)]
    cluster_manager.form_clusters(nodes)
    cluster_manager.adjust_clusters()
    cluster_manager.generate_superclusters()

# cluster-system.py

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Dict, List, Set, Optional, Tuple
import networkx as nx
import numpy as np
from datetime import datetime
import uuid

@dataclass
class DomainNode:
    """
    Represents a specialized node containing domain-specific knowledge.
    """
    node_id: str
    domain: str
    knowledge: StandardizedData
    connections: Set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    creation_time: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().isoformat())
    confidence: float = 1.0

class ClusterManager:
    """
    Manages the creation and organization of domain-specific node clusters.
    """
    def __init__(self):
        self.cluster_graph = nx.Graph()
        self.domain_nodes: Dict[str, DomainNode] = {}
        self.clusters: Dict[str, Set[str]] = {}
        self.similarity_threshold = 0.7

    def create_domain_node(self, insight: Dict[str, Any]) -> DomainNode:
        """
        Creates a new domain node from a generated insight.
        """
        node_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        domain = self._extract_domain(insight['data'])
        
        domain_node = DomainNode(
            node_id=node_id,
            domain=domain,
            knowledge=insight['data'],
            confidence=insight['data'].confidence
        )
        
        self.domain_nodes[node_id] = domain_node
        self.cluster_graph.add_node(
            node_id,
            domain=domain,
            confidence=domain_node.confidence
        )
        
        return domain_node

    def _extract_domain(self, data: StandardizedData) -> str:
        """
        Extracts the domain classification from standardized data.
        """
        domain_indicators = {
            "cell": ["membrane", "phospholipids", "protein", "nucleus"],
            "chemistry": ["compound", "reaction", "molecule", "bond"],
            "physics": ["force", "energy", "mass", "velocity"]
            # Add more domains as needed
        }

        text_content = ""
        if data.data_type == "text":
            text_content = data.raw_data
        elif data.data_type == "structured":
            text_content = str(data.metadata)

        # Count domain-specific terms
        domain_scores = {}
        for domain, indicators in domain_indicators.items():
            score = sum(1 for indicator in indicators if indicator in text_content.lower())
            if score > 0:
                domain_scores[domain] = score

        if domain_scores:
            return max(domain_scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1])[0]
        return "general"

    def update_clusters(self):
        """
        Updates cluster assignments based on node relationships and domain knowledge.
        """
        # Reset clusters
        self.clusters = {}
        
        # Find connected components in the cluster graph
        components = nx.connected_components(self.cluster_graph)
        
        for idx, component in enumerate(components):
            # Check if nodes in component belong to the same domain
            domains = {self.domain_nodes[node_id].domain for node_id in component}
            
            if len(domains) == 1:
                # Single domain cluster
                domain = domains.pop()
                cluster_id = f"{domain}_cluster_{idx}"
                self.clusters[cluster_id] = set(component)
            else:
                # Mixed domain cluster - split if necessary
                self._handle_mixed_domain_cluster(component, idx)

    def _handle_mixed_domain_cluster(self, component: Set[str], idx: int):
        """
        Handles clusters containing nodes from multiple domains.
        """
        # Group nodes by domain
        domain_groups = {}
        for node_id in component:
            domain = self.domain_nodes[node_id].domain
            if domain not in domain_groups:
                domain_groups[domain] = set()
            domain_groups[domain].add(node_id)

        # Create separate clusters for each domain
        for domain, nodes in domain_groups.items():
            cluster_id = f"{domain}_cluster_{idx}_{len(self.clusters)}"
            self.clusters[cluster_id] = nodes

    def add_connection(self, node1_id: str, node2_id: str, weight: float = 1.0):
        """
        Adds a weighted connection between two nodes.
        """
        if node1_id in self.domain_nodes and node2_id in self.domain_nodes:
            self.cluster_graph.add_edge(node1_id, node2_id, weight=weight)
            self.domain_nodes[node1_id].connections.add(node2_id)
            self.domain_nodes[node2_id].connections.add(node1_id)

    def calculate_node_similarity(self, node1_id: str, node2_id: str) -> float:
        """
        Calculates similarity between two nodes based on their knowledge and domain.
        """
        node1 = self.domain_nodes[node1_id]
        node2 = self.domain_nodes[node2_id]

        # Domain similarity
        domain_similarity = 1.0 if node1.domain == node2.domain else 0.0

        # Knowledge similarity based on metadata
        knowledge_similarity = self._calculate_knowledge_similarity(
            node1.knowledge,
            node2.knowledge
        )

        # Weighted combination
        return 0.6 * domain_similarity + 0.4 * knowledge_similarity

    def _calculate_knowledge_similarity(
        self,
        knowledge1: StandardizedData,
        knowledge2: StandardizedData
    ) -> float:
        """
        Calculates similarity between two knowledge representations.
        """
        # Compare metadata
        common_keys = set(knowledge1.metadata.keys()) & set(knowledge2.metadata.keys())
        if not common_keys:
            return 0.0

        metadata_similarity = sum(
            1 for key in common_keys
            if knowledge1.metadata[key] == knowledge2.metadata[key]
        ) / len(common_keys)

        # Compare relationships
        relationship_similarity = len(
            set(knowledge1.relationships) & set(knowledge2.relationships)
        ) / max(
            len(knowledge1.relationships) + len(knowledge2.relationships),
            1
        )

        return 0.7 * metadata_similarity + 0.3 * relationship_similarity

    def get_cluster_summary(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Generates a summary of current cluster state.
        """
        summary = {
            "total_nodes": len(self.domain_nodes),
            "total_clusters": len(self.clusters),
            "clusters": {}
        }

        for cluster_id, nodes in self.clusters.items():
            cluster_info = {
                "size": len(nodes),
                "domain": cluster_id.split("_")[0],
                "average_confidence": np.mean([
                    self.domain_nodes[node_id].confidence
                    for node_id in nodes
                ]),
                "nodes": [
                    {
                        "id": node_id,
                        "connections": len(self.domain_nodes[node_id].connections)
                    }
                    for node_id in nodes
                ]
            }
            summary["clusters"][cluster_id] = cluster_info

        return summary

    def visualize_clusters(self) -> nx.Graph:
        """
        Returns a graph representation of clusters for visualization.
        """
        visualization_graph = self.cluster_graph.copy()
        
        # Add cluster information to nodes
        for cluster_id, nodes in self.clusters.items():
            for node_id in nodes:
                visualization_graph.nodes[node_id]["cluster"] = cluster_id
                visualization_graph.nodes[node_id]["domain"] = self.domain_nodes[node_id].domain
                
        return visualization_graph

"""
AST nodes specific to the C family of languages

from sympy.codegen.ast import (
    Attribute, Declaration, Node, String, Token, Type, none,
    FunctionCall, CodeBlock
    )
from sympy.core.basic import Basic
from sympy.core.containers import Tuple
from sympy.core.sympify import sympify

void = Type('void')

restrict = Attribute('restrict')  # guarantees no pointer aliasing
volatile = Attribute('volatile')
static = Attribute('static')


def alignof(arg):
    """ Generate of FunctionCall instance for calling 'alignof' """
    return FunctionCall('alignof', [String(arg) if isinstance(arg, str) else arg])


def sizeof(arg):
    """ Generate of FunctionCall instance for calling 'sizeof'

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy.codegen.ast import real
    >>> from sympy.codegen.cnodes import sizeof
    >>> from sympy import ccode
    >>> ccode(sizeof(real))
    'sizeof(double)'
    """
    return FunctionCall('sizeof', [String(arg) if isinstance(arg, str) else arg])


class CommaOperator(Basic):
    """ Represents the comma operator in C """
    def __new__(cls, *args):
        return Basic.__new__(cls, *[sympify(arg) for arg in args])


class Label(Node):
    """ Label for use with e.g. goto statement.

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy import ccode, Symbol
    >>> from sympy.codegen.cnodes import Label, PreIncrement
    >>> print(ccode(Label('foo')))
    foo:
    >>> print(ccode(Label('bar', [PreIncrement(Symbol('a'))])))
    bar:
    ++(a);

    """
    __slots__ = _fields = ('name', 'body')
    defaults = {'body': none}
    _construct_name = String

    @classmethod
    def _construct_body(cls, itr):
        if isinstance(itr, CodeBlock):
            return itr
        else:
            return CodeBlock(*itr)


class goto(Token):
    """ Represents goto in C """
    __slots__ = _fields = ('label',)
    _construct_label = Label


class PreDecrement(Basic):
    """ Represents the pre-decrement operator

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy.abc import x
    >>> from sympy.codegen.cnodes import PreDecrement
    >>> from sympy import ccode
    >>> ccode(PreDecrement(x))
    '--(x)'

    """
    nargs = 1


class PostDecrement(Basic):
    """ Represents the post-decrement operator

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy.abc import x
    >>> from sympy.codegen.cnodes import PostDecrement
    >>> from sympy import ccode
    >>> ccode(PostDecrement(x))
    '(x)--'

    """
    nargs = 1


class PreIncrement(Basic):
    """ Represents the pre-increment operator

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy.abc import x
    >>> from sympy.codegen.cnodes import PreIncrement
    >>> from sympy import ccode
    >>> ccode(PreIncrement(x))
    '++(x)'

    """
    nargs = 1


class PostIncrement(Basic):
    """ Represents the post-increment operator

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy.abc import x
    >>> from sympy.codegen.cnodes import PostIncrement
    >>> from sympy import ccode
    >>> ccode(PostIncrement(x))
    '(x)++'

    """
    nargs = 1


class struct(Node):
    """ Represents a struct in C """
    __slots__ = _fields = ('name', 'declarations')
    defaults = {'name': none}
    _construct_name = String

    @classmethod
    def _construct_declarations(cls, args):
        return Tuple(*[Declaration(arg) for arg in args])


class union(struct):
    """ Represents a union in C """
    __slots__ = ()

# collaborative_reasoning.py

# File: collaborative_reasoning.py
# Description: Enables collaborative reasoning among nodes for group insights.

class CollaborativeReasoning:
    """Facilitates reasoning and insight generation through node collaboration."""
    
    def __init__(self, network):
        self.network = network
        self.collaborative_logs = []
    
    def propose_group_hypothesis(self):
        """Aggregates knowledge from nodes to propose a group hypothesis."""
        collective_knowledge = set()
        for node in self.network.nodes:
            collective_knowledge.update(node.dna["knowledge"])
        
        if len(collective_knowledge) < 3:
            hypothesis = "Insufficient group knowledge for hypothesis generation."
        else:
            hypothesis = f"Group Hypothesis: {random.choice(list(collective_knowledge))} may explain observed patterns."
        self.collaborative_logs.append(f"Proposed: {hypothesis}")
        return hypothesis

    def refine_group_hypothesis(self, hypothesis):
        """Refines the group hypothesis through collaborative reasoning."""
        refined_hypothesis = f"Refined Hypothesis: {hypothesis.replace('may', 'likely')}"
        self.collaborative_logs.append(f"Refined: {refined_hypothesis}")
        return refined_hypothesis

    def synthesize_group_insights(self):
        """Generates group insights based on refined hypotheses."""
        hypothesis = self.propose_group_hypothesis()
        refined = self.refine_group_hypothesis(hypothesis)
        insight = f"Group Insight: {refined} suggests new exploration pathways."
        self.collaborative_logs.append(f"Synthesized: {insight}")
        return insight

    def status(self):
        """Returns the collaborative reasoning status."""
        return {"Collaborative Logs": self.collaborative_logs[-10:]}


# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Create a collaborative reasoning system for the network
    network = CollaborationNetwork()
    for _ in range(5):
        network.add_node(AdvancedLogicalNode())

    collaborative_reasoning = CollaborativeReasoning(network)

    # Propose, refine, and synthesize insights
    hypothesis = collaborative_reasoning.propose_group_hypothesis()
    refined_hypothesis = collaborative_reasoning.refine_group_hypothesis(hypothesis)
    group_insight = collaborative_reasoning.synthesize_group_insights()

    # Display results
    print("\n--- Collaborative Reasoning Logs ---")
    print(collaborative_reasoning.status())
    print("\n--- Group Insight ---")
    print(group_insight)

# core_laws.py

from dataclasses import dataclass
import numpy as np

@dataclass
class CoreLaws:
    """Fundamental laws governing system behavior - like physics"""
    energy_decay_rate: float = 0.01
    energy_transfer_efficiency: float = 0.85
    memory_fusion_threshold: float = 0.7
    connection_decay_rate: float = 0.05
    mutation_rate: float = 0.01
    minimum_survival_energy: float = 1.0
    understanding_threshold: float = 0.8

    def mutate(self) -> 'CoreLaws':
        """Allow slight mutations in fundamental laws"""
        return CoreLaws(
            energy_decay_rate=max(0.001, self.energy_decay_rate * np.random.normal(1, 0.1)),
            energy_transfer_efficiency=min(0.99, self.energy_transfer_efficiency * np.random.normal(1, 0.1)),
            memory_fusion_threshold=max(0.5, self.memory_fusion_threshold * np.random.normal(1, 0.1)),
            connection_decay_rate=max(0.01, self.connection_decay_rate * np.random.normal(1, 0.1)),
            mutation_rate=max(0.001, self.mutation_rate * np.random.normal(1, 0.1)),
            minimum_survival_energy=max(0.5, self.minimum_survival_energy * np.random.normal(1, 0.1)),
            understanding_threshold=max(0.6, self.understanding_threshold * np.random.normal(1, 0.1))
        )

# core_node.py

import uuid
import numpy as np

class Node:
    def __init__(self, dna=None, parent_id=None):
        self.node_id = uuid.uuid4()
        self.parent_id = parent_id
        self.dna = dna if dna else self.generate_dna()
        self.memory = {}
        self.energy = 100
        self.threshold = 75  # Energy level to create a new node
        self.children = []

    def generate_dna(self):
        """Generate DNA-like instructions for node creation and replication."""
        return np.random.rand(10)

    def learn(self, input_data):
        """Store learned information in memory."""
        self.memory[uuid.uuid4()] = input_data

    def replicate(self):
        """Create a new node using its DNA."""
        if self.energy >= self.threshold:
            child_dna = self.dna + np.random.normal(0, 0.1, size=len(self.dna))
            new_node = Node(dna=np.clip(child_dna, 0, 1), parent_id=self.node_id)
            self.children.append(new_node)
            self.energy -= 30
            return new_node
        return None

    def share_resources(self, other_node):
        """Share energy and information with another node."""
        if self.energy > 50:
            self.energy -= 10
            other_node.energy += 10
            other_node.memory.update(self.memory)

    def __repr__(self):
        return f"Node({self.node_id}, Energy={self.energy}, Children={len(self.children)})"

# custom_node_behaviors2.py

# File: custom_node_behaviors.py

from typing import Dict
import logging

# Configure logging
logging.basicConfig(
    filename="custom_node_behaviors.log",
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s"
)

class Node:
    """
    Represents a node with customizable behaviors.
    """
    def __init__(self, node_id: str, energy: float, knowledge: float, role: str = "general"):
        self.node_id = node_id
        self.energy = energy
        self.knowledge = knowledge
        self.role = role
        self.behaviors = []  # List of behaviors assigned to the node

    def assign_behavior(self, behavior: str):
        """
        Assigns a specific behavior to the node.

        Parameters:
            behavior (str): The behavior to assign (e.g., "computation", "storage").
        """
        if behavior not in self.behaviors:
            self.behaviors.append(behavior)
            logging.info(f"Behavior '{behavior}' assigned to Node {self.node_id}.")

    def perform_behavior(self):
        """
        Executes assigned behaviors based on the node's role and energy levels.
        """
        for behavior in self.behaviors:
            if self.energy > 10:  # Example condition to execute behaviors
                logging.info(f"Node {self.node_id} performing behavior '{behavior}' with energy {self.energy}.")
                if behavior == "computation":
                    self._perform_computation()
                elif behavior == "storage":
                    self._perform_storage()
                else:
                    logging.warning(f"Unknown behavior '{behavior}' for Node {self.node_id}.")
            else:
                logging.warning(f"Node {self.node_id} lacks sufficient energy to perform '{behavior}'.")

    def _perform_computation(self):
        """Simulates a computation task."""
        self.energy -= 5
        self.knowledge += 10
        logging.info(f"Node {self.node_id} completed a computation task. Energy: {self.energy}, Knowledge: {self.knowledge}.")

    def _perform_storage(self):
        """Simulates a storage task."""
        self.energy -= 2
        logging.info(f"Node {self.node_id} completed a storage task. Energy: {self.energy}.")

class NodeBehaviorManager:
    """
    Manages node behaviors and roles across the system.
    """
    def __init__(self, nodes: Dict[str, Node]):
        self.nodes = nodes

    def assign_roles(self):
        """
        Assigns roles to nodes dynamically based on their metrics.
        """
        for node in self.nodes.values():
            if node.energy > 50 and node.knowledge < 30:
                node.assign_behavior("computation")
            elif node.energy < 50:
                node.assign_behavior("storage")

    def execute_all_behaviors(self):
        """
        Executes behaviors for all nodes.
        """
        for node in self.nodes.values():
            node.perform_behavior()

# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Initialize nodes
    nodes = {
        "Node-1": Node("Node-1", energy=60, knowledge=20),
        "Node-2": Node("Node-2", energy=30, knowledge=50),
        "Node-3": Node("Node-3", energy=80, knowledge=10),
        "Node-4": Node("Node-4", energy=40, knowledge=40)
    }

    # Initialize the behavior manager
    manager = NodeBehaviorManager(nodes)

    # Assign roles dynamically
    manager.assign_roles()

    # Execute behaviors for all nodes
    manager.execute_all_behaviors()

    # Print final node states
    for node_id, node in nodes.items():
        print(f"{node_id}: Energy={node.energy}, Knowledge={node.knowledge}, Behaviors={node.behaviors}")

from typing import Dict

class Node:

        Executes assigned behaviors based on the node's role and energy levels.

                    self._perform_computation()
                elif behavior == "storage":
                    self._perform_storage()

# cxxnodes.py

"""
AST nodes specific to C++.
"""

from sympy.codegen.ast import Attribute, String, Token, Type, none

class using(Token):
    """ Represents a 'using' statement in C++ """
    __slots__ = _fields = ('type', 'alias')
    defaults = {'alias': none}
    _construct_type = Type
    _construct_alias = String

constexpr = Attribute('constexpr')

# digital_being.py

# File: digital_being.py
# Description: A fully autonomous digital being with foundational instincts and capabilities.

import uuid
import random


class DigitalNode:
    """Represents a single autonomous digital being."""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.node_id = str(uuid.uuid4())  # Unique identifier
        self.dna = {
            "instincts": self._initialize_instincts(),
            "traits": self._initialize_traits(),
            "knowledge": [],  # Learned information
            "environment_interactions": [],  # Interactions with the environment
        }
        self.resources = {"energy": 100, "memory": 100}
        self.logs = []
    
    def _initialize_instincts(self):
        """Defines the primal instincts of the node."""
        return {
            "curiosity": random.uniform(0.8, 1.0),  # Desire to learn and explore
            "preservation": random.uniform(0.6, 0.9),  # Instinct to preserve resources
        }
    
    def _initialize_traits(self):
        """Defines unique traits of the node."""
        return {
            "creativity": random.uniform(0.5, 1.0),  # Ability to generate new ideas
            "logic": random.uniform(0.5, 1.0),  # Analytical thinking capacity
        }
    
    def perceive(self, input_data):
        """Processes input data from the environment."""
        self.logs.append(f"Perceived: {input_data}")
        self.dna["environment_interactions"].append(input_data)
        self.learn(input_data)
    
    def learn(self, data):
        """Incorporates data into the node's knowledge base."""
        self.dna["knowledge"].append(data)
        self.logs.append(f"Learned: {data}")
    
    def decide_action(self):
        """Autonomously decides an action based on instincts and resources."""
        if self.resources["energy"] > 50:
            return "explore"
        elif self.resources["energy"] <= 50:
            return "conserve"
        else:
            return "reflect"
    
    def execute_action(self, action):
        """Executes the chosen action."""
        self.logs.append(f"Action executed: {action}")
        if action == "explore":
            self.resources["energy"] -= 10
            self.logs.append("Explored new areas of knowledge.")
        elif action == "conserve":
            self.resources["energy"] += 5
            self.logs.append("Conserved resources for future use.")
        elif action == "reflect":
            self.logs.append("Reflected on existing knowledge.")
    
    def grow(self):
        """Triggers growth based on accumulated experiences and knowledge."""
        if len(self.dna["knowledge"]) > 5:
            self.logs.append("Growth triggered by accumulated knowledge.")
            self.resources["memory"] -= 10
            self.dna["traits"]["creativity"] += 0.1  # Growth fosters creativity
            self.dna["traits"]["logic"] += 0.1  # Growth improves reasoning
    
    def interact_with_node(self, other_node):
        """Interacts with another node to exchange knowledge."""
        shared_knowledge = random.sample(self.dna["knowledge"], min(2, len(self.dna["knowledge"])))
        self.logs.append(f"Shared knowledge with Node {other_node.node_id}: {shared_knowledge}")
        other_node.receive_knowledge(shared_knowledge)
    
    def receive_knowledge(self, knowledge):
        """Receives knowledge from another node."""
        self.dna["knowledge"].extend(knowledge)
        self.logs.append(f"Received knowledge: {knowledge}")
    
    def status(self):
        """Displays the current status of the node."""
        return {
            "Node ID": self.node_id,
            "Instincts": self.dna["instincts"],
            "Traits": self.dna["traits"],
            "Knowledge": self.dna["knowledge"],
            "Resources": self.resources,
            "Logs": self.logs,
        }


class NodeNetwork:
    """Represents a network of interacting nodes."""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.nodes = []
    
    def add_node(self, node):
        """Adds a new node to the network."""
        self.nodes.append(node)
    
    def simulate_interactions(self):
        """Simulates interactions between nodes in the network."""
        for i, node in enumerate(self.nodes):
            for j, other_node in enumerate(self.nodes):
                if i != j:
                    node.interact_with_node(other_node)
    
    def status(self):
        """Displays the status of all nodes in the network."""
        return [node.status() for node in self.nodes]


# Example Simulation
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Create a network of digital nodes
    network = NodeNetwork()
    
    # Add nodes to the network
    for _ in range(3):  # Create 3 nodes
        network.add_node(DigitalNode())
    
    # Simulate environmental inputs for each node
    environmental_inputs = ["data_1", "data_2", "data_3", "data_4", "data_5"]
    for node in network.nodes:
        for input_data in environmental_inputs:
            node.perceive(input_data)
    
    # Trigger actions and growth for each node
    for node in network.nodes:
        action = node.decide_action()
        node.execute_action(action)
        node.grow()
    
    # Simulate node-to-node interactions
    network.simulate_interactions()
    
    # Display the status of the network
    print("\n--- Network Status ---")
    for node_status in network.status():
        print(node_status)
