# dna_instruction.py

import random

def generate_dna(length=10):
    """Generate a random DNA sequence."""
    dna = [random.choice("ATCG") for _ in range(length)]
    return "".join(dna)

def mutate_dna(dna, mutation_rate=0.1):
    """Mutate a DNA sequence based on mutation rate."""
    dna_list = list(dna)
    for i in range(len(dna_list)):
        if random.random() < mutation_rate:
            dna_list[i] = random.choice("ATCG")
    return "".join(dna_list)

def combine_dna(parent1, parent2):
    """Combine DNA from two parents to create offspring DNA."""
    split_point = len(parent1) // 2
    return parent1[:split_point] + parent2[split_point:]

# dna-mutation.py

from __future__ import annotations
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Set
from dataclasses import dataclass
import logging
from datetime import datetime

from .structure import DNASequence
from .traits import TraitManager

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

@dataclass
class MutationProfile:
    """Defines mutation characteristics for DNA evolution."""
    base_rate: float
    trait_weights: Dict[str, float]
    stability_factor: float
    adaptive_pressure: float
    generation: int
    mutation_history: List[Dict] = field(default_factory=list)

class DNAMutator:
    """Handles DNA mutation and evolution mechanics."""
    
    def __init__(self, trait_manager: TraitManager):
        self.trait_manager = trait_manager
        self.mutation_profiles: Dict[str, MutationProfile] = {}
        
    async def mutate_sequence(
        self,
        sequence: DNASequence,
        pressure: float = 1.0
    ) -> DNASequence:
        profile = self._create_mutation_profile(sequence, pressure)
        mutated_traits = {}
        
        for trait, value in sequence.traits.items():
            if self._should_mutate(trait, profile):
                new_value = self._mutate_trait(
                    trait,
                    value,
                    profile
                )
                mutated_traits[trait] = new_value
            else:
                mutated_traits[trait] = value
                
        return DNASequence(
            sequence_id=f"{sequence.sequence_id}_m{profile.generation}",
            traits=mutated_traits,
            generation=profile.generation,
            parent_id=sequence.sequence_id
        )

    def _create_mutation_profile(
        self,
        sequence: DNASequence,
        pressure: float
    ) -> MutationProfile:
        """Creates mutation profile based on sequence state and pressure."""
        return MutationProfile(
            base_rate=0.01 * pressure,
            trait_weights=self._calculate_trait_weights(sequence),
            stability_factor=sequence.stability_factor,
            adaptive_pressure=pressure,
            generation=sequence.generation + 1
        )

    def _calculate_trait_weights(
        self,
        sequence: DNASequence
    ) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """Calculates weights for trait mutation probability."""
        weights = {}
        for trait, value in sequence.traits.items():
            trait_info = self.trait_manager.traits.get(trait)
            if trait_info:
                weights[trait] = (
                    trait_info.plasticity *
                    (1.0 - trait_info.adaptation_score)
                )
        return weights

    def _should_mutate(self, trait: str, profile: MutationProfile) -> bool:
        """Determines if a trait should mutate."""
        mutation_chance = (
            profile.base_rate *
            profile.trait_weights.get(trait, 1.0) *
            profile.adaptive_pressure
        )
        return np.random.random() < mutation_chance

    def _mutate_trait(
        self,
        trait: str,
        value: float,
        profile: MutationProfile
    ) -> float:
        """Applies mutation to a trait value."""
        trait_info = self.trait_manager.traits.get(trait)
        if not trait_info:
            return value

        # Calculate mutation magnitude
        magnitude = (
            np.random.normal(0, 0.1) *
            profile.adaptive_pressure *
            trait_info.plasticity
        )
        
        # Apply stability factor
        magnitude *= (1.0 - profile.stability_factor)
        
        # Calculate new value
        new_value = np.clip(
            value + magnitude,
            trait_info.min_value,
            trait_info.max_value
        )
        
        return new_value

    def record_mutation(
        self,
        sequence_id: str,
        profile: MutationProfile,
        mutations: Dict[str, float]
    ):
        """Records mutation event for analysis."""
        profile.mutation_history.append({
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "sequence_id": sequence_id,
            "mutations": mutations,
            "pressure": profile.adaptive_pressure
        })
        self.mutation_profiles[sequence_id] = profile
# dna-structure.py

from __future__ import annotations
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple, Union
from dataclasses import dataclass
import json
import logging
from pathlib import Path

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

@dataclass
class DNASequence:
    """Represents a fundamental DNA sequence with traits and mutation capabilities."""
    sequence_id: str
    traits: Dict[str, float]
    generation: int
    parent_id: Optional[str] = None
    mutation_rate: float = 0.01
    stability_factor: float = 0.95
    evolution_history: List[Dict] = None

    def __post_init__(self):
        self.evolution_history = self.evolution_history or []
        self._validate_traits()
        self._initialize_weights()

    def _validate_traits(self) -> None:
        """Validates that all required traits are present with appropriate values."""
        required_traits = {
            "learning_rate": (0.0, 1.0),
            "energy_efficiency": (0.5, 2.0),
            "processing_speed": (0.1, 5.0),
            "memory_capacity": (1.0, 10.0),
            "adaptation_rate": (0.0, 1.0),
            "stability": (0.0, 1.0),
            "specialization": (0.0, 1.0)
        }

        for trait, (min_val, max_val) in required_traits.items():
            if trait not in self.traits:
                self.traits[trait] = np.random.uniform(min_val, max_val)
            else:
                self.traits[trait] = np.clip(self.traits[trait], min_val, max_val)

    def _initialize_weights(self) -> None:
        """Initializes internal weights for trait interactions."""
        self._trait_weights = {
            ("learning_rate", "adaptation_rate"): 0.8,
            ("energy_efficiency", "processing_speed"): 0.6,
            ("memory_capacity", "specialization"): 0.7,
            ("stability", "adaptation_rate"): -0.4
        }

    def mutate(self, environmental_pressure: float = 1.0) -> DNASequence:
        """
        Creates a mutated copy of the DNA sequence.
        
        Args:
            environmental_pressure: Factor influencing mutation intensity (0.0-2.0)
            
        Returns:
            New DNASequence with mutated traits
        """
        mutated_traits = {}
        mutation_record = {
            "generation": self.generation + 1,
            "parent_id": self.sequence_id,
            "mutations": {}
        }

        # Calculate adaptive mutation rate based on environmental pressure
        adaptive_rate = self.mutation_rate * environmental_pressure
        
        for trait, value in self.traits.items():
            # Calculate trait-specific mutation based on weights
            trait_pressure = self._calculate_trait_pressure(trait)
            trait_mutation = np.random.normal(0, adaptive_rate * trait_pressure)
            
            # Apply stability factor
            trait_mutation *= (1 - self.stability_factor)
            
            # Record significant mutations
            if abs(trait_mutation) > 0.01:
                mutation_record["mutations"][trait] = {
                    "previous": value,
                    "change": trait_mutation
                }
            
            mutated_traits[trait] = np.clip(value + trait_mutation, 0.0, 1.0)

        # Create new DNA sequence with mutated traits
        new_history = self.evolution_history + [mutation_record]
        return DNASequence(
            sequence_id=f"{self.sequence_id}_m{self.generation + 1}",
            traits=mutated_traits,
            generation=self.generation + 1,
            parent_id=self.sequence_id,
            mutation_rate=self.mutation_rate,
            stability_factor=self.stability_factor,
            evolution_history=new_history
        )

    def _calculate_trait_pressure(self, trait: str) -> float:
        """Calculates the mutation pressure on a specific trait based on weights."""
        pressure = 1.0
        for (trait1, trait2), weight in self._trait_weights.items():
            if trait in (trait1, trait2):
                other_trait = trait2 if trait == trait1 else trait1
                pressure += weight * self.traits[other_trait]
        return np.clip(pressure, 0.5, 2.0)

    def combine(self, other: DNASequence) -> DNASequence:
        """
        Combines this DNA sequence with another to produce offspring.
        
        Args:
            other: Another DNASequence to combine with
            
        Returns:
            New DNASequence with combined traits
        """
        combined_traits = {}
        combination_record = {
            "type": "combination",
            "parents": [self.sequence_id, other.sequence_id],
            "traits": {}
        }

        for trait in self.traits:
            # Use weighted average with random factor
            weight = np.random.beta(2, 2)  # Beta distribution for natural variation
            combined_value = (
                weight * self.traits[trait] +
                (1 - weight) * other.traits[trait]
            )
            
            # Add some random variation
            variation = np.random.normal(0, 0.05)
            combined_value = np.clip(combined_value + variation, 0.0, 1.0)
            
            combined_traits[trait] = combined_value
            combination_record["traits"][trait] = {
                "parent1": self.traits[trait],
                "parent2": other.traits[trait],
                "combined": combined_value
            }

        # Create new DNA sequence with combined traits
        new_history = self.evolution_history + [combination_record]
        return DNASequence(
            sequence_id=f"combined_{self.sequence_id}_{other.sequence_id}",
            traits=combined_traits,
            generation=max(self.generation, other.generation) + 1,
            parent_id=f"{self.sequence_id}+{other.sequence_id}",
            mutation_rate=(self.mutation_rate + other.mutation_rate) / 2,
            stability_factor=(self.stability_factor + other.stability_factor) / 2,
            evolution_history=new_history
        )

    def save(self, path: Union[str, Path]) -> None:
        """Saves the DNA sequence to a file."""
        data = {
            "sequence_id": self.sequence_id,
            "traits": self.traits,
            "generation": self.generation,
            "parent_id": self.parent_id,
            "mutation_rate": self.mutation_rate,
            "stability_factor": self.stability_factor,
            "evolution_history": self.evolution_history
        }
        
        with open(path, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(data, f, indent=4)
        logger.info(f"DNA sequence saved to {path}")

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, path: Union[str, Path]) -> DNASequence:
        """Loads a DNA sequence from a file."""
        with open(path, 'r') as f:
            data = json.load(f)
        return cls(**data)

    def evaluate_fitness(self, environment_state: Dict[str, float]) -> float:
        """
        Evaluates the fitness of this DNA sequence in a given environment.
        
        Args:
            environment_state: Dictionary containing environmental parameters
            
        Returns:
            Fitness score (0.0-1.0)
        """
        fitness_scores = []
        
        # Evaluate each trait's contribution to fitness
        for trait, value in self.traits.items():
            if trait in environment_state:
                # Calculate how well the trait matches environmental demands
                difference = abs(value - environment_state[trait])
                trait_fitness = 1.0 - (difference / 1.0)  # Normalize to 0-1
                fitness_scores.append(trait_fitness)
        
        # Calculate overall fitness
        if fitness_scores:
            return np.mean(fitness_scores)
        return 0.0

    def analyze_evolution(self) -> Dict:
        """Analyzes the evolutionary history of the DNA sequence."""
        analysis = {
            "generations": self.generation,
            "mutation_count": 0,
            "trait_trends": {trait: [] for trait in self.traits},
            "significant_changes": []
        }

        # Analyze evolution history
        for record in self.evolution_history:
            if "mutations" in record:
                analysis["mutation_count"] += len(record["mutations"])
                for trait, change in record["mutations"].items():
                    if abs(change["change"]) > 0.1:  # Significant change threshold
                        analysis["significant_changes"].append({
                            "generation": record["generation"],
                            "trait": trait,
                            "change": change["change"]
                        })

        return analysis

# dna_structure.py

import random
from typing import Dict

class DNA:
    """
    Represents the DNA-like structure for nodes, defining traits and enabling adaptive replication.
    This implementation is optimized for seamless integration with Kaleidoscope AI and groundbreaking efficiency.
    """

    def __init__(self, traits: Dict[str, float] = None):
        """
        Initializes DNA with a set of traits optimized for AI adaptability.
        :param traits: A dictionary of trait names and their values.
        """
        self.traits = traits or {
            "learning_rate": 0.1,
            "energy_efficiency": 1.0,
            "replication_threshold": 0.8,
            "adaptation_rate": 0.02,
        }

    def mutate(self, context: Dict[str, float] = None) -> 'DNA':
        """
        Generates a mutated version of the DNA, dynamically influenced by context.
        :param context: Optional context to guide mutation (e.g., system energy, task complexity).
        :return: A new DNA instance with enhanced adaptive capabilities.
        """
        context_factor = context.get("mutation_rate", 1.0) if context else 1.0
        mutated_traits = {
            trait: max(0.01, value + random.uniform(-0.02, 0.02) * context_factor)
            for trait, value in self.traits.items()
        }
        return DNA(mutated_traits)

    def combine(self, other: 'DNA') -> 'DNA':
        """
        Combines traits from two DNA structures to produce an advanced version.
        :param other: Another DNA instance.
        :return: A new DNA instance with enhanced combined traits.
        """
        combined_traits = {
            trait: (self.traits.get(trait, 0.1) + other.traits.get(trait, 0.1)) / 2
            for trait in set(self.traits.keys()).union(other.traits.keys())
        }
        return DNA(combined_traits)

    def express_traits(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        Returns the current traits of the DNA, including derived attributes for advanced functionalities.
        :return: A dictionary of trait names and values.
        """
        derived_traits = {
            "collaboration_efficiency": self.traits["energy_efficiency"] * 0.9,
            "learning_stability": 1.0 - self.traits["adaptation_rate"],
            "replication_efficiency": self.traits["replication_threshold"] * self.traits["energy_efficiency"],
        }
        return {**self.traits, **derived_traits}

    def optimize_traits(self, global_state: Dict[str, float]):
        """
        Dynamically adjusts traits based on system-wide metrics, optimizing AI behavior.
        :param global_state: Dictionary representing global system metrics.
        """
        if global_state.get("energy_level", 1.0) < 0.5:
            self.traits["energy_efficiency"] *= 1.1  # Prioritize efficiency in low-energy states.
        if global_state.get("task_density", 1.0) > 0.8:
            self.traits["learning_rate"] *= 1.05  # Increase learning rate in high-task scenarios.

    def adjust_trait(self, trait: str, adjustment: float):
        """
        Adjusts a specific trait by a given amount with safeguards.
        :param trait: The name of the trait to adjust.
        :param adjustment: The adjustment value (positive or negative).
        """
        if trait in self.traits:
            self.traits[trait] = max(0.01, self.traits[trait] + adjustment)

    def __repr__(self):
        return f"DNA(traits={self.traits})"

# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Create initial DNA
    parent_dna = DNA()
    print("Parent DNA:", parent_dna)

    # Mutate DNA with context
    context = {"mutation_rate": 1.5, "energy_level": 0.4}
    mutated_dna = parent_dna.mutate(context)
    print("Mutated DNA:", mutated_dna)

    # Combine DNA
    another_dna = DNA({"learning_rate": 0.15, "energy_efficiency": 0.95})
    combined_dna = parent_dna.combine(another_dna)
    print("Combined DNA:", combined_dna)

    # Optimize traits based on global state
    global_state = {"energy_level": 0.3, "task_density": 0.9}
    combined_dna.optimize_traits(global_state)
    print("Optimized Traits:", combined_dna.express_traits())

    # Express traits
    print("Expressed Traits:", combined_dna.express_traits())


# dna-traits.py

from __future__ import annotations
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Set, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import logging
from enum import Enum, auto

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class TraitCategory(Enum):
    """Categories for different types of traits."""
    COGNITIVE = auto()
    PHYSICAL = auto()
    ADAPTIVE = auto()
    SOCIAL = auto()
    SPECIALIZED = auto()

@dataclass
class Trait:
    """Represents a single trait with its properties and behaviors."""
    name: str
    value: float
    category: TraitCategory
    min_value: float = 0.0
    max_value: float = 1.0
    mutation_probability: float = 0.1
    dependencies: Set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    antagonists: Set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    plasticity: float = 0.5  # How easily the trait can change

    def __post_init__(self):
        self._validate()
        self._initialize_metadata()

    def _validate(self):
        """Validates trait properties."""
        if not 0 <= self.value <= 1:
            raise ValueError(f"Trait value must be between 0 and 1, got {self.value}")
        if not 0 <= self.plasticity <= 1:
            raise ValueError(f"Plasticity must be between 0 and 1, got {self.plasticity}")

    def _initialize_metadata(self):
        """Initializes additional metadata for trait tracking."""
        self.history = []
        self.adaptation_score = 1.0
        self.last_update = 0
        self.interaction_strength = {}

class TraitManager:
    """Manages the collection and interaction of traits."""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.traits: Dict[str, Trait] = {}
        self.interaction_matrix: Dict[Tuple[str, str], float] = {}
        self._initialize_base_traits()

    def _initialize_base_traits(self):
        """Initializes the basic set of traits required for functioning."""
        base_traits = {
            "learning_capacity": Trait(
                name="learning_capacity",
                value=0.5,
                category=TraitCategory.COGNITIVE,
                dependencies={"memory_efficiency"},
                plasticity=0.7
            ),
            "memory_efficiency": Trait(
                name="memory_efficiency",
                value=0.5,
                category=TraitCategory.COGNITIVE,
                dependencies={"energy_efficiency"},
                plasticity=0.6
            ),
            "energy_efficiency": Trait(
                name="energy_efficiency",
                value=0.5,
                category=TraitCategory.PHYSICAL,
                antagonists={"processing_speed"},
                plasticity=0.4
            ),
            "processing_speed": Trait(
                name="processing_speed",
                value=0.5,
                category=TraitCategory.PHYSICAL,
                dependencies={"energy_efficiency"},
                plasticity=0.5
            ),
            "adaptation_rate": Trait(
                name="adaptation_rate",
                value=0.5,
                category=TraitCategory.ADAPTIVE,
                dependencies={"learning_capacity"},
                plasticity=0.8
            )
        }
        
        for name, trait in base_traits.items():
            self.add_trait(trait)

    def add_trait(self, trait: Trait):
        """Adds a new trait to the manager."""
        if trait.name in self.traits:
            logger.warning(f"Overwriting existing trait: {trait.name}")
        self.traits[trait.name] = trait
        self._update_interaction_matrix(trait)

    def _update_interaction_matrix(self, trait: Trait):
        """Updates the interaction matrix with a new trait."""
        for dep in trait.dependencies:
            self.interaction_matrix[(trait.name, dep)] = 0.5
            self.interaction_matrix[(dep, trait.name)] = 0.3

        for ant in trait.antagonists:
            self.interaction_matrix[(trait.name, ant)] = -0.4
            self.interaction_matrix[(ant, trait.name)] = -0.4

    def evolve_traits(self, environmental_pressure: float = 1.0) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """Evolves all traits based on environmental pressure and interactions."""
        evolved_values = {}
        
        for name, trait in self.traits.items():
            # Calculate interaction effects
            interaction_effect = self._calculate_interaction_effect(name)
            
            # Calculate environmental adaptation
            adaptation = self._calculate_adaptation(trait, environmental_pressure)
            
            # Apply evolution with constraints
            evolution_rate = trait.plasticity * environmental_pressure
            random_factor = np.random.normal(0, 0.1 * trait.mutation_probability)
            
            new_value = trait.value + (
                evolution_rate * interaction_effect +
                adaptation +
                random_factor
            )
            
            # Constrain within bounds
            new_value = np.clip(new_value, trait.min_value, trait.max_value)
            evolved_values[name] = new_value
            
            # Update trait history
            trait.history.append({
                "old_value": trait.value,
                "new_value": new_value,
                "pressure": environmental_pressure,
                "interaction_effect": interaction_effect,
                "adaptation": adaptation
            })
            
            # Update trait value
            trait.value = new_value

        return evolved_values

    def _calculate_interaction_effect(self, trait_name: str) -> float:
        """Calculates the effect of trait interactions."""
        effect = 0.0
        for (t1, t2), strength in self.interaction_matrix.items():
            if t1 == trait_name:
                effect += strength * self.traits[t2].value
        return effect / len(self.interaction_matrix) if self.interaction_matrix else 0.0
# dynamic_reorganization.py

# File: dynamic_reorganization.py
# Description: Dynamically reorganizes clusters and communities based on task demands.

class DynamicReorganizer:
    """Reorganizes clusters and communities dynamically based on task demands."""

    def reorganize(self, cluster_manager, task):
        """Reorganizes clusters to better handle the task."""
        relevant_clusters = [cluster for cluster in cluster_manager.clusters
                             if any(task in node.dna["knowledge"] for node in cluster)]

        if not relevant_clusters:
            cluster_manager.logs.append(f"No cluster can handle task '{task}'. Reorganization triggered.")
            cluster_manager.form_clusters(cluster_manager.network)
            return "Clusters reorganized to handle the task."

        cluster_manager.logs.append(f"Reorganization not required for task '{task}'.")
        return "Reorganization not required."

# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Reuse the cluster manager and network from previous examples
    dynamic_reorganizer = DynamicReorganizer()
    result = dynamic_reorganizer.reorganize(cluster_manager, "Knowledge_3")

    print("\n--- Reorganization Result ---")
    print(result)
    print("\n--- Cluster Manager Status ---")
    print(cluster_manager.status())


# dynamic_resource_manager.py

class DynamicResourceManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.resource_threshold = 0.5

    def allocate_resources(self, nodes):
        """Balance resources across all nodes."""
        total_energy = sum(node.energy for node in nodes)
        average_energy = total_energy / len(nodes)

        for node in nodes:
            if node.energy < average_energy * self.resource_threshold:
                node.energy += 0.1
            else:
                node.energy -= 0.05

    def monitor_resources(self, nodes):
        return {
            "Average Energy": sum(node.energy for node in nodes) / len(nodes),
            "Node Count": len(nodes)
        }

# File: dynamic_thresholds.py

import logging
from typing import Dict

# Configure logging
logging.basicConfig(
    filename="dynamic_thresholds.log",
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s"
)

class DynamicThresholdManager:
    """
    Manages dynamic thresholds for energy optimization based on system metrics.
    """
    def __init__(self, nodes: Dict[str, Dict[str, float]]):
        """
        Initializes the threshold manager.

        Parameters:
            nodes (Dict[str, Dict[str, float]]): A dictionary of nodes and their metrics.
        """
        self.nodes = nodes
        self.high_energy_threshold = 70.0
        self.low_energy_threshold = 30.0
        self.utilization_threshold = 50.0

    def adjust_thresholds(self, task_queue_length: int, avg_energy: float):
        """
        Adjusts thresholds dynamically based on system metrics.

        Parameters:
            task_queue_length (int): The length of the current task queue.
            avg_energy (float): The average energy level of all nodes.
        """
        if task_queue_length > 10:
            self.low_energy_threshold = max(20.0, self.low_energy_threshold - 5.0)
            self.high_energy_threshold = min(80.0, self.high_energy_threshold + 5.0)
        elif avg_energy > 60:
            self.low_energy_threshold = min(40.0, self.low_energy_threshold + 5.0)
            self.high_energy_threshold = max(60.0, self.high_energy_threshold - 5.0)

        logging.info(
            f"Adjusted thresholds: Low={self.low_energy_threshold}, High={self.high_energy_threshold}"
        )

    def get_thresholds(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        Returns the current thresholds.

        Returns:
            Dict[str, float]: The current high and low energy thresholds.
        """
        return {
            "low_energy_threshold": self.low_energy_threshold,
            "high_energy_threshold": self.high_energy_threshold
        }

# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Example nodes with metrics
    nodes = {
        "Node-1": {"energy": 90.0},
        "Node-2": {"energy": 20.0},
        "Node-3": {"energy": 50.0},
        "Node-4": {"energy": 70.0}
    }

    # Initialize the threshold manager
    manager = DynamicThresholdManager(nodes)

    # Simulate adjustments
    for i in range(5):
        avg_energy = sum(node["energy"] for node in nodes.values()) / len(nodes)
        manager.adjust_thresholds(task_queue_length=i * 5, avg_energy=avg_energy)

        # Print the updated thresholds
        print(manager.get_thresholds())

# emergent_behavior.c.py

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <math.h>

// Node structure
typedef struct {
    char id[36];
    double energy;
    int connection_count;
    int *connections;  // Array of indices for connected nodes
} Node;

// Simulate flocking behavior
void simulate_flocking(Node *nodes, int node_count, double alignment_factor) {
    for (int i = 0; i < node_count; i++) {
        double neighbor_average = 0.0;
        for (int j = 0; j < nodes[i].connection_count; j++) {
            neighbor_average += nodes[nodes[i].connections[j]].energy;
        }
        neighbor_average /= nodes[i].connection_count;
        nodes[i].energy += alignment_factor * (neighbor_average - nodes[i].energy);
    }
}

// Test function
int main() {
    Node nodes[3] = {
        {"node_1", 30.0, 2, (int[]){1, 2}},
        {"node_2", 20.0, 1, (int[]){0}},
        {"node_3", 50.0, 1, (int[]){0}},
    };

    simulate_flocking(nodes, 3, 0.2);

    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        printf("Node %d energy: %.2f\n", i + 1, nodes[i].energy);
    }

    return 0;
}

# emergent_behavior.py

# emergent_behavior.py

from typing import Dict, List
from core.organic_node import OrganicNode

class EmergentBehaviorSimulator:
    """Simulates emergent behaviors across decentralized nodes."""

    def __init__(self, nodes: Dict[str, OrganicNode]):
        self.nodes = nodes

    def simulate_flocking(self):
        """Simulates flocking behavior where nodes align their states."""
        for node in self.nodes.values():
            neighbors = self._find_neighbors(node)
            node_state = node.energy  # Example state to align (can include other metrics)
            for neighbor in neighbors:
                alignment_factor = 0.2  # Degree of alignment
                neighbor.energy += alignment_factor * (node_state - neighbor.energy)

    def _find_neighbors(self, node: OrganicNode) -> List[OrganicNode]:
        """Finds nearby nodes based on connection strength."""
        return [
            self.nodes[neighbor_id]
            for neighbor_id in node.connections
            if neighbor_id in self.nodes
        ]

# emergent_intelligence.py

# File: emergent_intelligence.py
# Description: Monitors node collaboration for emergent intelligence patterns.

class EmergentIntelligenceLayer:
    """Monitors and captures emergent intelligence within a network of nodes."""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.emergent_patterns = []
        self.logs = []

    def detect_emergence(self, network):
        """Analyzes the network for emergent patterns."""
        collective_knowledge = set()
        for node in network.nodes:
            collective_knowledge.update(node.dna["knowledge"])

        if len(collective_knowledge) > 20:  # Arbitrary threshold for emergence
            pattern = f"Emergent_Pattern_{len(self.emergent_patterns) + 1}"
            self.emergent_patterns.append(pattern)
            self.logs.append(f"Detected emergent intelligence: {pattern}")
            return pattern
        else:
            self.logs.append("No emergent pattern detected.")
            return None

    def synthesize_insight(self, pattern):
        """Synthesizes insights from detected emergent patterns."""
        if not pattern:
            return "No pattern available for synthesis."
        insight = f"Insight derived from {pattern}: Collaboration accelerates innovation."
        self.logs.append(f"Synthesized insight: {insight}")
        return insight

    def status(self):
        """Returns the status of emergent intelligence monitoring."""
        return {
            "Emergent Patterns": self.emergent_patterns,
            "Logs": self.logs[-10:],  # Last 10 log entries
        }


# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Initialize the emergent intelligence layer and a network
    emergent_layer = EmergentIntelligenceLayer()
    network = EmergentBehaviorNetwork()

    # Add nodes and simulate interactions
    for _ in range(5):
        network.add_node(MemoryEnhancedNode())
    network.simulate_interactions()

    # Detect and synthesize emergent intelligence
    pattern = emergent_layer.detect_emergence(network)
    insight = emergent_layer.synthesize_insight(pattern)

    # Display emergent intelligence status
    print("\n--- Emergent Intelligence Layer Status ---")
    print(emergent_layer.status())
    print("\n--- Synthesized Insight ---")
    print(insight)


# emotional _procc_sys.py

class EmotionalProcessor:
    """Processes emotional context and influence"""
    def __init__(self):
        self.emotional_state = EmotionalState()
        self.state_history = []
        self.response_patterns = defaultdict(list)
        
    def process_emotional_context(self, input_data: Dict) -> EmotionalResponse:
        """Process emotional aspects of input"""
        # Extract emotional indicators
        indicators = self._extract_emotional_indicators(input_data)
        
        # Update emotional state
        self._update_emotional_state(indicators)
        
        # Generate emotional response
        response = self._generate_response(indicators)
        
        # Record state change
        self._record_state_change(indicators, response)
        
        return response

    def _extract_emotional_indicators(self, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Extract emotional indicators from input data"""
        indicators = {
            'intensity': self._calculate_intensity(data),
            'valence': self._calculate_valence(data),
            'arousal': self._calculate_arousal(data),
            'context': self._extract_context(data)
        }
        return indicators

    def _update_emotional_state(self, indicators: Dict):
        """Update system's emotional state"""
        self.emotional_state.update(
            intensity=indicators['intensity'],
            valence=indicators['valence'],
            arousal=indicators['arousal'],
            context=indicators['context']
        )

# enhanced-adaptive-node (1).py

```python
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Dict, List, Set, Optional
import numpy as np
from collections import deque
import time

@dataclass
class DynamicWeights:
    """Adaptive weights that adjust based on conditions"""
    growth: float = 0.3
    efficiency: float = 0.3
    knowledge: float = 0.4
    adaptation_rate: float = 0.1
    history: Dict[str, List[float]] = field(default_factory=lambda: {
        'growth': [],
        'efficiency': [],
        'knowledge': []
    })

    def adapt_to_conditions(self, environment_state: Dict, node_state: Dict):
        """Dynamically adjust weights based on conditions"""
        # Calculate pressure factors
        energy_pressure = 1 - (node_state['energy'] / node_state['max_energy'])
        growth_pressure = 1 - len(node_state['connections']) / 10
        knowledge_pressure = 1 - len(node_state['knowledge_base']) / 100

        # Adjust weights based on pressures
        self.efficiency = min(0.6, self.efficiency + energy_pressure * self.adaptation_rate)
        self.growth = min(0.6, self.growth + growth_pressure * self.adaptation_rate)
        self.knowledge = min(0.6, self.knowledge + knowledge_pressure * self.adaptation_rate)

        # Normalize weights
        total = self.growth + self.efficiency + self.knowledge
        self.growth /= total
        self.efficiency /= total
        self.knowledge /= total

        # Record history
        self._record_weights()

    def _record_weights(self):
        """Record weight history for analysis"""
        self.history['growth'].append(self.growth)
        self.history['efficiency'].append(self.efficiency)
        self.history['knowledge'].append(self.knowledge)

@dataclass
class AdaptiveThresholds:
    """Dynamic thresholds that adjust based on performance"""
    base_thresholds: Dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=lambda: {
        'survival': 0.3,
        'growth': 0.5,
        'replication': 0.7,
        'teaching': 0.8
    })
    adaptation_rate: float = 0.05
    history: Dict[str, List[float]] = field(default_factory=lambda: {
        'survival': [],
        'growth': [],
        'replication': [],
        'teaching': []
    })

    def adapt_thresholds(self, performance_history: List[float]):
        """Adjust thresholds based on performance"""
        if not performance_history:
            return

        recent_performance = np.mean(performance_history[-10:])
        for mode, threshold in self.base_thresholds.items():
            if recent_performance > threshold + 0.1:
                # Increase threshold if consistently exceeding it
                self.base_thresholds[mode] += self.adaptation_rate
            elif recent_performance < threshold - 0.1:
                # Decrease threshold if consistently falling short
                self.base_thresholds[mode] = max(
                    0.1,
                    self.base_thresholds[mode] - self.adaptation_rate
                )
            
            self.history[mode].append(self.base_thresholds[mode])

class ContextualPattern:
    """Pattern with contextual awareness"""
    def __init__(self, content: Dict, context: Dict):
        self.content = content
        self.context = context
        self.timestamp = time.time()
        self.confidence = 0.5
        self.related_patterns = set()
        self.impact_score = 0.0
        
    def update_confidence(self, similar_patterns: List['ContextualPattern']):
        """Update confidence based on similar patterns"""
        if not similar_patterns:
            return
            
        confidence_sum = sum(p.confidence for p in similar_patterns)
        context_similarity = np.mean([
            self._calculate_context_similarity(p) for p in similar_patterns
        ])
        
        self.confidence = (
            0.7 * self.confidence +
            0.3 * (confidence_sum / len(similar_patterns)) * context_similarity
        )

    def _calculate_context_similarity(self, other: 'ContextualPattern') -> float:
        """Calculate similarity between contexts"""
        if not self.context or not other.context:
            return 0.0
            
        common_keys = set(self.context.keys()) & set(other.context.keys())
        if not common_keys:
            return 0.0
            
        similarities = []
        for key in common_keys:
            if isinstance(self.context[key], (int, float)) and \
               isinstance(other.context[key], (int, float)):
                # Numerical comparison
                max_val = max(abs(self.context[key]), abs(other.context[key]))
                if max_val == 0:
                    similarities.append(1.0)
                else:
                    similarities.append(
                        1 - abs(self.context[key] - other.context[key]) / max_val
                    )
            else:
                # String comparison
                similarities.append(
                    1.0 if self.context[key] == other.context[key] else 0.0
                )
                
        return np.mean(similarities)

class SharedKnowledgePool:
    """Distributed knowledge pool for collaborative learning"""
    def __init__(self):
        self.patterns: Dict[str, ContextualPattern] = {}
        self.node_contributions: Dict[str, Set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
        self.pattern_relationships = nx.Graph()
        self.access_history = defaultdict(list)
        
    def contribute_pattern(self, pattern: ContextualPattern, node_id: str):
        """Add pattern to shared pool"""
        pattern_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.patterns[pattern_id] = pattern
        self.node_contributions[node_id].add(pattern_id)
        
        # Update pattern relationships
        self._update_pattern_relationships(pattern_id)
        
    def _update_pattern_relationships(self, new_pattern_id: str):
        """Update relationships between patterns"""
        new_pattern = self.patterns[new_pattern_id]
        self.pattern_relationships.add_node(new_pattern_id)
        
        for pid, pattern in self.patterns.items():
            if pid != new_pattern_id:
                similarity = new_pattern._calculate_context_similarity(pattern)
                if similarity > 0.5:
                    self.pattern_relationships.add_edge(
                        new_pattern_id,
                        pid,
                        weight=similarity
                    )

    def get_relevant_patterns(self, context: Dict, limit: int = 10) -> List[ContextualPattern]:
        """Get patterns relevant to given context"""
        relevance_scores = {}
        
        for pid, pattern in self.patterns.items():
            similarity = pattern._calculate_context_similarity(
                ContextualPattern({}, context)
            )
            relevance_scores[pid] = similarity
            
        # Get most relevant patterns
        relevant_ids = sorted(
            relevance_scores.keys(),
            key=lambda x: relevance_scores[x],
            reverse=True
        )[:limit]
        
        return [self.patterns[pid] for pid in relevant_ids]

class EnhancedAdaptiveNode:
    """Node with enhanced adaptive capabilities"""
    def __init__(self, node_id: str, shared_pool: SharedKnowledgePool):
        self.id = node_id
        self.shared_pool = shared_pool
        self.weights = DynamicWeights()
        self.thresholds = AdaptiveThresholds()
        self.energy = 10.0
        self.max_energy = 100.0
        self.connections = set()
        self.knowledge_base = {}
        
        # Performance monitoring
        self.performance_history = deque(maxlen=100)
        self.action_outcomes = deque(maxlen=100)
        self.feedback_metrics = defaultdict(list)
        
    def process_with_context(self, data: Dict, context: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Process input with contextual awareness"""
        # Update weights based on current conditions
        self.weights.adapt_to_conditions(
            environment_state=context,
            node_state=self.get_state()
        )
        
        # Create contextual pattern
        pattern = ContextualPattern(data, context)
        
        # Get relevant patterns from shared pool
        relevant_patterns = self.shared_pool.get_relevant_patterns(context)
        
        # Update pattern confidence
        pattern.update_confidence(relevant_patterns)
        
        # Process based on current mode
        score = self.calculate_adaptive_score()
        result = self._process_in_mode(pattern, score)
        
        # Update performance history
        self._update_performance(result)
        
        # Contribute to shared pool if valuable
        if pattern.confidence > 0.7:
            self.shared_pool.contribute_pattern(pattern, self.id)
            
        return result

    def calculate_adaptive_score(self) -> float:
        """Calculate score with dynamic weights"""
        state = self.get_state()
        
        growth_score = len(self.connections) / 10
        efficiency_score = 1 - (self.energy / self.max_energy)
        knowledge_score = len(self.knowledge_base) / 100
        
        score = (
            self.weights.growth * growth_score +
            self.weights.efficiency * efficiency_score +
            self.weights.knowledge * knowledge_score
        )
        
        self.performance_history.append(score)
        return score

    def _process_in_mode(self, pattern: ContextualPattern, score: float) -> Dict:
        """Process pattern based on current mode"""
        # Get adaptive thresholds
        self.thresholds.adapt_thresholds(list(self.performance_history))
        
        # Choose processing mode
        if score < self.thresholds.base_thresholds['survival']:
            return self._survival_mode(pattern)
        elif score < self.thresholds.base_thresholds['growth']:
            return self._learning_mode(pattern)
        elif score < self.thresholds.base_thresholds['teaching']:
            return self._growth_mode(pattern)
        else:
            return self._teaching_mode(pattern)

    def _update_performance(self, result: Dict):
        """Update performance metrics and feedback"""
        # Record action outcome
        self.action_outcomes.append(result)
        
        # Update feedback metrics
        self.feedback_metrics[result['mode']].append({
            'score': self.performance_history[-1],
            'energy_used': result.get('energy_used', 0),
            'effectiveness': result.get('effectiveness', 0),
            'timestamp': time.time()
        })
        
        # Analyze recent performance
        self._analyze_performance()

    def _analyze_performance(self):
        """Analyze performance and adjust behavior"""
        if len(self.action_outcomes) < 10:
            return
            
        recent_outcomes = list(self.action_outcomes)[-10:]
        
        # Calculate success rates per mode
        mode_success = defaultdict(list)
        for outcome in recent_outcomes:
            mode = outcome['mode']
            success = outcome.get('effectiveness', 0) > 0.5
            mode_success[mode].append(success)
            
        # Adjust thresholds based on success rates
        for mode, successes in mode_success.items():
            success_rate = sum(successes) / len(successes)
            if success_rate < 0.3:
                # Lower threshold if struggling
                self.thresholds.base_thresholds[mode] = max(
                    0.1,
                    self.thresholds.base_thresholds[mode] - 0.05
                )
            elif success_rate > 0.8:
                # Raise threshold if too easy
                self.thresholds.base_thresholds[mode] += 0.05

    def get_state(self) -> Dict:
        """Get current node state"""
        return {
            'energy': self.energy,
            'max_energy': self.max_energy,
            'connections': self.connections,
            'knowledge_base': self.knowledge_base
        }
```

Key enhancements:

1. Dynamic Weight Adjustment:
- Weights adapt based on environmental pressure
- Historical tracking of weight changes
- Normalized adjustments

2. Contextual Pattern Processing:
- Patterns include environmental context
- Confidence updates based on similar patterns
- Context similarity calculations

3. Shared Knowledge Pool:
- Distributed pattern storage
- Pattern relationship tracking
- Relevance-based pattern retrieval

4. Adaptive Thresholds:
- Performance-based threshold adjustment
- Mode-specific adaptation
- Historical threshold tracking

5. Performance Monitoring:
- Detailed outcome tracking
- Mode-specific success rates
- Automatic behavior adjustment

1. Enhance the context similarity calculations?
2. Add more sophisticated performance metrics?
3. Implement advanced pattern relationship tracking?
4. Expand the shared knowledge pool capabilities?
