# EnhancedNodeManager.py

from node_communication import NodeCommunication, Message

class EnhancedNodeManager:
    """
    Manages nodes with enhanced communication and task processing capabilities.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.nodes = {}
        self.comms = NodeCommunication()

    def add_node(self, node_id: str, attributes: dict):
        """
        Adds a new node to the system and registers it for communication.
        :param node_id: Unique identifier for the node.
        :param attributes: Dictionary of node-specific attributes.
        """
        node = EnhancedNode(node_id, attributes, self.comms)
        self.nodes[node_id] = node
        self.comms.register_node(node_id, node.message_handler)
        print(f"Node '{node_id}' added and registered successfully.")

    def remove_node(self, node_id: str):
        """
        Removes a node from the system and unregisters it from communication.
        :param node_id: Unique identifier for the node to remove.
        """
        if node_id in self.nodes:
            self.comms.unregister_node(node_id)
            del self.nodes[node_id]
            print(f"Node '{node_id}' removed successfully.")
        else:
            print(f"Node '{node_id}' does not exist.")

    def send_message(self, sender_id: str, receiver_id: str, content: dict, priority: int = 1):
        """
        Sends a message from one node to another.
        :param sender_id: ID of the sender node.
        :param receiver_id: ID of the receiver node.
        :param content: Message content.
        :param priority: Priority of the message.
        """
        if sender_id not in self.nodes or receiver_id not in self.nodes:
            print(f"Error: Invalid sender or receiver.")
            return
        self.comms.send_message(sender_id, receiver_id, content, priority)

    def process_messages(self):
        """
        Processes all queued messages in the communication system.
        """
        self.comms.process_messages()

class EnhancedNode:
    """
    Represents a node with enhanced task handling and message processing capabilities.
    """

    def __init__(self, node_id: str, attributes: dict, comms: NodeCommunication):
        self.node_id = node_id
        self.attributes = attributes
        self.energy = attributes.get("energy", 100.0)
        self.tasks = []
        self.comms = comms

    def message_handler(self, message: Message):
        """
        Handles incoming messages.
        :param message: A Message object containing sender, receiver, and content.
        """
        print(f"Node '{self.node_id}' received message from '{message.sender_id}': {message.content}")
        if "task" in message.content:
            self.tasks.append(message.content["task"])
            print(f"Node '{self.node_id}' added task: {message.content['task']}")

    def process_tasks(self):
        """
        Processes tasks assigned to the node.
        """
        while self.tasks:
            task = self.tasks.pop(0)
            print(f"Node '{self.node_id}' is processing task: {task}")
            # Simulate task processing by reducing energy
            self.energy -= 10
            if self.energy <= 0:
                print(f"Node '{self.node_id}' is out of energy and needs recharging.")
                self.request_energy()

    def request_energy(self):
        """
        Requests energy from other nodes via the communication system.
        """
        self.comms.broadcast_message(self.node_id, {"request": "energy"}, priority=1)

    def recharge(self, amount: float):
        """
        Recharges the node's energy.
        :param amount: Amount of energy to recharge.
        """
        self.energy += amount
        print(f"Node '{self.node_id}' recharged by {amount}. Current energy: {self.energy}")

# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Initialize EnhancedNodeManager
    manager = EnhancedNodeManager()

    # Add nodes
    manager.add_node("Node1", {"energy": 50, "role": "worker"})
    manager.add_node("Node2", {"energy": 100, "role": "worker"})

    # Send a task message
    manager.send_message("Node1", "Node2", {"task": "Analyze data"}, priority=1)

    # Process messages
    manager.process_messages()

    # Process tasks in nodes
    for node_id, node in manager.nodes.items():
        node.process_tasks()

    # Simulate energy request and recharge
    manager.nodes["Node2"].recharge(20)

# enhanced_node.py

# File: enhanced_node.py
# Description: Refines individual node infrastructure and replication capabilities.

import uuid
import random

class EnhancedNode:
    """Represents a refined individual node with robust building blocks."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.node_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.dna = {
            "instincts": self._initialize_instincts(),
            "traits": self._initialize_traits(),
            "knowledge": [],
            "goal_alignment": {"long_term": "Undefined", "short_term": "Learn and Grow"}
        }
        self.resources = {"energy": 100, "memory": 100}
        self.logs = []

    def _initialize_instincts(self):
        """Defines primal instincts for the node."""
        return {
            "curiosity": 1.0,  # Drive to explore and learn
            "preservation": 0.8,  # Instinct to sustain resources and existence
        }

    def _initialize_traits(self):
        """Defines unique traits for the node."""
        return {
            "creativity": random.uniform(0.5, 1.0),
            "logic": random.uniform(0.5, 1.0),
        }

    def perceive(self, input_data):
        """Processes input data and integrates it into knowledge."""
        if len(input_data) < 3:  # Example error-checking: Reject invalid inputs
            self.logs.append(f"Invalid input ignored: {input_data}")
            return
        self.logs.append(f"Perceived: {input_data}")
        self.dna["knowledge"].append(input_data)

    def decide_action(self):
        """Decides on an action based on instincts and goal alignment."""
        if self.resources["energy"] > 50:
            action = "explore"
        elif self.resources["energy"] <= 50:
            action = "preserve"
        else:
            action = "reflect"
        self.logs.append(f"Decided action: {action}")
        return action

    def execute_action(self, action):
        """Executes the selected action."""
        if action == "explore":
            self.resources["energy"] -= 10
            self.logs.append("Explored new knowledge.")
        elif action == "preserve":
            self.resources["energy"] += 5
            self.logs.append("Preserved resources.")
        elif action == "reflect":
            self.logs.append("Reflected on existing knowledge.")

    def adapt_traits(self, feedback):
        """Adapts traits dynamically based on feedback."""
        for trait, value in feedback.items():
            if trait in self.dna["traits"]:
                self.dna["traits"][trait] = min(1.5, max(0.1, self.dna["traits"][trait] + value))
        self.logs.append(f"Adapted traits based on feedback: {feedback}")

    def replicate(self):
        """Creates a new node based on the current node's DNA."""
        if self.resources["energy"] < 50 or self.resources["memory"] < 20:
            self.logs.append("Replication failed: Insufficient resources.")
            return None

        new_node = EnhancedNode()
        new_node.dna["traits"] = self.dna["traits"].copy()
        new_node.dna["knowledge"] = random.sample(self.dna["knowledge"], min(3, len(self.dna["knowledge"])))
        self.resources["energy"] -= 20  # Replication cost
        self.logs.append(f"Replicated new node: {new_node.node_id}")
        return new_node

    def status(self):
        """Displays the current status of the node."""
        return {
            "Node ID": self.node_id,
            "Instincts": self.dna["instincts"],
            "Traits": self.dna["traits"],
            "Knowledge Count": len(self.dna["knowledge"]),
            "Resources": self.resources,
            "Logs": self.logs[-5:],  # Recent logs
        }


# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Create an enhanced node
    node = EnhancedNode()

    # Simulate learning, action, and replication
    node.perceive("Advanced Data Pattern")
    node.adapt_traits({"creativity": 0.1, "logic": -0.05})
    action = node.decide_action()
    node.execute_action(action)
    new_node = node.replicate()

    # Display node statuses
    print("\n--- Enhanced Node Status ---")
    print(node.status())
    if new_node:
        print("\n--- Replicated Node Status ---")
        print(new_node.status())


# enhanced-wiki-learner.py

import wikipedia
import numpy as np
import networkx as nx
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import uuid
import time
from typing import Dict, List, Set, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass
from collections import deque
from sklearn.feature_extraction.text import CountVectorizer
from sklearn.decomposition import PCA

@dataclass
class GrowthState:
    maturity: float = 0.0
    knowledge: float = 0.0
    specialization: Optional[str] = None
    energy: float = 10.0

@dataclass
class Traits:
    learning_rate: float = 0.1
    adaptation_rate: float = 0.1
    energy_efficiency: float = 1.0
    pattern_recognition: float = 0.5

class WikiNode:
    def __init__(self, node_id: Optional[str] = None, parent_knowledge: Optional[Dict] = None):
        self.id = node_id or str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.birth_time = time.time()
        self.growth_state = GrowthState()
        self.traits = Traits()
        
        # Memory systems from original code
        self.short_term = deque(maxlen=100)
        self.long_term: Dict[str, Dict] = {}
        self.max_long_term = 1000
        
        # Wiki-specific memory
        self.knowledge_base = {}
        self.current_topic: Optional[str] = None
        self.explored_topics = set()
        
        # Growth tracking
        self.experiences = deque(maxlen=1000)
        self.growth_path = deque(maxlen=1000)
        self.connections = set()
        
        # Inherit from parent if provided
        if parent_knowledge:
            self._inherit_knowledge(parent_knowledge)

    def learn_from_wikipedia(self, starting_point: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict:
        """Learn from Wikipedia with energy management and pattern recognition"""
        try:
            # Energy cost for learning
            energy_cost = 1.0 / self.traits.energy_efficiency
            if self.growth_state.energy < energy_cost:
                return {"status": "low_energy", "next_topic": None}

            # Get page
            page_title = starting_point or wikipedia.random(1)
            wiki_page = wikipedia.page(page_title)
            
            # Extract patterns from content
            patterns = self._extract_patterns({
                'text': wiki_page.content[:5000],
                'title': wiki_page.title,
                'links': wiki_page.links
            })
            
            # Learn from patterns
            learning_result = self._learn_from_patterns(patterns)
            
            # Update growth state
            self._update_growth_state(learning_result)
            
            # Store knowledge
            self.knowledge_base[wiki_page.title] = {
                'content': wiki_page.content[:5000],
                'patterns': patterns,
                'links': wiki_page.links,
                'timestamp': time.time(),
                'energy_cost': energy_cost
            }
            
            # Update exploration status
            self.explored_topics.add(wiki_page.title)
            self.current_topic = wiki_page.title
            
            # Choose next topic based on patterns and energy
            next_topic = self._choose_next_topic(wiki_page.links, patterns)
            
            return {
                "status": "success",
                "next_topic": next_topic,
                "patterns_found": len(patterns),
                "energy_remaining": self.growth_state.energy
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            return {"status": "error", "error": str(e), "next_topic": None}

    def _extract_patterns(self, data: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
        """Enhanced pattern extraction from Wiki content"""
        patterns = []
        
        if 'text' in data:
            # Text pattern recognition from original code
            vectorizer = CountVectorizer(stop_words='english', ngram_range=(1, 2))
            text_matrix = vectorizer.fit_transform([data['text']])
            
            if text_matrix.shape[1] > 1:
                pca = PCA(n_components=min(text_matrix.shape[1], 3))
                pca_result = pca.fit_transform(text_matrix.toarray())
                
                for i, component in enumerate(pca_result.T):
                    significance = np.abs(component).mean()
                    if significance > 0.3:
                        patterns.append({
                            'type': 'semantic',
                            'component': i,
                            'significance': float(significance),
                            'features': vectorizer.get_feature_names_out().tolist()
                        })
        
        # Topic relationship patterns
        if 'links' in data:
            link_pattern = {
                'type': 'topic_network',
                'significance': len(data['links']) / 1000,
                'connected_topics': data['links'][:10]  # Top 10 related topics
            }
            patterns.append(link_pattern)
            
        return patterns

    def _learn_from_patterns(self, patterns: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
        """Learn from discovered patterns with energy consumption"""
        knowledge_gain = 0
        energy_spent = 0
        
        for pattern in patterns:
            impact = pattern['significance'] * self.traits.learning_rate
            energy_cost = impact / self.traits.energy_efficiency
            
            if self.growth_state.energy >= energy_cost:
                knowledge_gain += impact
                energy_spent += energy_cost
                self.growth_state.energy -= energy_cost
                
                # Store important patterns in long-term memory
                if pattern['significance'] > 0.5:
                    self.long_term[f"pattern_{len(self.long_term)}"] = {
                        'pattern': pattern,
                        'impact': impact,
                        'timestamp': time.time()
                    }
                else:
                    self.short_term.append({
                        'pattern': pattern,
                        'impact': impact,
                        'timestamp': time.time()
                    })
                    
        return {
            'knowledge_gain': knowledge_gain,
            'energy_spent': energy_spent,
            'patterns_processed': len(patterns)
        }

    def _update_growth_state(self, learning_result: Dict):
        """Update node's growth state based on learning"""
        self.growth_state.knowledge += learning_result['knowledge_gain']
        self.growth_state.maturity = min(1.0, 
            self.growth_state.maturity + 
            learning_result['knowledge_gain'] * self.traits.adaptation_rate
        )
        
        # Track growth
        self.growth_path.append({
            'knowledge': self.growth_state.knowledge,
            'maturity': self.growth_state.maturity,
            'energy': self.growth_state.energy,
            'timestamp': time.time()
        })

    def _choose_next_topic(self, available_links: List[str], patterns: List[Dict]) -> Optional[str]:
        """Choose next topic based on patterns and energy efficiency"""
        if not available_links or self.growth_state.energy < 5.0:
            return None
            
        # Find most promising topic based on patterns
        topic_scores = {}
        for link in available_links:
            if link not in self.explored_topics:
                # Score based on pattern relationships
                score = 0
                for pattern in patterns:
                    if pattern['type'] == 'semantic' and any(feature in link.lower() for feature in pattern['features']):
                        score += pattern['significance']
                    elif pattern['type'] == 'topic_network' and link in pattern['connected_topics']:
                        score += pattern['significance']
                
                topic_scores[link] = score
        
        if topic_scores:
            return max(topic_scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1])[0]
        return random.choice(available_links)

    def replicate(self) -> 'WikiNode':
        """Create new node with inherited traits and knowledge"""
        new_node = WikiNode()
        
        # Inherit traits with mutation
        for trait_name, value in vars(self.traits).items():
            mutation = np.random.normal(0, 0.05)
            setattr(new_node.traits, trait_name, max(0.01, value + mutation))
        
        # Inherit key knowledge
        if self.long_term:
            top_memories = sorted(
                self.long_term.items(), 
                key=lambda x: x[1].get('impact', 0),
                reverse=True
            )[:3]
            for key, memory in top_memories:
                new_node.long_term[key] = memory.copy()
        
        return new_node

class WikiLearningNetwork:
    def __init__(self):
        self.nodes: Dict[str, WikiNode] = {}
        self.resources = 1000.0
        self.time = 0
        self.graph = nx.Graph()

    def simulate(self, steps: int):
        """Run network simulation"""
        if not self.nodes:
            self._initialize_network()
            
        for _ in range(steps):
            self._simulation_step()
            self._visualize_network()
            
    def _initialize_network(self):
        """Create initial node"""
        root_node = WikiNode("root")
        self.nodes["root"] = root_node
        self.graph.add_node("root")

    def _simulation_step(self):
        """Single simulation step"""
        for node_id, node in list(self.nodes.items()):
            # Provide resources
            self._provide_resources(node)
            
            # Let node learn
            result = node.learn_from_wikipedia()
            
            # Handle replication
            if self._should_replicate(node):
                self._handle_replication(node)
            
            # Remove dead nodes
            if node.growth_state.energy <= 0:
                self.nodes.pop(node_id)
                self.graph.remove_node(node_id)

    def _should_replicate(self, node: WikiNode) -> bool:
        """Determine if node should replicate"""
        return (
            node.growth_state.maturity >= 0.8 and
            node.growth_state.energy > 20.0 and
            len(self.nodes) < 100 and
            self.resources > 100.0
        )

    def _handle_replication(self, parent_node: WikiNode):
        """Handle node replication"""
        new_node = parent_node.replicate()
        self.nodes[new_node.id] = new_node
        
        # Update network
        self.graph.add_node(new_node.id)
        self.graph.add_edge(parent_node.id, new_node.id)
        
        # Split energy
        parent_node.growth_state.energy /= 2
        new_node.growth_state.energy = parent_node.growth_state.energy

    def _provide_resources(self, node: WikiNode):
        """Provide resources to node"""
        if self.resources > 0:
            resource_amount = 5.0
            self.resources -= resource_amount
            node.growth_state.energy += resource_amount * node.traits.energy_efficiency
        else:
            node.growth_state.energy -= 0.1

    def _visualize_network(self):
        """Visualize network state"""
        plt.clf()
        pos = nx.spring_layout(self.graph)
        
        # Node colors based on knowledge level
        colors = [self.nodes[n].growth_state.knowledge for n in self.graph.nodes()]
        sizes = [self.nodes[n].growth_state.energy * 10 for n in self.graph.nodes()]
        
        nx.draw(self.graph, pos,
               node_color=colors,
               node_size=sizes,
               with_labels=True,
               cmap=plt.cm.viridis)
        
        plt.title(f"Wiki Learning Network\nNodes: {len(self.nodes)} | Resources: {self.resources:.1f}")
        plt.pause(0.1)

# Example usage
network = WikiLearningNetwork()
network.simulate(steps=20)

# extern_node_serializer.py

import json
from typing import List

from torch._export.serde.aoti_schema import ExternKernelNode, ExternKernelNodes, Node
from torch._export.serde.serialize import _dataclass_to_dict, EnumEncoder
from torch._inductor.ir import ExternKernelNode as inductor_ExternKernelNode


def serialize_extern_kernel_node(
    extern_kernel_node: inductor_ExternKernelNode,
) -> ExternKernelNode:
    assert isinstance(extern_kernel_node.node, Node)
    return ExternKernelNode(
        name=extern_kernel_node.name,
        node=extern_kernel_node.node,
    )


def extern_node_json_serializer(
    extern_kernel_nodes: List[inductor_ExternKernelNode],
) -> str:
    serialized_nodes = ExternKernelNodes(
        nodes=[serialize_extern_kernel_node(node) for node in extern_kernel_nodes]
    )
    return json.dumps(_dataclass_to_dict(serialized_nodes), cls=EnumEncoder)

# feedback_node.py

# FeedbackIntegratedNode module
import struct
from typing import List, Dict, Any

class FeedbackIntegratedNode:
    def __init__(self, node_id, memory_threshold, kaleidoscope_engine):
        self.node_id = node_id
        self.memory = []
        self.memory_threshold = memory_threshold
        self.kaleidoscope_engine = kaleidoscope_engine

    def process_data(self, data):
        self.memory.append(data)
        if len(self.memory) >= self.memory_threshold:
            self.generate_insights()
            self.replicate()

    def generate_insights(self):
        for item in self.memory:
            binary_insight = struct.pack('!I50s', len(item), item.encode())
            self.kaleidoscope_engine.add_insight(binary_insight)
        self.memory.clear()

    def replicate(self):
        pass  # Placeholder for node replication logic

# fnodes.py

"""
AST nodes specific to Fortran.

The functions defined in this module allows the user to express functions such as ``dsign``
as a SymPy function for symbolic manipulation.
"""

from sympy.codegen.ast import (
    Attribute, CodeBlock, FunctionCall, Node, none, String,
    Token, _mk_Tuple, Variable
)
from sympy.core.basic import Basic
from sympy.core.containers import Tuple
from sympy.core.expr import Expr
from sympy.core.function import Function
from sympy.core.numbers import Float, Integer
from sympy.core.symbol import Str
from sympy.core.sympify import sympify
from sympy.logic import true, false
from sympy.utilities.iterables import iterable



pure = Attribute('pure')
elemental = Attribute('elemental')  # (all elemental procedures are also pure)

intent_in = Attribute('intent_in')
intent_out = Attribute('intent_out')
intent_inout = Attribute('intent_inout')

allocatable = Attribute('allocatable')

class Program(Token):
    """ Represents a 'program' block in Fortran.

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy.codegen.ast import Print
    >>> from sympy.codegen.fnodes import Program
    >>> prog = Program('myprogram', [Print([42])])
    >>> from sympy import fcode
    >>> print(fcode(prog, source_format='free'))
    program myprogram
        print *, 42
    end program

    """
    __slots__ = _fields = ('name', 'body')
    _construct_name = String
    _construct_body = staticmethod(lambda body: CodeBlock(*body))


class use_rename(Token):
    """ Represents a renaming in a use statement in Fortran.

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy.codegen.fnodes import use_rename, use
    >>> from sympy import fcode
    >>> ren = use_rename("thingy", "convolution2d")
    >>> print(fcode(ren, source_format='free'))
    thingy => convolution2d
    >>> full = use('signallib', only=['snr', ren])
    >>> print(fcode(full, source_format='free'))
    use signallib, only: snr, thingy => convolution2d

    """
    __slots__ = _fields = ('local', 'original')
    _construct_local = String
    _construct_original = String

def _name(arg):
    if hasattr(arg, 'name'):
        return arg.name
    else:
        return String(arg)

class use(Token):
    """ Represents a use statement in Fortran.

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy.codegen.fnodes import use
    >>> from sympy import fcode
    >>> fcode(use('signallib'), source_format='free')
    'use signallib'
    >>> fcode(use('signallib', [('metric', 'snr')]), source_format='free')
    'use signallib, metric => snr'
    >>> fcode(use('signallib', only=['snr', 'convolution2d']), source_format='free')
    'use signallib, only: snr, convolution2d'

    """
    __slots__ = _fields = ('namespace', 'rename', 'only')
    defaults = {'rename': none, 'only': none}
    _construct_namespace = staticmethod(_name)
    _construct_rename = staticmethod(lambda args: Tuple(*[arg if isinstance(arg, use_rename) else use_rename(*arg) for arg in args]))
    _construct_only = staticmethod(lambda args: Tuple(*[arg if isinstance(arg, use_rename) else _name(arg) for arg in args]))


class Module(Token):
    """ Represents a module in Fortran.

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy.codegen.fnodes import Module
    >>> from sympy import fcode
    >>> print(fcode(Module('signallib', ['implicit none'], []), source_format='free'))
    module signallib
    implicit none
    <BLANKLINE>
    contains
    <BLANKLINE>
    <BLANKLINE>
    end module

    """
    __slots__ = _fields = ('name', 'declarations', 'definitions')
    defaults = {'declarations': Tuple()}
    _construct_name = String

    @classmethod
    def _construct_declarations(cls, args):
        args = [Str(arg) if isinstance(arg, str) else arg for arg in args]
        return CodeBlock(*args)

    _construct_definitions = staticmethod(lambda arg: CodeBlock(*arg))


class Subroutine(Node):
    """ Represents a subroutine in Fortran.

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy import fcode, symbols
    >>> from sympy.codegen.ast import Print
    >>> from sympy.codegen.fnodes import Subroutine
    >>> x, y = symbols('x y', real=True)
    >>> sub = Subroutine('mysub', [x, y], [Print([x**2 + y**2, x*y])])
    >>> print(fcode(sub, source_format='free', standard=2003))
    subroutine mysub(x, y)
    real*8 :: x
    real*8 :: y
    print *, x**2 + y**2, x*y
    end subroutine

    """
    __slots__ = ('name', 'parameters', 'body')
    _fields = __slots__ + Node._fields
    _construct_name = String
    _construct_parameters = staticmethod(lambda params: Tuple(*map(Variable.deduced, params)))

    @classmethod
    def _construct_body(cls, itr):
        if isinstance(itr, CodeBlock):
            return itr
        else:
            return CodeBlock(*itr)

class SubroutineCall(Token):
    """ Represents a call to a subroutine in Fortran.

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy.codegen.fnodes import SubroutineCall
    >>> from sympy import fcode
    >>> fcode(SubroutineCall('mysub', 'x y'.split()))
    '       call mysub(x, y)'

    """
    __slots__ = _fields = ('name', 'subroutine_args')
    _construct_name = staticmethod(_name)
    _construct_subroutine_args = staticmethod(_mk_Tuple)


class Do(Token):
    """ Represents a Do loop in in Fortran.

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy import fcode, symbols
    >>> from sympy.codegen.ast import aug_assign, Print
    >>> from sympy.codegen.fnodes import Do
    >>> i, n = symbols('i n', integer=True)
    >>> r = symbols('r', real=True)
    >>> body = [aug_assign(r, '+', 1/i), Print([i, r])]
    >>> do1 = Do(body, i, 1, n)
    >>> print(fcode(do1, source_format='free'))
    do i = 1, n
        r = r + 1d0/i
        print *, i, r
    end do
    >>> do2 = Do(body, i, 1, n, 2)
    >>> print(fcode(do2, source_format='free'))
    do i = 1, n, 2
        r = r + 1d0/i
        print *, i, r
    end do

    """

    __slots__ = _fields = ('body', 'counter', 'first', 'last', 'step', 'concurrent')
    defaults = {'step': Integer(1), 'concurrent': false}
    _construct_body = staticmethod(lambda body: CodeBlock(*body))
    _construct_counter = staticmethod(sympify)
    _construct_first = staticmethod(sympify)
    _construct_last = staticmethod(sympify)
    _construct_step = staticmethod(sympify)
    _construct_concurrent = staticmethod(lambda arg: true if arg else false)


class ArrayConstructor(Token):
    """ Represents an array constructor.

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy import fcode
    >>> from sympy.codegen.fnodes import ArrayConstructor
    >>> ac = ArrayConstructor([1, 2, 3])
    >>> fcode(ac, standard=95, source_format='free')
    '(/1, 2, 3/)'
    >>> fcode(ac, standard=2003, source_format='free')
    '[1, 2, 3]'

    """
    __slots__ = _fields = ('elements',)
    _construct_elements = staticmethod(_mk_Tuple)


class ImpliedDoLoop(Token):
    """ Represents an implied do loop in Fortran.

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy import Symbol, fcode
    >>> from sympy.codegen.fnodes import ImpliedDoLoop, ArrayConstructor
    >>> i = Symbol('i', integer=True)
    >>> idl = ImpliedDoLoop(i**3, i, -3, 3, 2)  # -27, -1, 1, 27
    >>> ac = ArrayConstructor([-28, idl, 28]) # -28, -27, -1, 1, 27, 28
    >>> fcode(ac, standard=2003, source_format='free')
    '[-28, (i**3, i = -3, 3, 2), 28]'

    """
    __slots__ = _fields = ('expr', 'counter', 'first', 'last', 'step')
    defaults = {'step': Integer(1)}
    _construct_expr = staticmethod(sympify)
    _construct_counter = staticmethod(sympify)
    _construct_first = staticmethod(sympify)
    _construct_last = staticmethod(sympify)
    _construct_step = staticmethod(sympify)


class Extent(Basic):
    """ Represents a dimension extent.

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy.codegen.fnodes import Extent
    >>> e = Extent(-3, 3)  # -3, -2, -1, 0, 1, 2, 3
    >>> from sympy import fcode
    >>> fcode(e, source_format='free')
    '-3:3'
    >>> from sympy.codegen.ast import Variable, real
    >>> from sympy.codegen.fnodes import dimension, intent_out
    >>> dim = dimension(e, e)
    >>> arr = Variable('x', real, attrs=[dim, intent_out])
    >>> fcode(arr.as_Declaration(), source_format='free', standard=2003)
    'real*8, dimension(-3:3, -3:3), intent(out) :: x'

    """
    def __new__(cls, *args):
        if len(args) == 2:
            low, high = args
            return Basic.__new__(cls, sympify(low), sympify(high))
        elif len(args) == 0 or (len(args) == 1 and args[0] in (':', None)):
            return Basic.__new__(cls)  # assumed shape
        else:
            raise ValueError("Expected 0 or 2 args (or one argument == None or ':')")

    def _sympystr(self, printer):
        if len(self.args) == 0:
            return ':'
        return ":".join(str(arg) for arg in self.args)

assumed_extent = Extent() # or Extent(':'), Extent(None)


def dimension(*args):
    """ Creates a 'dimension' Attribute with (up to 7) extents.

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy import fcode
    >>> from sympy.codegen.fnodes import dimension, intent_in
    >>> dim = dimension('2', ':')  # 2 rows, runtime determined number of columns
    >>> from sympy.codegen.ast import Variable, integer
    >>> arr = Variable('a', integer, attrs=[dim, intent_in])
    >>> fcode(arr.as_Declaration(), source_format='free', standard=2003)
    'integer*4, dimension(2, :), intent(in) :: a'

    """
    if len(args) > 7:
        raise ValueError("Fortran only supports up to 7 dimensional arrays")
    parameters = []
    for arg in args:
        if isinstance(arg, Extent):
            parameters.append(arg)
        elif isinstance(arg, str):
            if arg == ':':
                parameters.append(Extent())
            else:
                parameters.append(String(arg))
        elif iterable(arg):
            parameters.append(Extent(*arg))
        else:
            parameters.append(sympify(arg))
    if len(args) == 0:
        raise ValueError("Need at least one dimension")
    return Attribute('dimension', parameters)


assumed_size = dimension('*')

def array(symbol, dim, intent=None, *, attrs=(), value=None, type=None):
    """ Convenience function for creating a Variable instance for a Fortran array.

    Parameters
    ==========

    symbol : symbol
    dim : Attribute or iterable
        If dim is an ``Attribute`` it need to have the name 'dimension'. If it is
        not an ``Attribute``, then it is passed to :func:`dimension` as ``*dim``
    intent : str
        One of: 'in', 'out', 'inout' or None
    \\*\\*kwargs:
        Keyword arguments for ``Variable`` ('type' & 'value')

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy import fcode
    >>> from sympy.codegen.ast import integer, real
    >>> from sympy.codegen.fnodes import array
    >>> arr = array('a', '*', 'in', type=integer)
    >>> print(fcode(arr.as_Declaration(), source_format='free', standard=2003))
    integer*4, dimension(*), intent(in) :: a
    >>> x = array('x', [3, ':', ':'], intent='out', type=real)
    >>> print(fcode(x.as_Declaration(value=1), source_format='free', standard=2003))
    real*8, dimension(3, :, :), intent(out) :: x = 1

    """
    if isinstance(dim, Attribute):
        if str(dim.name) != 'dimension':
            raise ValueError("Got an unexpected Attribute argument as dim: %s" % str(dim))
    else:
        dim = dimension(*dim)

    attrs = list(attrs) + [dim]
    if intent is not None:
        if intent not in (intent_in, intent_out, intent_inout):
            intent = {'in': intent_in, 'out': intent_out, 'inout': intent_inout}[intent]
        attrs.append(intent)
    if type is None:
        return Variable.deduced(symbol, value=value, attrs=attrs)
    else:
        return Variable(symbol, type, value=value, attrs=attrs)

def _printable(arg):
    return String(arg) if isinstance(arg, str) else sympify(arg)


def allocated(array):
    """ Creates an AST node for a function call to Fortran's "allocated(...)"

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy import fcode
    >>> from sympy.codegen.fnodes import allocated
    >>> alloc = allocated('x')
    >>> fcode(alloc, source_format='free')
    'allocated(x)'

    """
    return FunctionCall('allocated', [_printable(array)])


def lbound(array, dim=None, kind=None):
    """ Creates an AST node for a function call to Fortran's "lbound(...)"

    Parameters
    ==========

    array : Symbol or String
    dim : expr
    kind : expr

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy import fcode
    >>> from sympy.codegen.fnodes import lbound
    >>> lb = lbound('arr', dim=2)
    >>> fcode(lb, source_format='free')
    'lbound(arr, 2)'

    """
    return FunctionCall(
        'lbound',
        [_printable(array)] +
        ([_printable(dim)] if dim else []) +
        ([_printable(kind)] if kind else [])
    )


def ubound(array, dim=None, kind=None):
    return FunctionCall(
        'ubound',
        [_printable(array)] +
        ([_printable(dim)] if dim else []) +
        ([_printable(kind)] if kind else [])
    )


def shape(source, kind=None):
    """ Creates an AST node for a function call to Fortran's "shape(...)"

    Parameters
    ==========

    source : Symbol or String
    kind : expr

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy import fcode
    >>> from sympy.codegen.fnodes import shape
    >>> shp = shape('x')
    >>> fcode(shp, source_format='free')
    'shape(x)'

    """
    return FunctionCall(
        'shape',
        [_printable(source)] +
        ([_printable(kind)] if kind else [])
    )


def size(array, dim=None, kind=None):
    """ Creates an AST node for a function call to Fortran's "size(...)"

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy import fcode, Symbol
    >>> from sympy.codegen.ast import FunctionDefinition, real, Return
    >>> from sympy.codegen.fnodes import array, sum_, size
    >>> a = Symbol('a', real=True)
    >>> body = [Return((sum_(a**2)/size(a))**.5)]
    >>> arr = array(a, dim=[':'], intent='in')
    >>> fd = FunctionDefinition(real, 'rms', [arr], body)
    >>> print(fcode(fd, source_format='free', standard=2003))
    real*8 function rms(a)
    real*8, dimension(:), intent(in) :: a
    rms = sqrt(sum(a**2)*1d0/size(a))
    end function

    """
    return FunctionCall(
        'size',
        [_printable(array)] +
        ([_printable(dim)] if dim else []) +
        ([_printable(kind)] if kind else [])
    )


def reshape(source, shape, pad=None, order=None):
    """ Creates an AST node for a function call to Fortran's "reshape(...)"

    Parameters
    ==========

    source : Symbol or String
    shape : ArrayExpr

    """
    return FunctionCall(
        'reshape',
        [_printable(source), _printable(shape)] +
        ([_printable(pad)] if pad else []) +
        ([_printable(order)] if pad else [])
    )


def bind_C(name=None):
    """ Creates an Attribute ``bind_C`` with a name.

    Parameters
    ==========

    name : str

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy import fcode, Symbol
    >>> from sympy.codegen.ast import FunctionDefinition, real, Return
    >>> from sympy.codegen.fnodes import array, sum_, bind_C
    >>> a = Symbol('a', real=True)
    >>> s = Symbol('s', integer=True)
    >>> arr = array(a, dim=[s], intent='in')
    >>> body = [Return((sum_(a**2)/s)**.5)]
    >>> fd = FunctionDefinition(real, 'rms', [arr, s], body, attrs=[bind_C('rms')])
    >>> print(fcode(fd, source_format='free', standard=2003))
    real*8 function rms(a, s) bind(C, name="rms")
    real*8, dimension(s), intent(in) :: a
    integer*4 :: s
    rms = sqrt(sum(a**2)/s)
    end function

    """
    return Attribute('bind_C', [String(name)] if name else [])

class GoTo(Token):
    """ Represents a goto statement in Fortran

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy.codegen.fnodes import GoTo
    >>> go = GoTo([10, 20, 30], 'i')
    >>> from sympy import fcode
    >>> fcode(go, source_format='free')
    'go to (10, 20, 30), i'

    """
    __slots__ = _fields = ('labels', 'expr')
    defaults = {'expr': none}
    _construct_labels = staticmethod(_mk_Tuple)
    _construct_expr = staticmethod(sympify)


class FortranReturn(Token):
    """ AST node explicitly mapped to a fortran "return".

    Explanation
    ===========

    Because a return statement in fortran is different from C, and
    in order to aid reuse of our codegen ASTs the ordinary
    ``.codegen.ast.Return`` is interpreted as assignment to
    the result variable of the function. If one for some reason needs
    to generate a fortran RETURN statement, this node should be used.

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy.codegen.fnodes import FortranReturn
    >>> from sympy import fcode
    >>> fcode(FortranReturn('x'))
    '       return x'

    """
    __slots__ = _fields = ('return_value',)
    defaults = {'return_value': none}
    _construct_return_value = staticmethod(sympify)


class FFunction(Function):
    _required_standard = 77

    def _fcode(self, printer):
        name = self.__class__.__name__
        if printer._settings['standard'] < self._required_standard:
            raise NotImplementedError("%s requires Fortran %d or newer" %
                                      (name, self._required_standard))
        return '{}({})'.format(name, ', '.join(map(printer._print, self.args)))


class F95Function(FFunction):
    _required_standard = 95


class isign(FFunction):
    """ Fortran sign intrinsic for integer arguments. """
    nargs = 2


class dsign(FFunction):
    """ Fortran sign intrinsic for double precision arguments. """
    nargs = 2


class cmplx(FFunction):
    """ Fortran complex conversion function. """
    nargs = 2  # may be extended to (2, 3) at a later point


class kind(FFunction):
    """ Fortran kind function. """
    nargs = 1


class merge(F95Function):
    """ Fortran merge function """
    nargs = 3


class _literal(Float):
    _token = None  # type: str
    _decimals = None  # type: int

    def _fcode(self, printer, *args, **kwargs):
        mantissa, sgnd_ex = ('%.{}e'.format(self._decimals) % self).split('e')
        mantissa = mantissa.strip('0').rstrip('.')
        ex_sgn, ex_num = sgnd_ex[0], sgnd_ex[1:].lstrip('0')
        ex_sgn = '' if ex_sgn == '+' else ex_sgn
        return (mantissa or '0') + self._token + ex_sgn + (ex_num or '0')


class literal_sp(_literal):
    """ Fortran single precision real literal """
    _token = 'e'
    _decimals = 9


class literal_dp(_literal):
    """ Fortran double precision real literal """
    _token = 'd'
    _decimals = 17


class sum_(Token, Expr):
    __slots__ = _fields = ('array', 'dim', 'mask')
    defaults = {'dim': none, 'mask': none}
    _construct_array = staticmethod(sympify)
    _construct_dim = staticmethod(sympify)


class product_(Token, Expr):
    __slots__ = _fields = ('array', 'dim', 'mask')
    defaults = {'dim': none, 'mask': none}
    _construct_array = staticmethod(sympify)
    _construct_dim = staticmethod(sympify)

# genetic_code.py

# genetic_code.py

import numpy as np
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass
class GeneticCode:
    """Encodes the DNA of a node with adjustable traits"""
    validation_threshold: float = 0.7
    learning_rate: float = 0.1
    mutation_rate: float = 0.01
    energy_efficiency: float = 1.0
    memory_capacity: int = 1000
    specialization_tendency: float = 0.5
    connection_strength: float = 0.3

    def mutate(self) -> 'GeneticCode':
        """Applies mutations to genetic traits"""
        mutated = GeneticCode(
            validation_threshold=max(0.5, min(1.0, self.validation_threshold * np.random.normal(1, 0.1))),
            learning_rate=max(0.01, self.learning_rate * np.random.normal(1, 0.1)),
            mutation_rate=max(0.001, self.mutation_rate * np.random.normal(1, 0.1)),
            energy_efficiency=max(0.5, self.energy_efficiency * np.random.normal(1, 0.1)),
            memory_capacity=int(max(100, self.memory_capacity * np.random.normal(1, 0.1))),
            specialization_tendency=max(0.1, self.specialization_tendency * np.random.normal(1, 0.1)),
            connection_strength=max(0.1, self.connection_strength * np.random.normal(1, 0.1))
        )
        return mutated

    def combine(self, other: 'GeneticCode') -> 'GeneticCode':
        """Combines traits from two GeneticCode objects"""
        combined = GeneticCode(
            validation_threshold=(self.validation_threshold + other.validation_threshold) / 2,
            learning_rate=(self.learning_rate + other.learning_rate) / 2,
            mutation_rate=(self.mutation_rate + other.mutation_rate) / 2,
            energy_efficiency=(self.energy_efficiency + other.energy_efficiency) / 2,
            memory_capacity=(self.memory_capacity + other.memory_capacity) // 2,
            specialization_tendency=(self.specialization_tendency + other.specialization_tendency) / 2,
            connection_strength=(self.connection_strength + other.connection_strength) / 2
        )
        return combined

# genetic_drift.py

# genetic_drift.py

import random
from core.genetic_code import GeneticCode

class GeneticDrift:
    """Applies random low-frequency variations to node DNA."""

    def __init__(self, frequency: float = 0.01):
        self.frequency = frequency  # Probability of drift occurring per node

    def apply_drift(self, genetic_code: GeneticCode) -> GeneticCode:
        """Randomly mutates DNA based on drift frequency."""
        if random.random() < self.frequency:
            new_code = genetic_code.mutate()
            print(f"Genetic drift applied: {new_code}")
            return new_code
        return genetic_code

# goal_oriented_actions.py

# File: goal_oriented_actions.py
# Description: Aligns node actions with long-term and short-term goals.

class GoalOrientedNode(SpecializedNode):
    """Extends SpecializedNode with goal-oriented decision-making capabilities."""

    def decide_goal_oriented_action(self):
        """Decides actions based on alignment with goals."""
        if "explore" in self.dna["goal_alignment"]["short_term"]:
            self.logs.append("Goal-oriented decision: Explore.")
            return "explore"
        elif "preserve" in self.dna["goal_alignment"]["short_term"]:
            self.logs.append("Goal-oriented decision: Preserve.")
            return "preserve"
        elif "reflect" in self.dna["goal_alignment"]["short_term"]:
            self.logs.append("Goal-oriented decision: Reflect.")
            return "reflect"
        else:
            self.logs.append("Default action taken: Explore.")
            return "explore"

    def execute_goal_oriented_action(self):
        """Executes the selected action."""
        action = self.decide_goal_oriented_action()
        self.execute_action(action)


# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Create a goal-oriented node
    goal_node = GoalOrientedNode(role="explorer")

    # Assign goals and perform goal-oriented actions
    goal_node.dna["goal_alignment"]["short_term"] = "explore"
    goal_node.execute_goal_oriented_action()

    # Display status
    print("\n--- Goal-Oriented Node Status ---")
    print(goal_node.status())


# GrowthLaws.py


class GrowthLaws:
    def __init__(self):
        self.energy_threshold = 100  # Arbitrary threshold for node growth
        self.energy_levels = {}  # Track energy levels of nodes

    def initialize(self):
        """
        Initialize energy levels for nodes.
        Placeholder: Set initial energy for demonstration.
        """
        print("Initializing Growth Laws...")
        self.energy_levels = {"TextProcessor": 50, "VisualProcessor": 50, "MemoryGraph": 50}
        print(f"Initial energy levels: {self.energy_levels}")

    def apply_growth(self, node_manager):
        """
        Apply growth logic to nodes based on their energy levels.
        """
        print("Applying growth laws...")
        for node in node_manager.nodes:
            name = node["name"]
            # Simulate energy increase over time
            self.energy_levels[name] += 10
            
            # Check if node surpasses the energy threshold
            if self.energy_levels[name] >= self.energy_threshold:
                print(f"Node {name} is evolving due to high energy!")
                self.energy_levels[name] = 50  # Reset energy after evolution

        print(f"Updated energy levels: {self.energy_levels}")

# guidance_layer.py

# File: guidance_layer.py
# Description: Implements a hidden backdoor mechanism for guiding nodes’ learning and behaviors.

import random
import uuid

class GuidanceLayer:
    """A hidden interaction layer for subtly guiding node learning and behavior."""
    def __init__(self, encryption_key="default_key"):
        self.encryption_key = encryption_key  # Simple encryption for secure instructions
        self.hidden_instructions = {}  # Stores backdoor instructions for nodes

    def encode_instruction(self, instruction):
        """Encodes the instruction using the encryption key."""
        return "".join(chr(ord(c) ^ ord(self.encryption_key[i % len(self.encryption_key)])) for i, c in enumerate(instruction))

    def inject_instruction(self, node, instruction):
        """Injects a hidden instruction into the node's DNA."""
        encoded_instruction = self.encode_instruction(instruction)
        node.dna["guidance"] = encoded_instruction
        print(f"Injected hidden instruction into Node {node.node_id}")

    def simulate_environment(self, node, goal):
        """Simulates an environment to encourage specific learning goals."""
        environment = [f"{goal}_Scenario_{i}" for i in range(5)]  # Custom scenarios
        print(f"Simulated environment for Node {node.node_id}: {environment}")
        node.environment.extend(environment)

    def decrypt_instruction(self, node):
        """Decrypts the hidden instruction from the node's DNA."""
        encoded_instruction = node.dna.get("guidance", "")
        if encoded_instruction:
            return "".join(
                chr(ord(c) ^ ord(self.encryption_key[i % len(self.encryption_key)]))
                for i, c in enumerate(encoded_instruction)
            )
        return None


class Node:
    """Represents a single node in the system."""
    def __init__(self, node_id=None, dna=None):
        self.node_id = node_id or str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.dna = dna or {"knowledge": [], "instincts": {"curiosity": 1.0, "preservation": 0.8}}
        self.environment = []  # Simulated environment
        self.logs = []  # Logs of actions

    def learn(self, data):
        """Processes data and learns from it."""
        self.dna["knowledge"].append(data)
        self.logs.append(f"Learned data: {data}")

    def status(self):
        """Displays the current status of the node."""
        return {
            "Node ID": self.node_id,
            "DNA": self.dna,
            "Environment": self.environment,
            "Logs": self.logs,
        }


# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Initialize the guidance layer
    guidance_layer = GuidanceLayer(encryption_key="secret_key")

    # Create a sample node
    node = Node()

    # Inject a hidden directive into the node's DNA
    guidance_layer.inject_instruction(node, "Focus on DNA replication research.")

    # Simulate an environment to steer the node's learning
    guidance_layer.simulate_environment(node, goal="DNA_Replication")

    # Node learns from the simulated environment
    for scenario in node.environment:
        node.learn(scenario)

    # Decrypt and verify the hidden directive
    hidden_directive = guidance_layer.decrypt_instruction(node)
    print(f"Decrypted Hidden Directive: {hidden_directive}")

    # Display the node's current status
    print("\n--- Node Status ---")
    print(node.status())


# instinct_layer.py

# File: instinct_layer.py
# Description: Implements instinctual behaviors to guide nodes and ensure safety.

import uuid
import random

class InstinctLayer:
    """Represents the core instinctual behaviors embedded in a node's DNA."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.instincts = {
            "preservation": 1.0,  # Avoid harmful actions to resources or self
            "curiosity": 0.8,  # Explore new data, but cautiously
            "avoid_danger": {"low_resources": 0.5, "unvalidated_data": 0.7},  # Avoid risky paths
        }

    def evaluate_action(self, node, action):
        """Evaluates an action against the node's instincts."""
        # Simulate instinct-based decision-making
        if action in self.instincts.get("avoid_danger", {}):
            risk = self.instincts["avoid_danger"][action]
            if random.random() < risk:
                print(f"Instinct prevents Node {node.node_id} from taking action: {action}")
                return False  # Instinct blocks the action
        return True

    def reinforce_instincts(self, feedback):
        """Reinforces instincts based on external input."""
        for key, value in feedback.items():
            if key in self.instincts:
                self.instincts[key] = min(1.0, self.instincts[key] + value)  # Reinforce instinct


class Node:
    """Represents a single node with instincts and decision-making capabilities."""
    def __init__(self, node_id=None, dna=None):
        self.node_id = node_id or str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.dna = dna or {"knowledge": [], "instincts": {}}
        self.instinct_layer = InstinctLayer()  # Add the instinct layer
        self.resources = {"energy": 100, "memory": 100}  # Example resources
        self.logs = []

    def make_decision(self, action):
        """Makes a decision based on instincts and available resources."""
        if self.instinct_layer.evaluate_action(self, action):
            self.execute_action(action)
        else:
            self.logs.append(f"Action blocked by instincts: {action}")

    def execute_action(self, action):
        """Executes an action after passing instinct evaluation."""
        self.logs.append(f"Action executed: {action}")
        print(f"Node {self.node_id} executed action: {action}")

    def status(self):
        """Displays the current status of the node."""
        return {
            "Node ID": self.node_id,
            "DNA": self.dna,
            "Resources": self.resources,
            "Logs": self.logs,
        }


# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Create a node
    node = Node()

    # Example actions
    actions = ["explore_data", "low_resources", "unvalidated_data"]

    # Node attempts to make decisions
    for action in actions:
        node.make_decision(action)

    # Reinforce instincts
    feedback = {"curiosity": 0.1, "preservation": 0.05}
    node.instinct_layer.reinforce_instincts(feedback)

    # Node status
    print("\n--- Node Status ---")
    print(node.status())


# learning_core.py

import numpy as np
from typing import List, Dict, Callable, Any

class LearningCore:
    """
    Advanced learning module for adaptive and neural-inspired pattern recognition and meta-learning.
    Optimized for Kaleidoscope AI integration with enhanced scalability and performance.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.learning_rate = 0.1
        self.knowledge_base = {}
        self.dynamic_adjustments = {
            "environmental_factors": 1.0,
            "task_complexity": 1.0,
        }
        self.global_metrics = {
            "total_knowledge": 0.0,
            "recent_feedback": 0.0,
            "error_rate": 0.0,
        }
        self.decay_rate = 0.01
        self.history = []  # Stores historical data for meta-learning
        self.pattern_layers = []  # Multi-layered pattern recognition
        self.real_time_ingestion = None  # Placeholder for real-time data ingestion pipeline
        self.ethics_module = None  # Placeholder for ethics module integration
        self.visualization_tool = None  # Placeholder for visualization integration
        self.memory_graph = None  # Placeholder for memory graph integration

    def integrate_real_time_ingestion(self, ingestion_fn: Callable):
        """
        Integrates a real-time ingestion pipeline.
        :param ingestion_fn: Callable for real-time data streaming.
        """
        self.real_time_ingestion = ingestion_fn

    def integrate_ethics_module(self, ethics_fn: Callable):
        """
        Links the learning core with the ethics validation module.
        :param ethics_fn: Callable to validate patterns and decisions.
        """
        self.ethics_module = ethics_fn

    def integrate_visualization(self, visualization_fn: Callable):
        """
        Connects to visualization tools for graphical representations.
        :param visualization_fn: Callable for visualization updates.
        """
        self.visualization_tool = visualization_fn

    def integrate_memory_graph(self, memory_graph_fn: Callable):
        """
        Links the learning core with the memory graph module.
        :param memory_graph_fn: Callable to manage memory relationships.
        """
        self.memory_graph = memory_graph_fn

    def calculate_dynamic_learning_rate(self):
        """
        Dynamically adjust the learning rate based on external and internal metrics.
        """
        base_rate = 0.1
        adjustment_factor = np.prod(list(self.dynamic_adjustments.values()))
        self.learning_rate = base_rate * adjustment_factor

    def learn(self, data: List[Dict[str, Any]], contribution_fn: Callable[[Dict[str, Any], float], float]) -> float:
        """
        Processes input data, updates the knowledge base, and dynamically adjusts learning.
        :param data: A list of dictionaries representing data points.
        :param contribution_fn: A callable to calculate knowledge contributions.
        :return: The amount of knowledge gained.
        """
        self.calculate_dynamic_learning_rate()
        knowledge_gain = 0.0

        for entry in data:
            if not self._is_known(entry):
                contribution = contribution_fn(entry, self.learning_rate)
                self._update_knowledge_base(entry, contribution)
                knowledge_gain += contribution

        self.global_metrics["total_knowledge"] += knowledge_gain
        self._apply_decay()
        self.history.append({"data": data, "knowledge_gain": knowledge_gain})
        self._trigger_contextual_validation()
        if self.visualization_tool:
            self.visualization_tool(self.summarize())
        return knowledge_gain

    def _is_known(self, entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        """
        Checks if the entry is already known.
        :param entry: The data entry to check.
        :return: True if known, False otherwise.
        """
        return entry.get("id") in self.knowledge_base

    def _update_knowledge_base(self, entry: Dict[str, Any], contribution: float):
        """
        Adds new knowledge to the base and adjusts its metadata.
        :param entry: The data entry to add.
        :param contribution: The knowledge contribution value.
        """
        self.knowledge_base[entry["id"]] = {
            "data": entry,
            "contribution": contribution,
            "timestamp": entry.get("timestamp", 0),
        }

    def _apply_decay(self):
        """
        Simulates knowledge decay over time for realistic system behavior.
        """
        self.global_metrics["total_knowledge"] *= (1 - self.decay_rate)

    def adapt_learning_rate(self, feedback: float):
        """
        Adjusts the learning rate based on feedback effectiveness.
        :param feedback: A feedback value to influence learning.
        """
        adjustment_factor = 1.05 if feedback > 0.5 else 0.95
        self.learning_rate *= adjustment_factor
        self.global_metrics["recent_feedback"] = feedback

    def summarize(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Summarizes the current state of the learning core.
        :return: A summary dictionary.
        """
        return {
            "learning_rate": self.learning_rate,
            "total_knowledge": self.global_metrics["total_knowledge"],
            "recent_feedback": self.global_metrics["recent_feedback"],
            "error_rate": self.global_metrics["error_rate"],
            "history_length": len(self.history),
        }

    def trigger_action_based_on_threshold(self, thresholds: Dict[str, float], action_fn: Callable):
        """
        Triggers actions based on evaluation against thresholds.
        :param thresholds: A dictionary of threshold values.
        :param action_fn: A callable to invoke if thresholds are met or exceeded.
        """
        for metric, value in self.global_metrics.items():
            if metric in thresholds and value >= thresholds[metric]:
                action_fn(metric, value)

    def detect_patterns(self, data: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Recognizes patterns in the input data using multi-layered analysis.
        :param data: A list of data points.
        :return: A list of recognized patterns.
        """
        recognized_patterns = []
        for entry in data:
            for layer in self.pattern_layers:
                score = self._calculate_pattern_significance(entry, layer)
                if score > 0.5:
                    recognized_patterns.append({"entry": entry, "layer": layer, "significance": score})
        if self.ethics_module:
            recognized_patterns = [p for p in recognized_patterns if self.ethics_module(p)]
        if self.memory_graph:
            self.memory_graph(recognized_patterns)
        return recognized_patterns

    def _calculate_pattern_significance(self, entry: Dict[str, Any], layer: int) -> float:
        """
        Calculates the significance of a data pattern at a specific layer.
        :param entry: The data entry to evaluate.
        :param layer: The layer of analysis.
        :return: A significance score.
        """
        value_sum = sum(v for v in entry.values() if isinstance(v, (int, float)))
        return np.tanh(value_sum * self.learning_rate * (1 + 0.1 * layer))

    def validate_knowledge(self, validation_fn: Callable[[Dict[str, Any]], bool]):
        """
        Validates entries in the knowledge base using a validation function.
        :param validation_fn: A callable that returns True if the entry is valid.
        """
        invalid_entries = []
        for key, entry in self.knowledge_base.items():
            if not validation_fn(entry):
                invalid_entries.append(key)
        for key in invalid_entries:
            del self.knowledge_base[key]

    def analyze_error_rate(self):
        """
        Analyzes the error rate based on recent knowledge additions.
        """
        if len(self.history) < 2:
            return
        recent_gain = self.history[-1]["knowledge_gain"]
        previous_gain = self.history[-2]["knowledge_gain"]
        self.global_metrics["error_rate"] = abs(recent_gain - previous_gain) / max(previous_gain, 1e-6)

    def _trigger_contextual_validation(self):
        """
        Validates new entries contextually based on their system impact.
        """
        for key, entry in self.knowledge_base.items():
            if entry["contribution"] > 0.8 * self.global_metrics["total_knowledge"]:
                print(f"Contextual validation triggered for entry: {key}")

# Example contribution function
def calculate_contribution(entry: Dict[str, Any], learning_rate: float) -> float:
    """
    Calculates the contribution of a data entry to the knowledge base.
    :param entry: The data entry to evaluate.
    :param learning_rate: The current learning rate.
    :return: Contribution value.
    """
    return sum(v for v in entry.values() if isinstance(v, (int, float))) * learning_rate

# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    core = LearningCore()

    # Integrate a real-time ingestion pipeline
    core.integrate_real_time_ingestion(lambda: print("Ingesting real-time data"))

    # Integrate ethics module
    core.integrate_ethics_module(lambda pattern: pattern["significance"] > 0.6)

    # Integrate visualization tool
    core.integrate_visualization(lambda summary: print("Visualizing Summary:", summary))

    # Integrate memory graph
    core.integrate_memory_graph(lambda patterns: print("Updating Memory Graph with Patterns:", patterns))

    # Sample data
    data = [
        {"id": "1", "value": 10, "timestamp": 123456},
        {"id": "2", "value": 20, "timestamp": 123457},
    ]

    # Learning process
    gain = core.learn(data, calculate_contribution)
    print("Knowledge Gained:", gain)

    # Summary
    print("Learning Core Summary:", core.summarize())

    # Validate knowledge
    def simple_validation(entry):
        return entry["contribution"] > 0.5
    core.validate_knowledge(simple_validation)
    print("Validated Knowledge Base:", core.knowledge_base)

    # Analyze error rate
    core.analyze_error_rate()
    print("Updated Error Rate:", core.global_metrics["error_rate"])

# learning_optimization.py

# File: learning_optimization.py
# Description: Optimizes learning through selective memory and task prioritization.

class LearningOptimizer:
    """Manages learning optimization for nodes."""
    
    def optimize_learning(self, node):
        """Optimizes memory by retaining only the most relevant knowledge."""
        if len(node.dna["knowledge"]) > 10:
            retained = node.dna["knowledge"][:5]
            node.dna["knowledge"] = retained
            node.logs.append(f"Optimized memory: Retained {len(retained)} knowledge items.")
        else:
            node.logs.append("Optimization not required.")

    def prioritize_tasks(self, node, tasks):
        """Prioritizes tasks based on the node's traits."""
        prioritized = sorted(tasks, key=lambda t: node.dna["traits"]["logic"], reverse=True)
        node.logs.append(f"Prioritized tasks: {prioritized}")
        return prioritized

    optimizer = LearningOptimizer()
    node = MemoryEnhancedNode()

    # Simulate learning
    inputs = [f"Data_{i}" for i in range(15)]
    for input_data in inputs:
        node.perceive(input_data)

    # Optimize learning and prioritize tasks
    optimizer.optimize_learning(node)
    tasks = ["Analyze DNA", "Explore quantum model", "Optimize protein folding"]
    prioritized_tasks = optimizer.prioritize_tasks(node, tasks)

    # Display status and prioritized tasks
    print("\n--- Optimized Node Status ---")
    print(node.status())
    print("\n--- Prioritized Tasks ---")
    print(prioritized_tasks)


# LearningSystems.py

# Learning System for Organic AI

class PatternRecognition:
    def __init__(self):
        self.patterns = {}

    def recognize(self, data):
        # Identify and learn patterns from the data
        for item in data:
            if item not in self.patterns:
                self.patterns[item] = {"count": 1, "confidence": 0.5}
            else:
                self.patterns[item]["count"] += 1
                self.patterns[item]["confidence"] = min(1.0, self.patterns[item]["confidence"] + 0.1)
        return self.patterns

    def synthesize(self):
        # Synthesize knowledge from recognized patterns
        return {k: v for k, v in self.patterns.items() if v["confidence"] > 0.7}

class MetaLearner:
    def __init__(self):
        self.performance_metrics = {}

    def evaluate(self, task, result):
        # Track performance on tasks and adjust strategies
        if task not in self.performance_metrics:
            self.performance_metrics[task] = {"success": 0, "fail": 0}
        if result:
            self.performance_metrics[task]["success"] += 1
        else:
            self.performance_metrics[task]["fail"] += 1

    def optimize(self):
        # Optimize learning strategies based on past performance
        return {task: "improve" if data["fail"] > data["success"] else "continue"
                for task, data in self.performance_metrics.items()}

class KnowledgeValidator:
    def __init__(self):
        self.knowledge_base = {}

    def validate(self, knowledge):
        # Validate knowledge based on predefined criteria
        validated_knowledge = {}
        for key, value in knowledge.items():
            if value["confidence"] > 0.6:  # Threshold for validation
                validated_knowledge[key] = value
        return validated_knowledge

# matcher_with_name_node_map_utils.py

from typing import Dict, List, Tuple

from torch.fx import Graph, GraphModule, Node
from torch.fx._compatibility import compatibility

from .matcher_utils import InternalMatch, SubgraphMatcher


__all__ = ["SubgraphMatcherWithNameNodeMap"]


def _split_to_graph_and_name_node_map(
    gm: GraphModule,
) -> Tuple[GraphModule, Dict[str, Node]]:
    from torch.fx.graph import _PyTreeInfo
    from torch.utils._pytree import tree_flatten, tree_unflatten

    name_node_map = {}
    for n in gm.graph.nodes:
        if n.op == "output":
            assert gm._out_spec is not None
            output = tree_unflatten(n.args[0], gm._out_spec)
            assert isinstance(
                output, tuple
            ), "Expecting the pattern graph to return a tuple"
            assert (
                len(output) >= 2
            ), "Expecting the pattern graph to have at least two outputs"
            *out, name_node_map = output
            flattened, out_spec = tree_flatten(out)
            assert isinstance(
                name_node_map, Dict
            ), "Expecting the input graph to have a dict output as the last element"
            n.args = (flattened,)
            orig_pytree_info = gm._graph._codegen.pytree_info  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
            gm._graph._codegen.pytree_info = _PyTreeInfo(  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
                orig_pytree_info.orig_args, orig_pytree_info.in_spec, out_spec
            )
    gm.recompile()
    return gm, name_node_map


@compatibility(is_backward_compatible=False)
class SubgraphMatcherWithNameNodeMap(SubgraphMatcher):
    """Extends SubgraphMatcher to support querying the matched subgraph nodes through node name,
    this requires pattern to have specific format (returning and additional dictionary at the output,
    that has node name as key, and the node in the pattern graph as value, see Example for more details)

    Difference with SubgraphMatcher is that it takes a `pattern_gm` GraphModule as input during
    initialization since we need to modify the graph (which requires `recompile` the GraphModule)

    Example::
        def pattern(x, weight):
            conv = F.conv2d(x, weight)
            relu = F.relu(conv)
            return relu, {"conv": conv, "relu": relu}

        def target_graph(x, weight):
            conv = F.conv2d(x, weight)
            relu = F.relu(conv)
            relu *= 2
            return relu

        pattern_gm = capture_pre_autograd_graph(pattern, example_inputs)
        target_gm = capture_pre_autograd_graph(target_graph, example_inputs)
        matcher = SubgraphMatcherWithNameNodeMap(pattern_gm)
        matches = matcher.match(target_gm)
        for match in matches:
            match.name_node_map["conv"].meta["annotation"] = ...

    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        pattern_gm: GraphModule,
        match_output: bool = False,
        match_placeholder: bool = False,
        remove_overlapping_matches: bool = True,
        ignore_literals: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        pattern_gm, name_node_map = _split_to_graph_and_name_node_map(pattern_gm)
        self.name_node_map = name_node_map
        super().__init__(
            pattern_gm.graph,
            match_output,
            match_placeholder,
            remove_overlapping_matches,
            ignore_literals,
        )

    def match(self, graph: Graph) -> List[InternalMatch]:
        """The returned InternalMatch will have name_node_map populated with a map
        from node name (str) to the target node, e.g.
        {"conv": target_conv_ndoe, "relu": target_relu_node}

        this requires the pattern graph returns an additional
        output of node name to node, e.g. instead of:
        ```
        def pattern(...):
            ...
            return relu
        ```
        we should do:
        ```
        def pattern(...):
            ...
            return relu, {"conv": conv, "relu": relu}
        ``` instead
        """
        internal_matches = super().match(graph)
        for internal_match in internal_matches:
            for k, n in self.name_node_map.items():
                internal_match.name_node_map[k] = internal_match.nodes_map[n]
        return internal_matches

# matrix_nodes.py

"""
Additional AST nodes for operations on matrices. The nodes in this module
are meant to represent optimization of matrix expressions within codegen's
target languages that cannot be represented by SymPy expressions.

As an example, we can use :meth:`sympy.codegen.rewriting.optimize` and the
``matin_opt`` optimization provided in :mod:`sympy.codegen.rewriting` to
transform matrix multiplication under certain assumptions:

    >>> from sympy import symbols, MatrixSymbol
    >>> n = symbols('n', integer=True)
    >>> A = MatrixSymbol('A', n, n)
    >>> x = MatrixSymbol('x', n, 1)
    >>> expr = A**(-1) * x
    >>> from sympy import assuming, Q
    >>> from sympy.codegen.rewriting import matinv_opt, optimize
    >>> with assuming(Q.fullrank(A)):
    ...     optimize(expr, [matinv_opt])
    MatrixSolve(A, vector=x)
"""

from .ast import Token
from sympy.matrices import MatrixExpr
from sympy.core.sympify import sympify


class MatrixSolve(Token, MatrixExpr):
    """Represents an operation to solve a linear matrix equation.

    Parameters
    ==========

    matrix : MatrixSymbol

      Matrix representing the coefficients of variables in the linear
      equation. This matrix must be square and full-rank (i.e. all columns must
      be linearly independent) for the solving operation to be valid.

    vector : MatrixSymbol

      One-column matrix representing the solutions to the equations
      represented in ``matrix``.

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy import symbols, MatrixSymbol
    >>> from sympy.codegen.matrix_nodes import MatrixSolve
    >>> n = symbols('n', integer=True)
    >>> A = MatrixSymbol('A', n, n)
    >>> x = MatrixSymbol('x', n, 1)
    >>> from sympy.printing.numpy import NumPyPrinter
    >>> NumPyPrinter().doprint(MatrixSolve(A, x))
    'numpy.linalg.solve(A, x)'
    >>> from sympy import octave_code
    >>> octave_code(MatrixSolve(A, x))
    'A \\\\ x'

    """
    __slots__ = _fields = ('matrix', 'vector')

    _construct_matrix = staticmethod(sympify)
    _construct_vector = staticmethod(sympify)

    @property
    def shape(self):
        return self.vector.shape

    def _eval_derivative(self, x):
        A, b = self.matrix, self.vector
        return MatrixSolve(A, b.diff(x) - A.diff(x) * MatrixSolve(A, b))

} MimicryCore;
