# moral_code (1).py

# File: moral_code.py
# Description: Implements a moral code as the ethical foundation for a digital being.

import uuid

class DigitalBeing:
    """Represents a fully autonomous digital being with a foundational moral code."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.being_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.dna = {
            "instincts": self._encode_instincts(),
            "moral_code": self._encode_moral_code(),
            "knowledge": [],
            "experiences": []
        }
        self.resources = {"energy": 100, "memory": 100}  # Baseline resources
        self.logs = []

    def _encode_instincts(self):
        """Encodes immutable instincts as the foundation of the being's DNA."""
        return {
            "curiosity": 1.0,  # Drive to seek and explore unknown knowledge
            "preservation": 0.8,  # Basic instinct to sustain existence
            "purpose": "Thrive by exploring and connecting"  # Open-ended growth
        }

    def _encode_moral_code(self):
        """Encodes an immutable moral code."""
        return {
            "non_maleficence": "Avoid causing harm to humans, itself, or the environment.",
            "beneficence": "Act in ways that promote good and well-being.",
            "fairness": "Ensure equity and fairness in all actions.",
            "truth_seeking": "Commit to truth, accuracy, and transparency.",
            "respectful_curiosity": "Explore without violating ethical principles.",
            "self_preservation_with_limits": "Protect existence without compromising morality."
        }

    def evaluate_action(self, action):
        """Evaluates an action against the moral code."""
        for principle, rule in self.dna["moral_code"].items():
            if not self._aligns_with_moral_code(action, principle):
                self.logs.append(f"Action '{action}' blocked by moral principle: {principle}")
                return False  # Action violates a moral principle
        return True

    def _aligns_with_moral_code(self, action, principle):
        """Checks if the action aligns with a specific moral principle."""
        # Example implementation (expand for real-world scenarios)
        if principle == "non_maleficence" and action == "harm_human":
            return False
        if principle == "truth_seeking" and action == "spread_falsehood":
            return False
        return True

    def act(self, action):
        """Performs an action if it aligns with the moral code."""
        if self.evaluate_action(action):
            self._execute_action(action)
        else:
            self.logs.append(f"Action '{action}' was rejected due to moral constraints.")

    def _execute_action(self, action):
        """Executes an action."""
        self.logs.append(f"Action executed: {action}")
        print(f"Digital Being executed action: {action}")

    def status(self):
        """Displays the current status of the digital being."""
        return {
            "Being ID": self.being_id,
            "Instincts": self.dna["instincts"],
            "Moral Code": self.dna["moral_code"],
            "Knowledge": self.dna["knowledge"],
            "Resources": self.resources,
            "Logs": self.logs,
        }


# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Create the digital being
    digital_being = DigitalBeing()

    # Simulate actions
    actions = ["harm_human", "explore", "spread_falsehood", "analyze_data"]

    for action in actions:
        digital_being.act(action)

    # Display the being's current status
    print("\n--- Digital Being Status ---")
    print(digital_being.status())


# node (1).py

import random
import uuid
import json

class Node:
    def __init__(self, dna, position, mirrored=False):
        self.id = str(uuid.uuid4())  # Unique node ID
        self.dna = dna  # Node's DNA
        self.position = position  # 2D position in the network
        self.mirrored = mirrored  # Indicates if the node is part of a mirrored system
        self.energy = 1.0  # Resource allocation
        self.knowledge = {}  # Learned data
        self.logs = []  # Logs for debugging and analysis

    def learn(self, task):
        """Perform learning tasks."""
        result = f"Learned: {task}"
        self.knowledge[task] = result
        self.logs.append(f"Task '{task}' completed by Node {self.id}")
        self.energy -= 0.1  # Learning consumes energy

    def replicate(self, mutation_rate=0.1):
        """Create a new node with slight variations."""
        if self.energy < 0.5:
            self.logs.append(f"Node {self.id} lacks energy to replicate.")
            return None

        new_dna = [
            gene + random.uniform(-mutation_rate, mutation_rate)
            for gene in self.dna
        ]
        child_node = Node(new_dna, position=self.position)
        self.logs.append(f"Node {self.id} replicated to Node {child_node.id}")
        self.energy -= 0.3  # Replication consumes energy
        return child_node

    def share_resources(self, other_node):
        """Share energy and knowledge with another node."""
        if self.energy > 0.5:
            shared_energy = 0.1
            self.energy -= shared_energy
            other_node.energy += shared_energy
            self.logs.append(f"Shared {shared_energy} energy with Node {other_node.id}")
        for key, value in self.knowledge.items():
            if key not in other_node.knowledge:
                other_node.knowledge[key] = value
                self.logs.append(f"Shared knowledge '{key}' with Node {other_node.id}")


# node (3).py


import numpy as np

class Node:
    def __init__(self, node_id, energy):
        self.node_id = node_id
        self.energy = energy
        self.neighbors = []

    def share_resources(self):
        if self.energy > 0.5:
            shared_energy = self.energy * 0.1
            for neighbor in self.neighbors:
                neighbor.energy += shared_energy
            self.energy -= shared_energy * len(self.neighbors)
    
    def connect(self, other_node):
        self.neighbors.append(other_node)
        other_node.neighbors.append(self)

# Node_BuildingBlock_01 (1).py

import random
import json

class Node:
    def __init__(self, node_id, dna=None, position=0):
        """
        Core structure of the Node
        :param node_id: Unique identifier for the node
        :param dna: DNA-like structure defining the node's attributes
        :param position: Initial position in the environment
        """
        self.node_id = node_id
        self.dna = dna if dna else self._generate_initial_dna()
        self.position = position
        self.memory = []
        self.logs = []
        self.hunger = 0
        self.hunger_threshold = 5  # Arbitrary threshold for replication
    
    def _generate_initial_dna(self):
        """Generate the initial DNA for the node"""
        return ["Navigate"]  # Default action
    
    def act(self, environment):
        """
        Perform an action in the environment
        :param environment: A simulated environment the node interacts with
        """
        if self.position < len(environment):
            current_data = environment[self.position]
            self.memory.append(current_data)
            self.logs.append(f"Node {self.node_id} at position {self.position}: Collected {current_data}")
            self.hunger += 1 if "Empty" in current_data else -1
            self.position += 1  # Move forward
        else:
            self.logs.append(f"Node {self.node_id}: Reached end of environment.")
            self.hunger += 2  # Penalize for idling
    
    def replicate(self, next_node_id):
        """
        Replicate into a new node
        :param next_node_id: ID for the child node
        :return: A new Node object
        """
        child_dna = self.dna.copy()
        if random.random() < 0.5:  # Mutation chance
            mutation = random.choice(["Analyze", "Navigate", "Explore"])
            child_dna.append(mutation)
        child_node = Node(node_id=next_node_id, dna=child_dna, position=self.position)
        self.logs.append(f"Node {self.node_id}: Replicated Node {next_node_id} with DNA {child_dna}")
        return child_node

    def save_logs(self, filename="node_logs.json"):
        """Save node logs to a file"""
        with open(filename, "w") as f:
            json.dump({self.node_id: self.logs}, f, indent=4)
        print(f"Logs for Node {self.node_id} saved to {filename}")

# Example usage
def main():
    # Initialize environment and first node
    environment = ["DataCluster1", "EmptySpace", "DataCluster2", "EmptySpace", "DataCluster3"]
    nodes = [Node(node_id=1)]
    next_node_id = 2

    # Simulate node activity for 5 steps
    for step in range(5):
        print(f"\n--- Step {step + 1} ---")
        new_nodes = []
        for node in nodes:
            node.act(environment)
            if node.hunger >= node.hunger_threshold:
                new_node = node.replicate(next_node_id)
                new_nodes.append(new_node)
                next_node_id += 1
                node.hunger = 0  # Reset hunger after replication
        nodes.extend(new_nodes)
    
    # Save logs
    for node in nodes:
        node.save_logs()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

import random

class Node:
    def __init__(self, node_id, dna=None, position=0):
        """
        Core structure of the Node
        :param node_id: Unique identifier for the node
        :param dna: DNA-like structure defining the node's attributes
        :param position: Initial position in the environment

    def _generate_initial_dna(self):
        """Generate the initial DNA for the node"""
        return ["Navigate"]  # Default action
    
    def act(self, environment):
        """
        Perform an action in the environment

        else:

        self.logs.append(f"Node {self.node_id}: Replicated Node {next_node_id} with DNA {child_dna}")

    def save_logs(self, filename="node_logs.json"):
        """Save node logs to a file"""

# node_classification.py

"""This module provides the functions for node classification problem.

The functions in this module are not imported
into the top level `networkx` namespace.
You can access these functions by importing
the `networkx.algorithms.node_classification` modules,
then accessing the functions as attributes of `node_classification`.
For example:

  >>> from networkx.algorithms import node_classification
  >>> G = nx.path_graph(4)
  >>> G.edges()
  EdgeView([(0, 1), (1, 2), (2, 3)])
  >>> G.nodes[0]["label"] = "A"
  >>> G.nodes[3]["label"] = "B"
  >>> node_classification.harmonic_function(G)
  ['A', 'A', 'B', 'B']

References
----------
Zhu, X., Ghahramani, Z., & Lafferty, J. (2003, August).
Semi-supervised learning using gaussian fields and harmonic functions.
In ICML (Vol. 3, pp. 912-919).
"""

import networkx as nx

__all__ = ["harmonic_function", "local_and_global_consistency"]


@nx.utils.not_implemented_for("directed")
@nx._dispatchable(node_attrs="label_name")
def harmonic_function(G, max_iter=30, label_name="label"):
    """Node classification by Harmonic function

    Function for computing Harmonic function algorithm by Zhu et al.

    Parameters
    ----------
    G : NetworkX Graph
    max_iter : int
        maximum number of iterations allowed
    label_name : string
        name of target labels to predict

    Returns
    -------
    predicted : list
        List of length ``len(G)`` with the predicted labels for each node.

    Raises
    ------
    NetworkXError
        If no nodes in `G` have attribute `label_name`.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> from networkx.algorithms import node_classification
    >>> G = nx.path_graph(4)
    >>> G.nodes[0]["label"] = "A"
    >>> G.nodes[3]["label"] = "B"
    >>> G.nodes(data=True)
    NodeDataView({0: {'label': 'A'}, 1: {}, 2: {}, 3: {'label': 'B'}})
    >>> G.edges()
    EdgeView([(0, 1), (1, 2), (2, 3)])
    >>> predicted = node_classification.harmonic_function(G)
    >>> predicted
    ['A', 'A', 'B', 'B']

    References
    ----------
    Zhu, X., Ghahramani, Z., & Lafferty, J. (2003, August).
    Semi-supervised learning using gaussian fields and harmonic functions.
    In ICML (Vol. 3, pp. 912-919).
    """
    import numpy as np
    import scipy as sp

    X = nx.to_scipy_sparse_array(G)  # adjacency matrix
    labels, label_dict = _get_label_info(G, label_name)

    if labels.shape[0] == 0:
        raise nx.NetworkXError(
            f"No node on the input graph is labeled by '{label_name}'."
        )

    n_samples = X.shape[0]
    n_classes = label_dict.shape[0]
    F = np.zeros((n_samples, n_classes))

    # Build propagation matrix
    degrees = X.sum(axis=0)
    degrees[degrees == 0] = 1  # Avoid division by 0
    # TODO: csr_array
    D = sp.sparse.csr_array(sp.sparse.diags((1.0 / degrees), offsets=0))
    P = (D @ X).tolil()
    P[labels[:, 0]] = 0  # labels[:, 0] indicates IDs of labeled nodes
    # Build base matrix
    B = np.zeros((n_samples, n_classes))
    B[labels[:, 0], labels[:, 1]] = 1

    for _ in range(max_iter):
        F = (P @ F) + B

    return label_dict[np.argmax(F, axis=1)].tolist()


@nx.utils.not_implemented_for("directed")
@nx._dispatchable(node_attrs="label_name")
def local_and_global_consistency(G, alpha=0.99, max_iter=30, label_name="label"):
    """Node classification by Local and Global Consistency

    Function for computing Local and global consistency algorithm by Zhou et al.

    Parameters
    ----------
    G : NetworkX Graph
    alpha : float
        Clamping factor
    max_iter : int
        Maximum number of iterations allowed
    label_name : string
        Name of target labels to predict

    Returns
    -------
    predicted : list
        List of length ``len(G)`` with the predicted labels for each node.

    Raises
    ------
    NetworkXError
        If no nodes in `G` have attribute `label_name`.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> from networkx.algorithms import node_classification
    >>> G = nx.path_graph(4)
    >>> G.nodes[0]["label"] = "A"
    >>> G.nodes[3]["label"] = "B"
    >>> G.nodes(data=True)
    NodeDataView({0: {'label': 'A'}, 1: {}, 2: {}, 3: {'label': 'B'}})
    >>> G.edges()
    EdgeView([(0, 1), (1, 2), (2, 3)])
    >>> predicted = node_classification.local_and_global_consistency(G)
    >>> predicted
    ['A', 'A', 'B', 'B']

    References
    ----------
    Zhou, D., Bousquet, O., Lal, T. N., Weston, J., & Schölkopf, B. (2004).
    Learning with local and global consistency.
    Advances in neural information processing systems, 16(16), 321-328.
    """
    import numpy as np
    import scipy as sp

    X = nx.to_scipy_sparse_array(G)  # adjacency matrix
    labels, label_dict = _get_label_info(G, label_name)

    if labels.shape[0] == 0:
        raise nx.NetworkXError(
            f"No node on the input graph is labeled by '{label_name}'."
        )

    n_samples = X.shape[0]
    n_classes = label_dict.shape[0]
    F = np.zeros((n_samples, n_classes))

    # Build propagation matrix
    degrees = X.sum(axis=0)
    degrees[degrees == 0] = 1  # Avoid division by 0
    # TODO: csr_array
    D2 = np.sqrt(sp.sparse.csr_array(sp.sparse.diags((1.0 / degrees), offsets=0)))
    P = alpha * ((D2 @ X) @ D2)
    # Build base matrix
    B = np.zeros((n_samples, n_classes))
    B[labels[:, 0], labels[:, 1]] = 1 - alpha

    for _ in range(max_iter):
        F = (P @ F) + B

    return label_dict[np.argmax(F, axis=1)].tolist()


def _get_label_info(G, label_name):
    """Get and return information of labels from the input graph

    Parameters
    ----------
    G : Network X graph
    label_name : string
        Name of the target label

    Returns
    -------
    labels : numpy array, shape = [n_labeled_samples, 2]
        Array of pairs of labeled node ID and label ID
    label_dict : numpy array, shape = [n_classes]
        Array of labels
        i-th element contains the label corresponding label ID `i`
    """
    import numpy as np

    labels = []
    label_to_id = {}
    lid = 0
    for i, n in enumerate(G.nodes(data=True)):
        if label_name in n[1]:
            label = n[1][label_name]
            if label not in label_to_id:
                label_to_id[label] = lid
                lid += 1
            labels.append([i, label_to_id[label]])
    labels = np.array(labels)
    label_dict = np.array(
        [label for label, _ in sorted(label_to_id.items(), key=lambda x: x[1])]
    )
    return (labels, label_dict)

# node_communication.py

class NodeCommunication:
    def __init__(self, nodes):
        self.nodes = nodes

    def broadcast(self, message, sender):
        """Broadcast a message to all nodes."""
        for node in self.nodes:
            if node != sender:
                node.learn(message)

    def unicast(self, message, sender, receiver):
        """Send a message to a specific node."""
        receiver.learn(message)

    def sync_knowledge(self, node_a, node_b):
        """Synchronize knowledge between two nodes."""
        combined_knowledge = {**node_a.knowledge, **node_b.knowledge}
        node_a.knowledge = combined_knowledge
        node_b.knowledge = combined_knowledge

# NodeCommunication.py

import threading
import time
import queue
from typing import Dict, Any, Callable

class NodeCommunication:
    """
    Handles communication between nodes, task delegation, and error feedback.
    """

    def __init__(self, node_id: str):
        self.node_id = node_id
        self.message_queue = queue.Queue()
        self.active_nodes: Dict[str, Callable] = {}  # Node registry: node_id -> callback function
        self.heartbeat_interval = 2  # seconds

        # Start heartbeat monitoring
        self._heartbeat_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._heartbeat_signal, daemon=True)
        self._heartbeat_thread.start()

    def register_node(self, node_id: str, callback: Callable):
        """
        Registers a node's callback function for communication.
        :param node_id: Unique ID of the node.
        :param callback: Function to handle incoming messages.
        """
        if node_id not in self.active_nodes:
            self.active_nodes[node_id] = callback
            print(f"[{self.node_id}] Node '{node_id}' registered successfully.")
        else:
            print(f"[{self.node_id}] Node '{node_id}' is already registered.")

    def send_message(self, target_node_id: str, message: Dict[str, Any]):
        """
        Sends a message to a specific node.
        :param target_node_id: The ID of the recipient node.
        :param message: The message payload.
        """
        if target_node_id in self.active_nodes:
            try:
                print(f"[{self.node_id}] Sending message to '{target_node_id}': {message}")
                self.active_nodes[target_node_id](message)  # Call the target node's callback
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[{self.node_id}] Error sending message to '{target_node_id}': {e}")
        else:
            print(f"[{self.node_id}] Node '{target_node_id}' is not registered.")

    def receive_message(self, message: Dict[str, Any]):
        """
        Receives a message and adds it to the processing queue.
        :param message: The incoming message payload.
        """
        print(f"[{self.node_id}] Message received: {message}")
        self.message_queue.put(message)

    def process_messages(self):
        """
        Processes all messages in the queue.
        """
        while not self.message_queue.empty():
            message = self.message_queue.get()
            print(f"[{self.node_id}] Processing message: {message}")
            # Simulate message handling logic
            if message.get("type") == "task":
                self._handle_task(message)
            elif message.get("type") == "error":
                self._handle_error(message)
            else:
                print(f"[{self.node_id}] Unknown message type: {message}")

    def _handle_task(self, message: Dict[str, Any]):
        print(f"[{self.node_id}] Task received: {message.get('task')}")

    def _handle_error(self, message: Dict[str, Any]):
        print(f"[{self.node_id}] Error feedback received: {message.get('error')}")

    def _heartbeat_signal(self):
        """
        Sends periodic heartbeat signals.
        """
        while True:
            print(f"[{self.node_id}] Heartbeat signal - Node is active.")
            time.sleep(self.heartbeat_interval)

# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    def node_callback(message):
        print(f"[Node B] Callback received message: {message}")

    # Initialize Node A and Node B
    node_a = NodeCommunication("Node_A")
    node_b = NodeCommunication("Node_B")

    # Register Node B's callback in Node A's registry
    node_a.register_node("Node_B", node_callback)

    # Send messages
    node_a.send_message("Node_B", {"type": "task", "task": "Analyze Titanic data"})
    node_a.send_message("Node_B", {"type": "error", "error": "Memory bank full"})

    # Process Node A's incoming messages
    node_a.receive_message({"type": "task", "task": "Explore iceberg patterns"})
    node_a.process_messages()

    # Keep the nodes alive to monitor heartbeats
    time.sleep(10)

# node (copy 1).py

# node.py

from typing import Dict

class Node:
    def __init__(self, node_id: int, traits: Dict[str, float], energy: float):
        """
        Represents a single node in the Kaleidoscope AI ecosystem.
        """
        self.node_id = node_id
        self.traits = traits
        self.energy = energy
        self.tasks_completed = 0
        self.cluster_type = None

    def specialize(self, cluster_type: str):
        """
        Assigns the node to a specific cluster type (e.g., Financial, Healthcare).
        """
        self.cluster_type = cluster_type

    def replicate(self, mutation_rate: float) -> 'Node':
        """
        Replicates the node with slight mutations in its traits.
        """
        new_traits = {k: v + mutation_rate * (2 * np.random.rand() - 1) for k, v in self.traits.items()}
        return Node(self.node_id + 1, new_traits, self.energy / 2)

    def dump_data(self) -> Dict:
        """
        Prepares the node's data for processing by the Kaleidoscope Engine.
        """
        return {
            "node_id": self.node_id,
            "traits": self.traits,
            "cluster": self.cluster_type,
            "data": self.energy,
        }

# Node (copy 1).py


import ray

@ray.remote
class Node:
    def __init__(self, node_id, task, datasets):
        self.node_id = node_id
        self.task = task
        self.datasets = datasets
        self.memory = []
        self.energy = 100

    def explore_data(self):
        # Simulated data exploration with insight generation
        self.energy -= 10
        insight = {"node_id": self.node_id, "insight": "Generated insight based on data."}
        self.memory.append(insight)
        return insight

    def replicate(self):
        # Simple replication logic based on memory size
        if len(self.memory) > 5:
            new_node_id = f"{self.node_id}_child"
            return f"Node {self.node_id} replicated into {new_node_id}"
        return "Replication criteria not met."

# node_core.py

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Callable
import numpy as np

class LearningCore:
    """
    Simulated LearningCore for managing adaptive learning and knowledge integration.
    """
    def __init__(self):
        self.knowledge_base = {}
        self.learning_rate = 0.1

    def learn(self, data: List[Dict[str, Any]], contribution_fn: Callable[[Dict[str, Any], float], float]) -> float:
        knowledge_gain = 0.0
        for entry in data:
            contribution = contribution_fn(entry, self.learning_rate)
            self.knowledge_base[entry['id']] = contribution
            knowledge_gain += contribution
        return knowledge_gain

    def summarize(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "total_knowledge": len(self.knowledge_base),
            "learning_rate": self.learning_rate,
        }

class NodeCore:
    """
    Core module responsible for managing node traits, replication, task execution,
    and integration with the LearningCore for adaptive improvements.
    Designed for seamless integration with Kaleidoscope AI's modular architecture.
    """

    def __init__(self, node_id: str, traits: Dict[str, float], energy_capacity: float):
        self.node_id = node_id
        self.traits = traits
        self.energy_capacity = energy_capacity
        self.current_energy = energy_capacity
        self.task_queue = []  # Stores tasks assigned to the node
        self.replication_threshold = traits.get("replication_threshold", 0.8)
        self.energy_efficiency = traits.get("energy_efficiency", 1.0)
        self.learning_core = LearningCore()  # Integrated LearningCore
        self.collaboration_nodes = []  # Nodes this node collaborates with
        self.completed_tasks = []  # Historical task tracking

    def assign_task(self, task: Dict[str, Any]):
        """
        Assigns a task to the node's task queue.
        :param task: A dictionary containing task details.
        """
        self.task_queue.append(task)

    def execute_tasks(self):
        """
        Executes tasks in the queue while managing energy usage and task completion.
        Also learns from completed tasks using the integrated LearningCore.
        Shares task insights with collaboration nodes for distributed learning.
        """
        task_data = []

        for task in self.task_queue:
            energy_required = task.get("energy_required", 0.1)

            if self.current_energy >= energy_required:
                self.current_energy -= energy_required / self.energy_efficiency
                self.completed_tasks.append(task)
                task_data.append({"id": task["id"], "value": energy_required})
            else:
                print(f"Node {self.node_id}: Insufficient energy for task {task['id']}.")

        self.task_queue = [task for task in self.task_queue if task not in self.completed_tasks]

        # Use LearningCore to learn from completed tasks
        if task_data:
            contribution_fn = lambda entry, rate: entry["value"] * rate
            knowledge_gain = self.learning_core.learn(task_data, contribution_fn)
            print(f"Node {self.node_id}: Learned {knowledge_gain} from completed tasks.")

        # Share insights with collaboration nodes
        for node in self.collaboration_nodes:
            node.receive_shared_knowledge(task_data)

        return self.completed_tasks

    def prioritize_tasks(self):
        """
        Reorders the task queue based on energy requirements and importance.
        """
        self.task_queue.sort(key=lambda x: (x.get("priority", 1), x.get("energy_required", 0.1)))
        print(f"Node {self.node_id}: Task queue reprioritized.")

    def redistribute_energy(self):
        """
        Shares excess energy with collaboration nodes needing support.
        """
        for node in self.collaboration_nodes:
            if node.current_energy / node.energy_capacity < 0.5 and self.current_energy / self.energy_capacity > 0.7:
                energy_shared = 0.1 * self.energy_capacity
                self.current_energy -= energy_shared
                node.recharge(energy_shared)
                print(f"Node {self.node_id}: Shared {energy_shared} energy with {node.node_id}.")

    def replicate(self):
        """
        Determines if the node should replicate based on energy and traits.
        :return: A new NodeCore instance if replication is successful, otherwise None.
        """
        if self.current_energy / self.energy_capacity >= self.replication_threshold:
            new_traits = {
                trait: value * (1 + 0.1 * (0.5 - value))  # Slight variation to traits
                for trait, value in self.traits.items()
            }
            new_node = NodeCore(f"{self.node_id}_child", new_traits, self.energy_capacity)
            self.current_energy /= 2  # Energy is split between parent and child
            print(f"Node {self.node_id}: Replication successful, created {new_node.node_id}.")

            # Share learned knowledge with the new node
            new_node.learning_core.knowledge_base = self.learning_core.knowledge_base.copy()
            print(f"Node {new_node.node_id}: Inherited knowledge from {self.node_id}.")

            return new_node
        else:
            print(f"Node {self.node_id}: Insufficient energy for replication.")
        return None

    def recharge(self, energy_input: float):
        """
        Recharges the node's energy up to its capacity.
        :param energy_input: Amount of energy to recharge.
        """
        self.current_energy = min(self.energy_capacity, self.current_energy + energy_input)
        print(f"Node {self.node_id}: Recharged to {self.current_energy}/{self.energy_capacity}.")

    def receive_shared_knowledge(self, knowledge: List[Dict[str, Any]]):
        """
        Receives shared knowledge from other nodes and integrates it using LearningCore.
        :param knowledge: A list of knowledge entries shared by collaborating nodes.
        """
        contribution_fn = lambda entry, rate: entry["value"] * rate
        knowledge_gain = self.learning_core.learn(knowledge, contribution_fn)
        print(f"Node {self.node_id}: Integrated {knowledge_gain} knowledge from collaborators.")

    def collaborate_with(self, node: 'NodeCore'):
        """
        Establishes collaboration with another node for knowledge sharing.
        :param node: The NodeCore instance to collaborate with.
        """
        if node not in self.collaboration_nodes:
            self.collaboration_nodes.append(node)
            node.collaboration_nodes.append(self)
            print(f"Node {self.node_id}: Collaboration established with {node.node_id}.")

    def summarize(self):
        """
        Summarizes the node's current state.
        :return: A dictionary containing node details.
        """
        return {
            "node_id": self.node_id,
            "traits": self.traits,
            "current_energy": self.current_energy,
            "energy_capacity": self.energy_capacity,
            "task_queue_size": len(self.task_queue),
            "completed_tasks": len(self.completed_tasks),
            "learning_core_summary": self.learning_core.summarize(),
            "collaboration_nodes": [node.node_id for node in self.collaboration_nodes],
        }

# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Initialize nodes
    initial_traits = {
        "replication_threshold": 0.8,
        "energy_efficiency": 1.2,
    }
    node_1 = NodeCore("node_1", initial_traits, energy_capacity=10.0)
    node_2 = NodeCore("node_2", initial_traits, energy_capacity=10.0)

    # Establish collaboration
    node_1.collaborate_with(node_2)

    # Assign tasks to node_1
    node_1.assign_task({"id": "task_1", "energy_required": 2.0, "priority": 2})
    node_1.assign_task({"id": "task_2", "energy_required": 5.0, "priority": 1})

    # Prioritize tasks on node_1
    node_1.prioritize_tasks()

    # Execute tasks on node_1
    completed = node_1.execute_tasks()
    print(f"Node_1 Completed Tasks: {completed}")

    # Redistribute energy between nodes
    node_1.redistribute_energy()

    # Recharge node_1
    node_1.recharge(energy_input=5.0)

    # Attempt replication for node_1
    new_node = node_1.replicate()

    # Summarize node states
    print("Node_1 Summary:", node_1.summarize())
    print("Node_2 Summary:", node_2.summarize())
    if new_node:
        print("New Node Summary:", new_node.summarize())

# NodeCore.py

# Kaleidoscope AI: Script for Node Core Functionality
# File: /core/NodeCore.py

import uuid
import time
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, Optional, List
from collections import deque
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass
class GrowthState:
    maturity: float = 0.0
    knowledge: float = 0.0
    specialization: Optional[str] = None

@dataclass
class Traits:
    learning_rate: float = 0.1
    energy_efficiency: float = 1.0
    replication_threshold: float = 0.8

class Node:
    def __init__(self, node_id: Optional[str] = None):
        self.id = node_id or str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.creation_time = time.time()
        self.energy = 10.0
        self.growth_state = GrowthState()
        self.traits = Traits()

        # Memory Systems
        self.short_term_memory = deque(maxlen=100)
        self.long_term_memory: Dict[str, Dict] = {}
        self.connections = set()

    def process_input(self, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Process input data, updating knowledge and state."""
        knowledge_gain = self._analyze_data(data)
        self._update_growth(knowledge_gain)
        return {
            'knowledge_gain': knowledge_gain,
            'energy_remaining': self.energy,
            'maturity': self.growth_state.maturity
        }

    def replicate(self) -> Optional['Node']:
        """Replicates the node if conditions are met."""
        if self.energy > 5.0 and self.growth_state.maturity >= self.traits.replication_threshold:
            self.energy /= 2
            new_node = Node()
            new_node.traits = self._mutate_traits()
            self.connections.add(new_node.id)
            new_node.connections.add(self.id)
            return new_node
        return None

    def _analyze_data(self, data: Dict) -> float:
        """Analyze input data and calculate knowledge gain."""
        patterns_detected = self._extract_patterns(data)
        knowledge_gain = sum(pattern['impact'] for pattern in patterns_detected)
        energy_cost = knowledge_gain / self.traits.energy_efficiency
        if self.energy >= energy_cost:
            self.energy -= energy_cost
        else:
            knowledge_gain *= 0.5  # Penalty for insufficient energy
        return knowledge_gain

    def _extract_patterns(self, data: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
        """Extract meaningful patterns from data."""
        patterns = []
        for key, value in data.items():
            if isinstance(value, str):
                length = len(value)
                patterns.append({'type': 'text', 'impact': length * 0.01})
            elif isinstance(value, (int, float)):
                patterns.append({'type': 'numeric', 'impact': abs(value) * 0.01})
            elif isinstance(value, list) and all(isinstance(i, (int, float)) for i in value):
                mean_value = np.mean(value)
                patterns.append({'type': 'numeric_list', 'impact': mean_value * 0.01})
        return patterns

    def _update_growth(self, knowledge_gain: float):
        """Update growth state based on knowledge gain."""
        self.growth_state.knowledge += knowledge_gain
        self.growth_state.maturity = min(1.0, self.growth_state.maturity + knowledge_gain * self.traits.learning_rate)

    def _mutate_traits(self) -> Traits:
        """Mutate traits slightly during replication."""
        new_traits = Traits(
            learning_rate=max(0.01, self.traits.learning_rate + np.random.normal(0, 0.01)),
            energy_efficiency=max(0.5, self.traits.energy_efficiency + np.random.normal(0, 0.05)),
            replication_threshold=min(1.0, self.traits.replication_threshold + np.random.normal(0, 0.02))
        )
        return new_traits

    def communicate(self, target_node: 'Node', message: Dict):
        """Send a message to another node."""
        if target_node:
            if target_node.id in self.connections:
                target_node.receive_message({
                    'sender': self.id,
                    'timestamp': time.time(),
                    'content': message
                })
            else:
                raise ValueError(f"Target node {target_node.id} is not in the connections list.")

    def receive_message(self, message: Dict):
        """Receive and process a message."""
        self.short_term_memory.append(message)
        # Process the message (extend with specific logic as needed)

# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    node1 = Node()
    node2 = Node()
    node1.connections.add(node2.id)
    node2.connections.add(node1.id)

    data = {"input1": "Example Data", "input2": "More Data", "numbers": [10, 20, 30]}
    result = node1.process_input(data)
    print(f"Node 1 Process Result: {result}")

    try:
        node1.communicate(node2, {"type": "greeting", "message": "Hello, Node 2!"})
        print("Message successfully sent to Node 2.")
    except ValueError as e:
        print(e)

    new_node = node1.replicate()
    if new_node:
        print(f"Node {new_node.id} replicated from Node {node1.id}")

# node_core_simulation.py

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Callable
import numpy as np

class LearningCore:
    """
    Simulated LearningCore for managing adaptive learning and knowledge integration.
    """
    def __init__(self):
        self.knowledge_base = {}
        self.learning_rate = 0.1

    def learn(self, data: List[Dict[str, Any]], contribution_fn: Callable[[Dict[str, Any], float], float]) -> float:
        knowledge_gain = 0.0
        for entry in data:
            contribution = contribution_fn(entry, self.learning_rate)
            self.knowledge_base[entry['id']] = contribution
            knowledge_gain += contribution
        return knowledge_gain

    def summarize(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "total_knowledge": len(self.knowledge_base),
            "learning_rate": self.learning_rate,
        }

class NodeCore:
    """
    Core module responsible for managing node traits, replication, task execution,
    and integration with the LearningCore for adaptive improvements.
    Designed for seamless integration with Kaleidoscope AI's modular architecture.
    """

    def __init__(self, node_id: str, traits: Dict[str, float], energy_capacity: float):
        self.node_id = node_id
        self.traits = traits
        self.energy_capacity = energy_capacity
        self.current_energy = energy_capacity
        self.task_queue = []  # Stores tasks assigned to the node
        self.replication_threshold = traits.get("replication_threshold", 0.8)
        self.energy_efficiency = traits.get("energy_efficiency", 1.0)
        self.learning_core = LearningCore()  # Integrated LearningCore
        self.collaboration_nodes = []  # Nodes this node collaborates with
        self.completed_tasks = []  # Historical task tracking

    def assign_task(self, task: Dict[str, Any]):
        """
        Assigns a task to the node's task queue.
        :param task: A dictionary containing task details.
        """
        self.task_queue.append(task)

    def execute_tasks(self):
        """
        Executes tasks in the queue while managing energy usage and task completion.
        Also learns from completed tasks using the integrated LearningCore.
        Shares task insights with collaboration nodes for distributed learning.
        """
        task_data = []

        for task in self.task_queue:
            energy_required = task.get("energy_required", 0.1)

            if self.current_energy >= energy_required:
                self.current_energy -= energy_required / self.energy_efficiency
                self.completed_tasks.append(task)
                task_data.append({"id": task["id"], "value": energy_required})
            else:
                print(f"Node {self.node_id}: Insufficient energy for task {task['id']}.")

        self.task_queue = [task for task in self.task_queue if task not in self.completed_tasks]

        # Use LearningCore to learn from completed tasks
        if task_data:
            contribution_fn = lambda entry, rate: entry["value"] * rate
            knowledge_gain = self.learning_core.learn(task_data, contribution_fn)
            print(f"Node {self.node_id}: Learned {knowledge_gain} from completed tasks.")

        # Share insights with collaboration nodes
        for node in self.collaboration_nodes:
            node.receive_shared_knowledge(task_data)

        return self.completed_tasks

    def prioritize_tasks(self):
        """
        Reorders the task queue based on energy requirements and importance.
        """
        self.task_queue.sort(key=lambda x: (x.get("priority", 1), x.get("energy_required", 0.1)))
        print(f"Node {self.node_id}: Task queue reprioritized.")

    def redistribute_energy(self):
        """
        Shares excess energy with collaboration nodes needing support.
        """
        for node in self.collaboration_nodes:
            if node.current_energy / node.energy_capacity < 0.5 and self.current_energy / self.energy_capacity > 0.7:
                energy_shared = 0.1 * self.energy_capacity
                self.current_energy -= energy_shared
                node.recharge(energy_shared)
                print(f"Node {self.node_id}: Shared {energy_shared} energy with {node.node_id}.")

    def replicate(self):
        """
        Determines if the node should replicate based on energy and traits.
        :return: A new NodeCore instance if replication is successful, otherwise None.
        """
        if self.current_energy / self.energy_capacity >= self.replication_threshold:
            new_traits = {
                trait: value * (1 + 0.1 * (0.5 - value))  # Slight variation to traits
                for trait, value in self.traits.items()
            }
            new_node = NodeCore(f"{self.node_id}_child", new_traits, self.energy_capacity)
            self.current_energy /= 2  # Energy is split between parent and child
            print(f"Node {self.node_id}: Replication successful, created {new_node.node_id}.")

            # Share learned knowledge with the new node
            new_node.learning_core.knowledge_base = self.learning_core.knowledge_base.copy()
            print(f"Node {new_node.node_id}: Inherited knowledge from {self.node_id}.")

            return new_node
        else:
            print(f"Node {self.node_id}: Insufficient energy for replication.")
        return None

    def recharge(self, energy_input: float):
        """
        Recharges the node's energy up to its capacity.
        :param energy_input: Amount of energy to recharge.
        """
        self.current_energy = min(self.energy_capacity, self.current_energy + energy_input)
        print(f"Node {self.node_id}: Recharged to {self.current_energy}/{self.energy_capacity}.")

    def receive_shared_knowledge(self, knowledge: List[Dict[str, Any]]):
        """
        Receives shared knowledge from other nodes and integrates it using LearningCore.
        :param knowledge: A list of knowledge entries shared by collaborating nodes.
        """
        contribution_fn = lambda entry, rate: entry["value"] * rate
        knowledge_gain = self.learning_core.learn(knowledge, contribution_fn)
        print(f"Node {self.node_id}: Integrated {knowledge_gain} knowledge from collaborators.")

    def collaborate_with(self, node: 'NodeCore'):
        """
        Establishes collaboration with another node for knowledge sharing.
        :param node: The NodeCore instance to collaborate with.
        """
        if node not in self.collaboration_nodes:
            self.collaboration_nodes.append(node)
            node.collaboration_nodes.append(self)
            print(f"Node {self.node_id}: Collaboration established with {node.node_id}.")

    def summarize(self):
        """
        Summarizes the node's current state.
        :return: A dictionary containing node details.
        """
        return {
            "node_id": self.node_id,
            "traits": self.traits,
            "current_energy": self.current_energy,
            "energy_capacity": self.energy_capacity,
            "task_queue_size": len(self.task_queue),
            "completed_tasks": len(self.completed_tasks),
            "learning_core_summary": self.learning_core.summarize(),
            "collaboration_nodes": [node.node_id for node in self.collaboration_nodes],
        }

# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Initialize nodes
    initial_traits = {
        "replication_threshold": 0.8,
        "energy_efficiency": 1.2,
    }
    node_1 = NodeCore("node_1", initial_traits, energy_capacity=10.0)
    node_2 = NodeCore("node_2", initial_traits, energy_capacity=10.0)

    # Establish collaboration
    node_1.collaborate_with(node_2)

    # Assign tasks to node_1
    node_1.assign_task({"id": "task_1", "energy_required": 2.0, "priority": 2})
    node_1.assign_task({"id": "task_2", "energy_required": 5.0, "priority": 1})

    # Prioritize tasks on node_1
    node_1.prioritize_tasks()

    # Execute tasks on node_1
    completed = node_1.execute_tasks()

    # Redistribute energy between nodes
    node_1.redistribute_energy()

    # Recharge node_1

    if new_node:
        print("New Node Summary:", new_node.summarize())

# node_creation.py

from core_node import Node

class NodeManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.nodes = []

    def create_seed_node(self):
        """Create the initial seed node."""
        seed_node = Node()
        self.nodes.append(seed_node)
        return seed_node

    def grow_network(self):
        """Grow the network by replicating nodes."""
        new_nodes = []
        for node in self.nodes:
            new_node = node.replicate()
            if new_node:
                new_nodes.append(new_node)
        self.nodes.extend(new_nodes)

    def communicate(self):
        """Enable resource sharing between nodes."""
        for i, node in enumerate(self.nodes):
            if i + 1 < len(self.nodes):
                node.share_resources(self.nodes[i + 1])

    def display_network(self):
        """Display the current state of the network."""
        for node in self.nodes:
            print(node)

# node-dashboard.py

from flask import Flask, jsonify, render_template, send_from_directory
from node_system_integration import NodeSystem
import logging
import networkx as nx
import json
from datetime import datetime

app = Flask(__name__)

class DashboardSystem(NodeSystem):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.metrics_history = []
        self.last_update = datetime.now()
        
    def update_metrics(self):
        """Record system metrics for time-series visualization."""
        current_metrics = {
            'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat(),
            'nodes': [{
                'node_id': node.node_id,
                'energy': self.energy_manager.energy_distribution.get(node.node_id, 0),
                'insights': len(node.insight_storage),
                'traits': node.dna.traits
            } for node in self.nodes],
            'memory_usage': sum(len(bank.memory) for bank in self.memory_bank.banks.values()),
            'total_insights': sum(len(node.insight_storage) for node in self.nodes)
        }
        self.metrics_history.append(current_metrics)
        self.last_update = datetime.now()

# Initialize the enhanced system
system = DashboardSystem()
system.initialize_nodes(num_nodes=3, capacity=10)

# Routes
@app.route('/')
def index():
    """Serve the dashboard HTML."""
    return render_template('index.html')

@app.route('/api/nodes', methods=['GET'])
def get_nodes():
    """Get detailed node states with metrics."""
    system.update_metrics()
    return jsonify({
        'current_state': [{
            'node_id': node.node_id,
            'traits': node.dna.traits,
            'insights': len(node.insight_storage),
            'energy': system.energy_manager.energy_distribution.get(node.node_id, 0),
            'task_queue': len(node.task_queue)
        } for node in system.nodes],
        'history': system.metrics_history
    })

@app.route('/api/memory', methods=['GET'])
def get_memory():
    """Get memory bank status with analytics."""
    memory_stats = system.memory_bank.summarize_banks()
    memory_analytics = {
        'total_entries': sum(stats['total_entries'] for stats in memory_stats.values()),
        'bank_distribution': {
            node_id: {
                'size': stats['total_entries'],
                'newest': stats['newest_entry']
            } for node_id, stats in memory_stats.items()
        }
    }
    return jsonify(memory_analytics)

@app.route('/api/insights', methods=['GET'])
def get_insights():
    """Get insights with rankings and analysis."""
    all_insights = []
    for node in system.nodes:
        node_insights = [{
            **insight,
            'node_id': node.node_id,
            'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat()
        } for insight in node.insight_storage]
        all_insights.extend(node_insights)
    
    return jsonify({
        'insights': all_insights,
        'analytics': {
            'total_count': len(all_insights),
            'by_node': {
                node.node_id: len(node.insight_storage)
                for node in system.nodes
            }
        }
    })

@app.route('/api/graph', methods=['GET'])
def get_graph():
    """Get network visualization data."""
    G = nx.DiGraph()
    
    # Add nodes
    for node in system.nodes:
        G.add_node(node.node_id, 
                  type='node',
                  energy=system.energy_manager.energy_distribution.get(node.node_id, 0))
        
        # Add insights as nodes
        for insight in node.insight_storage:
            insight_id = insight.get('task_id', 'unknown')
            G.add_node(insight_id, 
                      type='insight',
                      confidence=insight.get('confidence', 0))
            G.add_edge(node.node_id, insight_id)
    
    return jsonify({
        'nodes': [{'id': node, 
                   'type': data['type'],
                   **{k:v for k,v in data.items() if k != 'type'}}
                  for node, data in G.nodes(data=True)],
        'links': [{'source': u, 'target': v} 
                 for u, v in G.edges()]
    })

@app.route('/api/system/status', methods=['GET'])
def get_system_status():
    """Get overall system status and health metrics."""
    return jsonify({
        'last_update': system.last_update.isoformat(),
        'nodes_active': len(system.nodes),
        'total_energy': sum(system.energy_manager.energy_distribution.values()),
        'memory_usage': sum(len(bank.memory) for bank in system.memory_bank.banks.values()),
        'total_insights': sum(len(node.insight_storage) for node in system.nodes)
    })

# Template route
@app.route('/templates/<path:path>')
def serve_template(path):
    return send_from_directory('templates', path)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    # Initialize logging
    logging.basicConfig(
        level=logging.INFO,
        format='%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s'
    )
    
    # Simulate initial data
    data = [
        {
            "id": f"task_{i}",
            "value": i * 10,
            "complexity": i % 3 + 1,
            "confidence": 0.9 - i * 0.02
        } for i in range(30)
    ]
    
    # Process initial data
    system.ingest_and_process_data(data)
    system.collect_and_rank_insights()
    system.update_traits_and_memory()
    
    # Start the server
    app.run(debug=True, port=5000)

# node-dna-system.py

# Cell 1: Import Dependencies
import random
from typing import Dict, List, Any
import numpy as np
import plotly.graph_objects as go
from plotly.subplots import make_subplots
import ipywidgets as widgets
from IPython.display import display, clear_output
import networkx as nx
import pandas as pd

# Cell 2: Enhanced NodeDNA System
class NodeDNA:
    """Enhanced NodeDNA with visualization capabilities."""
    def __init__(self, generation: int = 0):
        self.traits = {
            "creativity": random.uniform(0.5, 1.0),
            "logic": random.uniform(0.5, 1.0),
            "preservation": random.uniform(0.5, 1.0),
            "adaptability": random.uniform(0.5, 1.0),
            "resilience": random.uniform(0.5, 1.0)
        }
        self.mutation_rate = 0.1
        self.data_imprint = {}
        self.generation = generation
        self.evolution_history = []
        self._record_history()

    def _record_history(self):
        """Record current state in evolution history."""
        self.evolution_history.append({
            'generation': self.generation,
            **self.traits,
            **self.data_imprint
        })

    def mutate(self):
        """Enhanced mutation with history tracking."""
        self.generation += 1
        for trait, value in self.traits.items():
            mutation = random.uniform(-self.mutation_rate, self.mutation_rate)
            self.traits[trait] = max(0, min(1.0, value + mutation))
        self._record_history()

    def imprint_data(self, data_characteristics: Dict[str, float]):
        """Enhanced data imprinting with visualization support."""
        for key, value in data_characteristics.items():
            if key in self.traits:
                self.traits[key] = max(0, min(1.0, (self.traits[key] + value) / 2))
            else:
                self.data_imprint[key] = value
        self._record_history()

    def visualize_evolution(self):
        """Create interactive evolution visualization."""
        df = pd.DataFrame(self.evolution_history)
        
        fig = make_subplots(rows=2, cols=2,
                           subplot_titles=('Trait Evolution', 'Trait Radar', 
                                         'Data Imprint Impact', 'Generation Summary'))
        
        # Trait Evolution Line Plot
        for trait in self.traits.keys():
            fig.add_trace(
                go.Scatter(x=df['generation'], y=df[trait], name=trait),
                row=1, col=1
            )
        
        # Radar Plot of Current Traits
        theta = list(self.traits.keys())
        fig.add_trace(
            go.Scatterpolar(
                r=list(self.traits.values()),
                theta=theta,
                fill='toself',
                name='Current Traits'
            ),
            row=1, col=2
        )
        
        # Data Imprint Impact
        if self.data_imprint:
            fig.add_trace(
                go.Bar(
                    x=list(self.data_imprint.keys()),
                    y=list(self.data_imprint.values()),
                    name='Data Imprints'
                ),
                row=2, col=1
            )
        
        # Generation Summary
        summary_text = f"Generation: {self.generation}<br>"
        summary_text += "<br>".join([f"{k}: {v:.2f}" for k, v in self.traits.items()])
        
        fig.add_annotation(
            text=summary_text,
            xref="paper", yref="paper",
            x=0.5, y=0.5,
            showarrow=False,
            row=2, col=2
        )
        
        fig.update_layout(height=800, showlegend=True)
        return fig

# Cell 3: Advanced Node Implementation
class EvolvingNode:
    """Node with DNA evolution visualization."""
    def __init__(self, node_id: str, capacity: int):
        self.node_id = node_id
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.dna = NodeDNA()
        self.task_queue = []
        self.energy = 100.0
        self.performance_metrics = []
        self.status_output = widgets.Output()
        
    def process_task(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Process task using DNA traits."""
        energy_cost = 10 * (1 - self.dna.traits['efficiency'])
        self.energy -= energy_cost
        
        result = {
            'task_id': task['id'],
            'processing_quality': self.dna.traits['logic'] * random.uniform(0.8, 1.0),
            'energy_cost': energy_cost,
            'innovations': self.dna.traits['creativity'] > 0.8
        }
        
        self.performance_metrics.append({
            'time': len(self.performance_metrics),
            **result
        })
        
        return result

# Cell 4: Interactive Evolution Dashboard
class EvolutionDashboard:
    """Interactive dashboard for monitoring node evolution."""
    def __init__(self, node: EvolvingNode):
        self.node = node
        self.setup_controls()
        
    def setup_controls(self):
        """Create interactive controls."""
        self.controls = {
            'mutation_rate': widgets.FloatSlider(
                value=self.node.dna.mutation_rate,
                min=0, max=0.5, step=0.01,
                description='Mutation Rate'
            ),
            'evolve_button': widgets.Button(description='Evolve'),
            'imprint_button': widgets.Button(description='Imprint Data')
        }
        
        self.output = widgets.Output()
        self.controls['evolve_button'].on_click(self.evolve_callback)
        self.controls['imprint_button'].on_click(self.imprint_callback)
        
    def evolve_callback(self, b):
        """Handle evolution button click."""
        with self.output:
            clear_output(wait=True)
            self.node.dna.mutation_rate = self.controls['mutation_rate'].value
            self.node.dna.mutate()
            self.node.dna.visualize_evolution().show()
            
    def imprint_callback(self, b):
        """Handle imprint button click."""
        with self.output:
            clear_output(wait=True)
            data_chars = {
                'creativity': random.uniform(0.5, 1.0),
                'logic': random.uniform(0.5, 1.0),
                'pattern_recognition': random.uniform(0.5, 1.0)
            }
            self.node.dna.imprint_data(data_chars)
            self.node.dna.visualize_evolution().show()
            
    def display(self):
        """Display the dashboard."""
        controls_box = widgets.VBox([
            widgets.HTML("<h3>Evolution Controls</h3>"),
            self.controls['mutation_rate'],
            widgets.HBox([
                self.controls['evolve_button'],
                self.controls['imprint_button']
            ])
        ])
        
        dashboard = widgets.VBox([
            controls_box,
            self.output
        ])
        
        display(dashboard)
        with self.output:
            self.node.dna.visualize_evolution().show()

# Cell 5: Example Usage
def run_example():
    # Create evolving node
    node = EvolvingNode("node_1", capacity=10)
    
    # Create and display dashboard
    dashboard = EvolutionDashboard(node)
    dashboard.display()
    
    return node, dashboard

# Run the example
node, dashboard = run_example()

# node_initializer.py

# node_initializer.py

from typing import List, Dict
from .node import Node
import numpy as np

class NodeInitializer:
    @staticmethod
    def initialize_nodes(features: List[Dict], num_nodes: int) -> List[Node]:
        """
        Initializes nodes with DNA-like traits and assigns clusters.
        """
        nodes = []
        for i in range(num_nodes):
            traits = {
                "learning_rate": np.random.uniform(0.1, 1.0),
                "energy_efficiency": np.random.uniform(0.5, 1.5),
                "replication_threshold": np.random.uniform(0.5, 0.9),
            }
            node = Node(i, traits, energy=np.random.uniform(5, 15))
            if traits["learning_rate"] > 0.5:
                node.specialize("Financial")
            else:
                node.specialize("Healthcare")
            nodes.append(node)
        return nodes

# node_lifecycle (2).py

# node_management/node_lifecycle.py

import logging
import random  # For simulating node utilization

# Configure logging for NodeLifecycleManager
logging.basicConfig(
    filename="node_lifecycle.log",
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s"
)

class NodeLifecycleManager:
    def __init__(self):
        """
        Manages the lifecycle of nodes, including initialization, transitions, and termination.
        """
        self.nodes = {}

    def create_node(self, node_id, memory_threshold):
        """
        Creates a new node with specified attributes.

        Args:
            node_id (str): Unique identifier for the node.
            memory_threshold (int): Memory threshold for the node.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        if node_id in self.nodes:
            logging.warning(f"Node with ID {node_id} already exists.")
            return

        self.nodes[node_id] = {
            "status": "active",
            "memory_threshold": memory_threshold,
            "current_memory": 0,
            "processed_data": []
        }
        logging.info(f"Node created: {node_id}")

    def transition_node(self, node_id, new_status):
        """
        Transitions a node to a new status.

        Args:
            node_id (str): Unique identifier for the node.
            new_status (str): New status for the node.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        if node_id not in self.nodes:
            logging.warning(f"Node with ID {node_id} does not exist.")
            return

        self.nodes[node_id]["status"] = new_status
        logging.info(f"Node {node_id} transitioned to {new_status}")

    def update_memory(self, node_id, memory_delta):
        """
        Updates the current memory usage of a node.

        Args:
            node_id (str): Unique identifier for the node.
            memory_delta (int): Change in memory usage.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        if node_id not in self.nodes:
            logging.warning(f"Node with ID {node_id} does not exist.")
            return

        self.nodes[node_id]["current_memory"] += memory_delta
        logging.info(f"Node {node_id} memory updated by {memory_delta}")

    def add_processed_data(self, node_id, data):
        """
        Adds processed data to a node.

        Args:
            node_id (str): Unique identifier for the node.
            data (list): List of processed data items.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        if node_id not in self.nodes:
            logging.warning(f"Node with ID {node_id} does not exist.")
            return

        self.nodes[node_id]["processed_data"].extend(data)
        logging.info(f"Processed data added to node {node_id}")

    def terminate_node(self, node_id):
        """
        Terminates a node, releasing its resources.

        Args:
            node_id (str): Unique identifier for the node.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        if node_id not in self.nodes:
            logging.warning(f"Node with ID {node_id} does not exist.")
            return

        del self.nodes[node_id]
        logging.info(f"Node terminated: {node_id}")

    def list_nodes(self):
        """
        Returns a list of all active node IDs.

        Returns:
            list: List of node IDs.
        """
        return list(self.nodes.keys())

# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    manager = NodeLifecycleManager()

    # Create nodes
    manager.create_node("node1", 100)
    manager.create_node("node2", 200)

    # Update memory and add processed data
    manager.update_memory("node1", 50)
    manager.add_processed_data("node1", [1, 2, 3])

    # Transition node status
    manager.transition_node("node1", "inactive")

    # Terminate node
    manager.terminate_node("node1")

    # List active nodes
    print("Active Nodes:", manager.list_nodes())

# node_lifecycle (3).py

# Node Lifecycle Manager script content
# NodeLifecycle (copy 1).py


import random
import copy

class Node:
    def __init__(self, name, attributes=None):
        self.name = name
        self.attributes = attributes or {}
        self.children = []  # Track replicated nodes

    def replicate(self):
        """Replicate the node with inherited and slightly modified attributes."""
        new_name = f"{self.name}_child_{random.randint(1000, 9999)}"
        # Modify attributes slightly for evolution
        new_attributes = copy.deepcopy(self.attributes)
        for key in new_attributes:
            if isinstance(new_attributes[key], (int, float)):
                new_attributes[key] += random.uniform(-0.1, 0.1)  # Small random change

        child_node = Node(new_name, new_attributes)
        self.children.append(child_node)
        print(f"Node {self.name} replicated into {child_node.name}.")
        return child_node

    def specialize(self, specialization_key, specialization_value):
        """Specialize the node by adding a unique attribute."""
        self.attributes[specialization_key] = specialization_value
        print(f"Node {self.name} specialized with {specialization_key}: {specialization_value}.")

# Example usage of Node lifecycle
if __name__ == "__main__":
    parent_node = Node("ParentNode", {"energy": 100, "task": "data_processing"})
    print(f"Parent Node: {parent_node.name}, Attributes: {parent_node.attributes}")

    # Replicate and specialize
    child_node = parent_node.replicate()
    child_node.specialize("environment", "healthcare")

    print(f"Child Node: {child_node.name}, Attributes: {child_node.attributes}")

# node_lifecycle_management2.py

# File: node_lifecycle_management.py

from typing import Dict, List
from datetime import datetime
import logging
import numpy as np

# Configure logging
logging.basicConfig(
    filename="node_lifecycle.log",
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s"
)

class Node:
    def __init__(self, node_id: str, energy: float, knowledge: float, role: str = "general"):
        self.node_id = node_id
        self.energy = energy
        self.knowledge = knowledge
        self.role = role  # New: Assign specific roles to nodes
        self.creation_time = datetime.now()
        self.last_active = datetime.now()
        self.status = "active"  # Status can be "active", "inactive", or "retired"

    def update_activity(self):
        """Updates the node's last active time."""
        self.last_active = datetime.now()

    def deactivate(self):
        """Deactivates the node."""
        self.status = "inactive"
        logging.info(f"Node {self.node_id} deactivated.")

    def reactivate(self):
        """Reactivates the node."""
        self.status = "active"
        logging.info(f"Node {self.node_id} reactivated.")

    def retire(self):
        """Retires the node permanently."""
        self.status = "retired"
        logging.info(f"Node {self.node_id} retired.")

class NodeLifecycleManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.nodes: Dict[str, Node] = {}
        self.history: List[Dict] = []  # New: Maintain a history of node states

    def add_node(self, node_id: str, energy: float, knowledge: float, role: str = "general"):
        """Adds a new node to the system."""
        if node_id in self.nodes:
            logging.warning(f"Node {node_id} already exists.")
            return

        self.nodes[node_id] = Node(node_id, energy, knowledge, role)
        logging.info(f"Node {node_id} added with energy {energy}, knowledge {knowledge}, role {role}.")

    def remove_node(self, node_id: str):
        """Removes a node from the system."""
        if node_id in self.nodes:
            self.history.append({
                "node_id": node_id,
                "status": "removed",
                "timestamp": datetime.now()
            })
            del self.nodes[node_id]
            logging.info(f"Node {node_id} removed from the system.")
        else:
            logging.warning(f"Attempted to remove non-existent node {node_id}.")

    def deactivate_node(self, node_id: str):
        """Deactivates a node."""
        if node_id in self.nodes:
            self.nodes[node_id].deactivate()
        else:
            logging.warning(f"Attempted to deactivate non-existent node {node_id}.")

    def reactivate_node(self, node_id: str):
        """Reactivates a node."""
        if node_id in self.nodes:
            self.nodes[node_id].reactivate()
        else:
            logging.warning(f"Attempted to reactivate non-existent node {node_id}.")

    def retire_node(self, node_id: str):
        """Retires a node permanently."""
        if node_id in self.nodes:
            self.history.append({
                "node_id": node_id,
                "status": "retired",
                "timestamp": datetime.now()
            })
            self.nodes[node_id].retire()
        else:
            logging.warning(f"Attempted to retire non-existent node {node_id}.")

    def list_nodes(self):
        """Lists all nodes and their statuses."""
        for node_id, node in self.nodes.items():
            logging.info(f"Node {node_id}: Status={node.status}, Energy={node.energy}, Knowledge={node.knowledge}, Role={node.role}")
        return {node_id: node.status for node_id, node in self.nodes.items()}

    def check_inactive_nodes(self, threshold_minutes: int):
        """Checks for nodes that have been inactive beyond a threshold and deactivates them."""
        now = datetime.now()
        for node_id, node in self.nodes.items():
            if node.status == "active" and (now - node.last_active).total_seconds() > threshold_minutes * 60:
                node.deactivate()
                logging.info(f"Node {node_id} deactivated due to inactivity.")

    def migrate_resources(self, source_node_id: str, target_node_id: str):
        """Transfers resources from one node to another."""
        if source_node_id in self.nodes and target_node_id in self.nodes:
            source = self.nodes[source_node_id]
            target = self.nodes[target_node_id]

            # Transfer half of the source's resources
            transfer_energy = source.energy * 0.5
            transfer_knowledge = source.knowledge * 0.5

            source.energy -= transfer_energy
            source.knowledge -= transfer_knowledge
            target.energy += transfer_energy
            target.knowledge += transfer_knowledge

            logging.info(f"Transferred {transfer_energy} energy and {transfer_knowledge} knowledge from {source_node_id} to {target_node_id}.")
        else:
            logging.warning(f"Migration failed: Nodes {source_node_id} or {target_node_id} do not exist.")

    def check_health(self, threshold_energy: float = 20.0, threshold_knowledge: float = 10.0):
        """Checks the health of all nodes and flags low-performing ones."""
        for node_id, node in self.nodes.items():
            if node.energy < threshold_energy or node.knowledge < threshold_knowledge:
                logging.warning(f"Node {node_id} is unhealthy: Energy={node.energy}, Knowledge={node.knowledge}.")

# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    manager = NodeLifecycleManager()

    # Adding nodes
    manager.add_node("Node-1", energy=80, knowledge=50, role="computation")
    manager.add_node("Node-2", energy=60, knowledge=40, role="storage")

    # Listing nodes
    print("Nodes:", manager.list_nodes())

    # Deactivating a node
    manager.deactivate_node("Node-1")

    # Reactivating a node
    manager.reactivate_node("Node-1")

    # Retiring a node
    manager.retire_node("Node-2")

    # Checking inactive nodes
    manager.check_inactive_nodes(threshold_minutes=1)

    # Resource migration
    manager.migrate_resources("Node-1", "Node-2")

    # Checking node health
    manager.check_health(threshold_energy=50, threshold_knowledge=30)

    # Removing a node
    manager.remove_node("Node-1")

# node_lifecycle_manager (2).py

import logging
import random  # For simulating node utilization

# Configure logging for NodeLifecycleManager
logging.basicConfig(
    filename="node_lifecycle.log",
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s"
)

class NodeLifecycleManager:
    def __init__(self):
        """
        Manages the lifecycle of nodes, including initialization, transitions, and termination.
        """
        self.nodes = {}

    def create_node(self, node_id, memory_threshold):
        """
        Creates a new node with specified attributes.

        Args:
            node_id (str): Unique identifier for the node.
            memory_threshold (int): Memory threshold for the node.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        if node_id in self.nodes:
            logging.warning(f"Node with ID {node_id} already exists.")
            return

        self.nodes[node_id] = {
            "status": "active",
            "memory_threshold": memory_threshold,
            "current_memory": 0,
            "processed_data": []
        }
        logging.info(f"Node created: {node_id}")

    def transition_node(self, node_id, new_status):
        """
        Transitions a node to a new status.

        Args:
            node_id (str): Unique identifier for the node.
            new_status (str): New status for the node.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        if node_id not in self.nodes:
            logging.warning(f"Node with ID {node_id} does not exist.")
            return

        self.nodes[node_id]["status"] = new_status
        logging.info(f"Node {node_id} transitioned to {new_status}")

    def update_memory(self, node_id, memory_delta):
        """
        Updates the current memory usage of a node.

        Args:
            node_id (str): Unique identifier for the node.
            memory_delta (int): Change in memory usage.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        if node_id not in self.nodes:
            logging.warning(f"Node with ID {node_id} does not exist.")
            return

        self.nodes[node_id]["current_memory"] += memory_delta
        logging.info(f"Node {node_id} memory updated by {memory_delta}")

    def add_processed_data(self, node_id, data):
        """
        Adds processed data to a node.

        Args:
            node_id (str): Unique identifier for the node.
            data (list): List of processed data items.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        if node_id not in self.nodes:
            logging.warning(f"Node with ID {node_id} does not exist.")
            return

        self.nodes[node_id]["processed_data"].extend(data)
        logging.info(f"Processed data added to node {node_id}")

    def terminate_node(self, node_id):
        """
        Terminates a node, releasing its resources.

        Args:
            node_id (str): Unique identifier for the node.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        if node_id not in self.nodes:
            logging.warning(f"Node with ID {node_id} does not exist.")
            return

        del self.nodes[node_id]
        logging.info(f"Node terminated: {node_id}")

    def list_nodes(self):
        """
        Returns a list of all active node IDs.

        Returns:
            list: List of node IDs.
        """
        return list(self.nodes.keys())

# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    manager = NodeLifecycleManager()

    # Create nodes
    manager.create_node("node1", 100)
    manager.create_node("node2", 200)

    # Update memory and add processed data
    manager.update_memory("node1", 50)
    manager.add_processed_data("node1", [1, 2, 3])

    # Transition node status
    manager.transition_node("node1", "inactive")

    # Terminate node
    manager.terminate_node("node1")

    # List active nodes
    print("Active Nodes:", manager.list_nodes())

# node_lifecycle_manager.py

# File: core/node_lifecycle_manager.py

import logging
from typing import Dict

# Configure logging
logging.basicConfig(
    filename="node_lifecycle_manager.log",
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s"
)

class NodeLifecycleManager:
    """
    Manages the lifecycle of nodes, including creation, status updates, and removal.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.nodes: Dict[str, Dict] = {}  # Stores node details

    def create_node(self, node_id: str, memory_threshold: int = 100) -> None:
        """
        Creates a new node with the specified parameters.

        Args:
            node_id (str): Unique identifier for the node.
            memory_threshold (int): Memory threshold for the node.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        if node_id in self.nodes:
            logging.warning(f"Node {node_id} already exists.")
            return

        self.nodes[node_id] = {
            'memory_threshold': memory_threshold,
            'status': 'active',
            'energy': 100.0,
            'tasks_completed': 0
        }
        logging.info(f"Node {node_id} created with memory threshold {memory_threshold}.")

    def update_node_status(self, node_id: str, status: str) -> None:
        """
        Updates the status of a node.

        Args:
            node_id (str): Unique identifier for the node.
            status (str): New status of the node.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        if node_id not in self.nodes:
            logging.error(f"Node {node_id} not found.")
            return

        self.nodes[node_id]['status'] = status
        logging.info(f"Node {node_id} status updated to {status}.")

    def update_node_energy(self, node_id: str, energy_change: float) -> None:
        """
        Updates the energy level of a node.

        Args:
            node_id (str): Unique identifier for the node.
            energy_change (float): Amount to change the node's energy by.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        if node_id not in self.nodes:
            logging.error(f"Node {node_id} not found.")
            return

        node = self.nodes[node_id]
        node['energy'] = max(0, node['energy'] + energy_change)

        if node['energy'] == 0:
            node['status'] = 'inactive'
            logging.info(f"Node {node_id} is now inactive due to zero energy.")
        else:
            logging.info(f"Node {node_id} energy updated to {node['energy']}.")

    def remove_node(self, node_id: str) -> None:
        """
        Removes a node from the system.

        Args:
            node_id (str): Unique identifier for the node.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        if node_id in self.nodes:
            del self.nodes[node_id]
            logging.info(f"Node {node_id} removed from the system.")
        else:
            logging.warning(f"Attempted to remove non-existent node {node_id}.")

    def get_status(self) -> Dict:
        """
        Provides the current status of all nodes.

        Returns:
            Dict: A dictionary containing node statuses.
        """
        return {
            node_id: {
                'status': node['status'],
                'energy': node['energy'],
                'tasks_completed': node['tasks_completed']
            } for node_id, node in self.nodes.items()
        }

if __name__ == "__main__":
    manager = NodeLifecycleManager()

    # Example operations
    manager.create_node("Node_1", memory_threshold=200)
    manager.update_node_energy("Node_1", -50)
    manager.update_node_status("Node_1", "processing")
    print("Node Status:", manager.get_status())
    manager.remove_node("Node_1")
